ivaṉ ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil iruntavaṉ. cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉum, koṅku nāṭṭu vēntaṉ peruñcēral irumpoṟai yum ivaṉuṭaiya kālattavar āvar. ivaṉaik kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅkaṇṇa ṉārum muṭattāmakkaṇṇiyārum veṇṇikkuyattiyārum kaḻāttalai yārum pāṭiyuḷḷaṉar. ivaṉ āṭcikku varātapaṭi ivaṉuṭaiya tāya muṟaiyiṉar ivaṉaic ciṟaiyil aṭaittaṉar. ivaṉ ac ciṟaiyiṉiṉṟu tappiveḷivantu aracukkaṭṭil ēṟiṉāṉ. 
veṇṇip pōrukkuppiṉ toṇṭai nāṭṭaiyāṇṭa toṇṭaimāṉ iḷantiraiyaṉ mītu paṭaiyeṭuttu veṟṟi kaṇṭāṉ. toṇṭai nāṭu aruvā nāṭu eṉṟum, annāṭṭu makkaḷ aruvāḷar eṉṟum ak kālattil aṟiyappaṭṭaṉar. koṭumpāḷūrai āṇṭa iruṅkōvēḷ maṉṉaṉaiyum, potuvar eṉpāraiyum, oḷiyar eṉpāraiyum ivaṉ veṉṟatāka aṟikiṟōm. 
koṅku nāṭṭaiyāṇṭa peruñcēral irumpoṟai takaṭūr eṟintapiṉ cōḻariṉ pukāraiyum kaippaṟṟiṉāṉ. karikālaṉ pukārai mīṭṭapiṉ avaḷiṉ veḷiyūril poruti niṉṟu veṟṟikaṇṭāṉ. iḵtu ivaṉuṭaiya iraṇṭāvatu veṇṇip pōrākum. ip pōril peruñcēral irumpoṟaimutukil puṇpaṭṭu avamāṉam tāṅkāmal pōrkkaḷattilēyē vaṭakku iruntu uyirnīttāṉ, 
karikālaṉ toṇṭai nāṭṭil kāṭu koṉṟu nāṭākki vaḷap paṭutti 'māvaḷavaṉ' eṉṉum virutuppeyarum peṟṟāṉ. kāvirikkut kuṟukkē kallaṇaiyoṉṟaik kaṭṭi nīrppācaṉa ēntukaḷaic ceytāṉ. kāvirikkuk kuṟukkē kallaṇai cuṭṭaivaṉ ilaṅkaiyiliruntu 12,000 āṭkaḷaik koṇṭu vantāṉ eṉṟum, piṉṉar an nāṭṭu aracaṉ mutalām cuyavāku cōḻaṉai veṉṟu avarkaḷai mīṭṭuc ceṉṟatōṭu, cōḻa nāṭṭiliruntu 1200 āṭkaḷaiyum aṭimaippaṭuttic ceṉṟāṉ eṉṟum annāṭṭu irācāvaḷi" eṉṉum piṟkāla nūl kūṟukiṟatu. ivaṉ uṟaiyūrk kōṭṭaiyai valuppaṭuttiṉāṉ. mutiyavaṉ pōl urumāṟi muṟai vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ; tammaip pāṭiya kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉ ṉārukkup patiṉāṟilakkam poṉṉaip paricākak koṭuttatōṭu, patiṉāṟukāl maṇṭapam oṉṟaiyum avar niṉaivāka uṟaiyūril kaṭṭiṉāṉ' muṭattāmakkaṇṇiyār, vāṇar, pāṇar, pāṭiṉi mutali yōrukkup poṉṉaiyum, muttaiyum paricāka aḷittāṉ maṇi mēkalaiyiṉ aṉṉai mātavi, cōḻaṉuṭaiya avaiyil araṅkēṟṟam ceytāḷ eṉṉum ceytiyai āyntu nōkkumpōtu, avaḷ araṅ kēṟiya avai karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉuṭaiya avaiyāka irukkalām eṉat tōṉṟukiṟatu. 
mutalām kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ neṭumuṭikkiḷḷi) 
karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉukku āṇ vāricu illai. avaṉukkup piṉ aracuk kaṭṭil ēṟiya mutalām kiḷḷivaḷavaṉukkum karikāṟ peruvaḷat tāṉukkum uṟavumuṟai eṉṉaveṉpatu teriyavillai. āṉāl, cilar ivaṉ karikālaṉ makaṉ eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. maṇimēkalaiyil ivaṉ kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ, neṭumuṭikkiḷḷi, vaṭivēṟ kiḷḷi, toṭukaḻaṟkiḷḷi, māvaṇkiḷḷi mutaliya peyarkaḷāl aṟiyap paṭukiṟāṉ. ivaṉ toṇṭai maṇṭalattu vāṇar araca kulattu iḷavaraci cīrtti eṉpavaḷai maṇantāṉ. ivviruvarukkum piṟantavaṉē kōvalaṉukkum mātavikkum piṟanta maṇimēkalaiyaik kātalittuk kolaiyuṇṭa utayakumaraṉ eṉpāṉ. ivaṉ kālattil toṇṭai nāṭu cōḻanāṭ ṭuṭaṉ iṇaikkappaṭṭiruntatu. itaṉai ivaṉ tampi iḷaṅkiḷḷi (iḷaṅkōṉ) kāñciyiliruntu āṇṭu vantāṉ. iḷaṅkiḷḷi taṉ aṇṇaṉiṉ āṇaippaṭi toṇṭai maṇṭalattaik kaippaṟṟa vanta cēra, pāṇṭiyaraik kāriyāṟṟaṅ karaiyil tōṟkaṭittāṉ. ivaṉ. nākanāṭṭu yāḻppāṇam) iḷavaraci pīlivaḷai eṉpavaḷaik kaḷavu maṇam pūṇṭatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. 
kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ kālattil tamiḻakam tolaikkiḻakku nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ neruṅkiya vāṇikattoṭarpukoṇṭiruntatu. cāvaka nāṭṭu aracaṉ puṇṇiyarācaṉ aṉuppiya tūtukkuḻu kiḷḷi vaḷavaṉiṭam vantu ceṉṟataiyum akkuḻuviṉar aṟavaṇa aṭi kaḷaiyum, maṇimēkalaiyai yum kaṇṭuceṉṟataiyum maṇimēkalai yāl aṟikiṟōm. ivaṉuṭaiya kālattil perumpuyalāl pūmpukār mūḻkiyataiyum ik kāppiyam kūṟukiṟatu'. ivaṉ kāri yāṟṟaṅkaraiyil tuñciṉāṉ. ivaṉ kālam tōrā. ki.pi.150 āka irukkalām 
kōpperuñ cōḻaṉ 
kōpperuñ cōḻaṉ kiḷḷivaḷavaṉukkup piṉ aracukkaṭṭil ēṟiṉāṉ. ivaṉukkum picir eṉṉum ūrilirunta āntaiyārukkum iṭaiyē oruvaraiyoruvar kāṇāmalē irunta naṭpaip paṟṟiya katai ak kālattil palapaṭap pukaḻntu pēcappaṭṭatākum. ic cōḻaṉaik koṅku nāṭṭuccēraṉ iḷañcēral irumpoṟai veṉṟāṉ eṉṟu terikiṟatu. ivaṉukkup piḷḷaikaḷ iruvar iruntaṉar eṉṟum, avarkaḷ ivaṉmēl kāḻppuk koṇṭu ivaṉait tākka muṉaintaṉar eṉṟum, itaṉāl ivaṉ maṉam varunti vaṭakkiruntu uyir nīttāṉ eṉṟum puṟanāṉūṟṟuc cāṉṟukaḷāl aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. avaṉuṭaiya naṇparāṉa picirāntaiyārum ivaṉuṭaiya pirivāṟṟāḻaiyait tāṅkātu vaṭakkiruntu uyirnīttārām', 
kōpperuñ cōḻaṉukkup piṉ cēṭceṉṉi nalaṅkiḷḷi eṉpavaṉum, avaṉ tampi iḷañcēṭceṉṉiyum muṟaiyē pukāraiyum ataṉ kaṭaṟkaraip pakutiyākiya neytalaṅkāṉalaiyum talainakarākak koṇṭu aracāṇṭaṉar. cēṭceṉṉi nalaṅkiḷḷiyai, uṟaiyūrai yāṇṭa ivaṉuṭaiya tāyvaḻi neṭuṅkiḷḷi etirttāṉ. uṟaiyūr muṟṟukai yiṭappaṭṭatu. aṅkup pōnta pulavar kōvūrkkiḻāriṉ talaiyīṭṭāl camaiti camātāṉam ēṟpaṭṭatu. uṟaiyūr nalaṅkiḷ ḷikkuk koṭukkappaṭṭatu. neṭuṅkiḷḷi taṉ talainakarai āvūrukku māṟṟik koṇṭāṉ. itaṉāl cōḻanāṭu muḻuvatum nalaṅkiḷḷi vayamāyiṟṟu. ivaṉ pāṇṭiyarōṭu pōriṭṭu ēḻeyil eṉṉum tōṭṭaiyaik kaippaṟṟi ak kōṭṭaik katavil taṉ pulic ciṉṉattaip poṟittāṉ, itoru vākaip pōr, vākaip pōr veṟṟikkāka maṭṭum mēṟkoḷḷum pōr; vañcip pōr eṉpatu nāṭṭaik kaippaṟṟa mēṟkoḷḷum pōrākum. 
ivaṉ tampi iḷañcēṭceṉṉi ivaṉuṭaiya āṇaippaṭi tuḷuva nāṭṭup pāḻiyūrai veṉṟu ceruppāḻi yeṟinta iḷañcēṭ ceṉṉi' eṉṟu peyar peṟṟāṉ. cēraruṭaiya pāmūraiyum veṉṟāṉ, ivaṉ kaṭaicivarai taṉ aṇṇaṉukkut tuṇaiyākavē perumakaṉ) iruntāṉ. cēṭ ceṉṉi nalaṅkiḷḷi kāriyāṟṟaṅ karaiyil iṟantāṉ. iṟappiṉ karaṇiyam teriyavillai . 
ivarkaḷukkuppiṉ cōḻa nāṭṭaiyāṇṭavaṉ perumpūṭ ceṉṉiyāvāṉ. ivaṉ aḻumpil, kuṭavāyil kumpakōṇam mutalāṉa ūrkaṉai yāṇṭāṉ. ivaṉ taṉṉuṭaiya tāṉait talaivaṉaiyaṉuppik koṅku nāṭṭuccēraṉ. iḷañcēral irumpoṟaiyiṉ kīḻirunta puṉṉāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭaiyeṭukkac ceytāṉ. cēraṇukkut tuṇaiyāka ivar pōril kalantu koṇṭa tāl paḻaiyaṉ tōṟṟatōṭu pōrk kaḷattilēyē kollap paṭṭāṉ. piṉṉar perumpūṭceṉṉicērarukku uṭpaṭṭirunta kaḻumalattaik kaippaṟṟi ataṉaik kāttu niṉṟa kaṇaiyaṉ eṉpavaṉaiyum ciṟaip piṭittuc ceṉṟāṉ. itaṉaiyaṟinta iḷañ cēraṉ irumpoṟai taṉ paṭaikaḷaiyaṉuppi atē cuḻumalattil cōḻap paṭaikaḷait tōṟkaṭittu, avarkaḷ pōṭṭuviṭṭu ōṭiya paṭaikaḷai yum kaippaṟṟac ceytāṉ. ivvaḷavē ivaṉaippaṟṟi nām aṟiya muṭikiṟatu. 
iraṇṭām kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ cōḻaṉ kuḷamuṟṟattut tuñciya kiḷḷi vaḷavaṉ) 
karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉukkuppiṉ āṇṭa kiḷḷivaḷavaṉai mutalām kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ eṉṟatāl ivaṉai iraṇṭāmkiḷḷi vaḷavaṉ eṉkiṟōm. ivaṉ uṟaiyūraiyāṇṭavaṉ. ivaṉukku neṭumāvaḷavaṉ, pacumpūṇ kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ, poṉṉiyal perumpūṇvaḷavaṉ, polam pūṇvaḷavaṉ eṉṟa virutup peyar kaḷum uṇṭu. itaṉāl ivaṉ poṉ aṇikaḷ aṇivatil viruppa muḷḷavaṉ eṉpatu terikiṟatu. ivaṉ kālattilum uḷnāṭṭup pōrkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa, avaṟṟiṉiṉṟu taṉṉāṭciyai nilai niṟuttik koṇṭu, cēra nāṭṭiṉ kōnakarāṉa karuvūrai muṟṟukaiyiṭṭu veṉṟāṉ. muḷḷūr maṉṉaṉ malaiyamāṉiṭam pakaimaikoṇṭu avaṉuṭaiya iru kuḻantaikaḷaip piṭittuvantu yāṉaikkālil iṭṭukkollamuyaṉṟāṉ. itaṉaiyaṟinta kōvūrk kiḻār takka aṟivurai kūṟi ac ciṟārkaḷai mīṭṭār eṉa aṟikiṟōm. 
pāṇṭiyaṉ kōtai mārpaṉ mēl paṭaiyeṭutta ivaṉaip pāṇṭiyaṉuṭaiya paṭaittalaivaṉ paḻaiyaṉ māṟaṉ kūṭalukku arukil tōṟkaṭittāṉ. appoḻutu pāṇṭiya nāṭṭaiyāṇṭavaṉ talai yālaṅkāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉ āvāṉ. ivarkaḷuṭaiya kālam ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyākavum, mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkamākavum irukkalām. ivaṉ kurāppaḷḷiyil (kuḷamuṟṟam iṟantupaṭṭāṉ. itaṉāl ivaṉaic 'cōḻaṉ kurāp paḷḷit tuñciya kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ' allatu "kuḷamuṟṟattut tuñciya kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ' eṉṟu aḻaikkiṉṟaṉar. 
ivaṉukkup piṉ irācacūyam vēṭṭa perunaṟkiḷḷi eṉpāṉ āṇṭāṉ. ivaṉ koṅku nāṭṭaiyāṇṭayāṉaik kaṇcēy māntarañ cēral irumpoṟaiyuṭaṉ pōriṭṭu veṟṟiyaṭaintāṉ. itait tavira ivaṉaip paṟṟiya vēṟu mukāmaic ceyti etuvum namakkuk kiṭaikka villai . 
cōḻaṉceṅkaṇāṉ 
iraṇṭām kiḷḷivaḷavaṉukkuppiṉ aracāṇṭavaril kuṟippiṭat takkavaṉ cōḻaṉ ceṅkaṇāṉ' āvāṉ. kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil aṟiyap paṭum kaṭaiciyaracaṉ ivaṉēyāvāṉ. ivaṉ koṅku nāṭṭaiyāṇṭa cēraṉ kaṇaikkāl irumpoṟaiyuṭaṉ cuḻumalam eṉṉumiṭattil pōriṭṭu veṟṟi kaṇṭāṉ. mīṇṭum pōr' eṉṉum ūril iruvarum mōtikkoṇṭaṉar. atilum poṟaiyaṉ tōṟṟuc ciṟaip piṭikkap paṭṭāṉ. cōḻa nāṭṭuc ciṟaiyil vāṭiya poṟaiyaṉ orunāḷ nīrvēṭkai mēliṭṭu nīr kēṭka, avaṉaik kāvalarkaḷ iḻivāka naṭattiṉar. māṉam tāṅkātu poṟaiyaṉ ciṟaiyilēyē uyir nīttāṉ eṉṟu avaṉē pāṭiyatākak karutappeṟum puṟappāṭaliliruntu aṟikiṟōm. ciṟaimīṇṭa ivaṉ taṉ nāṭṭiliruntu cōḻaṉukkaṭaṅki āṇṭāṉeṉpatu kaliṅkattupparaṇiyil kāṇum ceytiyākum, 
ceṅkaṇāṉ ēṟattāḻa irupattaintu āṇṭukaḷ āṇṭāṉ. tōrāyamāka ki.pi. 225-l ivaṉ iṟantirukkalām. ivaṉukkuppiṉ kaḷappirar cōḻanāṭṭaik kaippaṟṟik koṇṭaṉar. ivaṉōṭu kaḻakakkālac cōḻar āṭciyum muṭivaṭaintatu. 
(i) kaḻakakkālap pāṇṭiya vēntar
tamiḻkkaḻakam eṉṟavuṭaṉ pāṇṭiyariṉ tamiḻccaṅkamē niṉai vukku varum. caṅkam vaittut tamiḻ vaḷartta ap pāṇṭiya vēntariṉ varalāṟṟaic caṟṟē aṟivōm. 
kaḻakakkālap pāṇṭiyanāṭu 
ellaikaḷ: paḻampāṇṭiya nāṭu eṉpatu tamiḻakattiṉ teṉ pakutiyil iruntatu. itaṉmēṟkilum kiḻakkilum kaṭalkaḷēellai kaḷāyiruntaṉa.kaḻakakkālattil ilaṅkaikkum pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṟku miṭaiyil kuṟukalāṉa ciṟukaṭal oṉṟu iruntatu. itaṉai 'ārkalik kaṭal' eṉṟaṉar. ik kaṭal paṟṟit tālamiyiṉ kuṟippukaḷilum, perippuḷucu' eṉṟa nūlilum aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. avaṟṟil iḵtu 'ōrkalik kaṭal' (Orgalic Sea) eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṟatu. kiḻakku, mēṟkuk kaṭalkaḷaiyum, inta ārculik kaṭalaiyum yavaṉarkaḷ potuvāka 'eritiraikkaṭal' ceṅ kaṭal) eṉṟu aḻaittaṉar. itaṉait tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷ koṟkaik kaṭal' allatu 'koṟkaik kuṭākkaṭal" eṉṟu aḻaittaṉa. ak kālattil tāmparaparaṇiyāṟu intak koṟkaikkuṭākkaṭalil kalantatu. inta āṟu kaṭaluṭaṉ kalakkumiṭattiltāṉ koṟkait tuṟaimukap paṭṭiṉam iruntatu. ittuṟaimukappaṭṭiṉattil kiṭaitta muttukaḷaip paṟṟi ak kāla ulaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷum, varalāṟukaḷum palappaṭap pēcukiṉṟaṉa, cāṇakkiyaṉ taṉatu aruttacāttirattil koṟkaiyiṉ muttuc celvattaip pōṟṟik kūṟiyuḷḷāṉ. ittakaiya ciṟappuvāynta ik koṟkait tuṟai tōrāyamāka ki.pi. 10 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tārntu pōyiṟṟu. iṉṟu iṅku māṟamaṅkalam eṉṉum ciṟṟūrē kāṇappaṭum kiṟatu. kaḻakakkālattil pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ teṉpakuti iṟṟai irāmēcu varattaiyum tāṇṭiyiruntatu. tōrāyamāka ki.mu. 500 āṇṭukaṭku muṉputāṉ perumpuyalāl kaṭal poṅki arippu ēṟpaṭṭatāl irāmēcuvaram taṉittīvu ākiyatu. pōrttukkīciya vaṇikarkaḷ kaṭalaritta pakutiyai āḻappaṭuttik kappal pōkkuvarattukkup payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭaṉar. 
itaṟkum palavāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉ irāmēcuvarattaip pōl ilaṅkaiyum pāṇṭiya nāṭṭup pakutiyiliruntu pirintu viṭṭatu. itaṉai īḻanāṭṭu nūlāṉa makāvamcattil virikkak kāṇalām. avvāṟu ilaṅkaikkum, teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ teṉkaraikkumiṭaiyil tuṇṭippu ēṟpaṭṭa piṉ nīril maṇ karaintuviṭṭatu. āṉāl, pāṟai kaḷum, kuṉṟukaḷum iṉṟuḷḷaṉa. iḵtu iyaṟkaiyāl nērnta ceyal. āṉāl, 'pāmarar' ivai irāmar ilaṅkaikkuc cellumpōtu vāṉaraṅkaḷāl pōṭappaṭṭa kaṟkaḷ eṉkiṉṟaṉar. 
ilaṅkai, tamiḻakattiṉiṉṟu pirintatu pōl, paḻantamiḻakattiṉ teṉpakuti kaṭal koṇṭa ceytiyai iṟaiyaṉārakapporuḷ uraip pāyiram, cilappatikāram, kalittokai mutaliya nūlkaḷum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāṟṟāṉ mutal iṭaik kaḻakaṅkaḷaik kaṭal koṇṭateṉṟum, iṉṟaiya akaḻvārāycciyum, maṇṇiyalum, kaṭal nūlum itaṉai uṟutippaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. 
kaḻakakkālap pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ mēṟ kellai kaṭal eṉṟālum nuṇukippārkkumpōtu iṉṟaiya teṉtiruvitāṅkūr, potikaimalai, nāñcilnāṭu mutaliyavaṟṟai itu taṉṉakattē koṇṭiruntatu. potikai malaiyil āyeṉṉum vēḷirkaḷ pāṇṭiyarukku uṭpaṭṭu āṇṭaṉar. viḻiñam vēḷir tuṟaimuka mākum. iṉṟaiya taṉucukōṭi mutukōṭi) aṉṟaiya pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ vaṭavellaiyāka iruntatu. vaṭappuṟattil koṭumpāḷūr koṭumpai) iruntatu. ataṉaiyaṭuttuc cōḻa nāṭu iruntatu. koṭumpāḷūraiyāṇṭa kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷ iruṅkō vēḷ' eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷ camayattiṟkēṟṟapaṭi pāṇṭiyarōṭum, cōḻarōṭum kūṭṭuc cērntu koḷvar. 
malaikaḷum kuṉṟukaḷum 
kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷai āyumpōtu pāṇṭiya nāṭṭilirunta malaikaḷ, kuṉṟukaḷ, āṟukaḷ, tuṟaimukaṅkaḷ, nakaraṅkaḷ mutaliyavai paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa. 
iṉṟaiya maturaikku vaṭakkiluḷḷa iruṅkuṉṟam kaḻakak kālattil tirumālkuṉṟu, tirumāliruñcōlai, cōlaimalai mutaliya peyarkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉ ucciyilirunta iyaṟkaik kukaiyil camaṇa, cākkiya muṉivarkaḷ vāḻntārkaḷ. itaṉ kal paṭukkaikaḷiluḷḷa pirāmik kalveṭṭu iv vuṇmaiyait teḷivākkukiṟatu, kaḻakakkāl nakkīrar tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭaiyil pāṭiya tirupparaṅkuṉṟam' eṉṉum tiruppatiyilum, camaṇa cākkiya muṉivarkaḷ vāḻnta iyaṟkaik kukaiyum, kal paṭukkaikaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itiluḷḷa pirāmik kalveṭṭil kīḻakappulavar nallantuvaṉārpeyarum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. itaippōlavē āṉaimalaiyiṉ ucciyil iyaṟkaik kukaiyum, kalpaṭukkaikaḷum uḷḷaṉa. itiluḷḷa pirāmik kalveṭṭu kaḻakak kālattuc cākkiya muṉivarkaḷ iṅkut tavam ceytaṉar eṉpataik kūṟukiṟatu. 
caṅka nūlkaḷil aṟiyappaṭum potikaimalai pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil iruntatu. malayattuvaca pāṇṭiyaṉ itil tavam ceytatākak kūṟappaṭu kiṟatu. akattiyarukkum im malaikkum toṭarpu illai. avaraip piṇaittup paṭaitta katai kipi. 8 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiyatu. caṅka nūlkaḷil perumaiyuṭaṉ pēcappaṭum paṟampu malaiyum, paṟampunāṭum pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiltām iruntaṉa. itil vāḻnta pāriyiṉ kālam tōrāyamāka. kipi. 100-125 āka irukkalām. iṉṟu kaḻukumalai eṉa . vaḻaṅkappaṭum kuṉṟukaḷil kukaikaḷum, kalpaṭukkaikaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉ ṟaṉa. avaṟṟiluḷḷa pirāmik kalveṭṭukaḷ pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉ, nanti āciriyar eṉṉum muṉivarukku oru kukaiyait tāṉam ceytataik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. ikkalveṭṭukimu. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭaic cērntatu. 
āṟukaḷ 
vaikai, tāmparaparaṇi, cilampāṟu, paḵṟuḷiyāṟu, kumariyāṟu mutaliya āṟukaḷ pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil pāyntōṭiyataik kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅ cukaḷiliruntu aṟikiṟōm. ivaṟṟil paḵṟuḷiyāṟum, kumariyāṟum kaṭal koṇṭa pāṇṭiya nāṭṭuṭaṉ kaṭalil maṟaintu viṭṭaṉa. 
tuṟaimukappaṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ 
toṇṭimaruṅkūrppaṭṭiṉam, koṟkai, kumari, viḻiñam mutaliya tuṉmukaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟil ēṟṟumatiyāṉa poruḷkaḷ paṟṟiyum, ayalakac culuṅkaḷ ivaṟṟil vantu pōṉamaipaṟṟiyum kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷilēyaṉṟi ayalakac cāṉṟukaḷilum kuṟippukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. 
nakaraṅkaḷ 
pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ mukāmai nakaraṅkaḷāṉa teṉmaturaiyum katava puramum kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiṉa. kūṭal (maturai), mōkūr, taṅkāl, alaivāy centil, picir ākiyavai caṅkakālap pāṇṭiyar nakaraṅkaḷākum. pāṇṭiyariṉ koṭivaḻit tāṉait talaivarkaḷ (paḻaiyar, paḻaiyaṉ māṟaṉ mōkūril iruntaṉar, 
pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ toṉmai 
pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ toṉmaiyaip paṟṟi mēṟkaṇṭa ceytikaḷi viruntē oruvāṟu ūkittaṟiyalām. tamiḻaka varalāṟu' pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil tāṉ toṭaṅkukiṟatu. ulaka varalāṟē ilemūriyā allatu kaṭal koṇṭa teṉṉāṭṭilētāṉ toṭaṅkukiṟatu. mutal māntaṉ iṅku tāṉ tōṉṟiṉāṉ eṉṟellām kūṟumpōtu pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ toṉmaiyaik kaṇittuk kūṟaviyalātu. kimu. nāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭaic cērnta ilaṅkai varalāṟu tīpavamcam an nāṭṭu mutal ciṅkaḷa maṉṉaṉāṉa vicayakumāraṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ makaḷai maṇantatōṭu avaṉuṭaiya rappa tōḻarkaḷum pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ peṇkaḷai maṇantu koṇṭu pāṇṭiyarukku āṇṭutōṟum vilaiyuyarnta muttukkaḷaiyum, piṟa poruḷkaḷaiyum paricāka aṉuppiṉar eṉak kūṟukiṟatu. pāṇṭiyaṉ taṉ makaḷaik koṭuttatōṭu parivāraṅkaḷaiyum, patiṉeṇ vakaittoḻi lāḷarkaḷaiyum ilaṅkaikku aṉuppi, an nāṭṭai nākarikamuṭaiya nāṭākkiṉāṉ eṉavum aṟikiṟōm. ki. mu. nāṉkām nāṟṟāṇṭil aracāṇṭa cantirakupta mauriyaruṭaiya avaikku vanta mekcuttaṉicu eṉṟa yavaṉat tūtuvaṉ pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil oru peṇ aracāṇṭatāka kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷāṉ.kimu. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil mauriyap pēraracarāy viḷaṅkiya acōkar tamatu pāṟaik kalveṭṭukaḷil mūvēntarkaḷaip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭuvataiyaṟivōm. kimu 185 ām āṇṭil poṟikkappaṭṭa kaliṅka nāṭṭukkāravēlaṉiṉ attikumpāk kukaik kalveṭṭil avaṉuṭaiya pāṇṭiya nāṭṭup paṭaiyeṭuppu kuṟippiṭap paṭukiṟatu. 
ivaṟṟait tavira nām mēlē kaṇṭa kuṉṟukaḷiluḷḷa pirāmik kalveṭṭukaḷ kaḻakakkālattil pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil camaṇamum, cākkiyamumpeṟṟirunta ciṟappiṉai aṟivikkiṉṟaṉa. 
pāṇṭiya vēntar 
iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷuraippaṭi katavapurattiliruntu āṇṭa pāṇṭiya vēntaril muṭattirumāṟaṉ kaṭaiciyaracaṉ āvāṉ. ivarukkup piṉ āṇṭa vēntarkaḷiṉ vivaram kōvaiyākat teriyavillai. ki.mu. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil veṭṭappaṭṭa mēṟkūṟiya kaḻukumalaiyi luḷḷa kalveṭṭiṉpaṭi mutalām pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉai muṭattirumāṟaṉai yaṭuttu aṟikiṟōm. ik kalveṭṭu maturaikku vaṭakiḻakkē patiṉāṟu kal tolaiviluḷḷa ariṭṭāpaṭṭi eṉṉum ūrukku arukiluḷḷa māṅkuḷam eṉṉum ūriluḷḷa kuṉṟukaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. tamiḻaka maṉṉarkaḷiṉ pirāmik kalveṭṭukaḷil mikavum paḻamaiyāṉatu ituvēyākum. ip pāṇṭiyaṉ taṉ kulakkuru nanti āciriyaṉukkum, avaraic cērntavarukkum tavam ceyya ik kuṭaivarai yaiyum, kaṟpaṭukkaikaḷaiyum camaittuk koṭuttāṉ eṉṟa ceytiyē ik kalveṭṭil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉaic camaitta peruntaccaṉ paḻaiyaṉ kaṭalaṉvaḻuti eṉṟum, maṟṟum caṭikaṉ, iḷañcaṭicaṉ ākiya araca ūḻiyarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum itil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟait tavira ik kalveṭṭil ivaṉaip paṟṟi vēṟoṉṟum aṟiya  muṭiyavillai , 
mutalām pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉukku muṉ āṇṭavar yāreṉṟu teriyātatu pōlavē piṉ āṇṭavar-yāreṉṟum teriya villai. āyiṉum puṟanāṉūṟu oṉpatām pāṭalil 'neṭiyōṉ” eṉpavaṉaippaṟṟik kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ivaṉtāṉ paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟai veṭṭi nīrppācaṉa ēntukaḷ ceytāṉ eṉpataiyum, munnīr viḻā (vāraṇaṉ viḻā eṭuttāṉ eṉṟum ip pāṭaliliruntu aṟikiṟōm. inta āṟu kaṭalkōḷāl aḻintupaṭṭatu eṉpatiliruntu ivaṉ ki.mu. mutalām nūṟṟāṇṭukkum muṟpaṭṭavaṉ eṉat teḷiyalām. 
neṭṭimaiyār eṉṉum pulavar (puṟam 15itaṉaip paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟu maṇaliṉum atikamāṉa āṇṭukaḷ vāḻumpaṭi vāḻttukiṉṟamaiyāl palyākacālai mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti paḵṟuḷiyāṟu kaṭal koḷḷappaṭu vataṟku muṉ āṇṭatākak karutappaṭu kiṟatu. āṉāl, atu poruttamā yillai, cilappatikārattiṉ kālamāṉa ki.pi. 180kku muṉpē āṇṭirukka vēṇṭumeṉa aṟikiṟōm. 
puṟanāṉūṟu tarum cāṉṟukaḷiliruntu ivaṉ pala nāṭukaḷai veṉṟā ṉeṉṟum, veṉṟa nāṭukaḷilirunta nakaraṅkaḷaik kaḻutaikaḷaik kaṭṭi ēr uḻutavaṉeṉṟum, veṉṟa nāṭukaḷil kiṭaitta koṇṭip poruḷkaḷaip pulavarukkum, vaṟiyarukkum vāri vaḻaṅkiyavaṉeṉṟum aṟikiṟōm. ivvāṟu pala nāṭukaḷaiyum veṟṟi koṇṭa perumitat tāl ivaṉ vēḷvikaḷ pala ceytu 'palyākacālai' eṉṉum virutum peṟṟāṉ. piṉ eḻunta maturaik kāñciyilum "māṅkuṭi - marutaṉār' talaiyālaṅkāṉattuc ceru veṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉai vāḻttumpōtu palyākacālai mutukuṭumiyaippōl pala ciṟappukaḷ peṟṟu vāḻumāṟu vāḻttu kiṟār (matu. 759.763). ki.pi. 8 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻutappaṭṭa vēḷvik kuṭic ceppēṭṭilum ivaṉaip paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷ kāṇappaṭum kiṉṟaṉa. ivaṉ āṇṭa āṇṭukaḷil eṇṇikkaiyum, ivaṉukkuppiṉ āṇṭavarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum teriyavillai. 
aṭuttu āṇṭa pāṇṭiyaṉ peruvaḻuti eṉpavaṉaippaṟṟip puṟa nāṉūṟu vāyilāka aṟikiṟōm. ivaṉ pōril vallavaṉ (karuṅkai oḷvāḷ peruvaḻuti eṉṟum, kavuriyar marukaṉ eṉṟum irumpiṭart talaiyār eṉṉum pulavar pāṭukiṟār. itait tavira ivaṉaip paṟṟiyaṟiya vēṟu ceytikaḷ kiṭaikkavillai. ivaṉuṭaiya tampi iḷamperuvaḻutieṉpavaṉ oru pulavaṉ āvāṉ. ivaṉ puṟanāṉūṟṟil 12 ām pāṭalai yum, paripāṭalil 15 ām pāṭalaiyum pāṭiyavaṉ. ivaṉ kalattil cellum pōtu kaṭalil mūḻki iṟantāṉ. 
aṭuttu aṟiyappaṭum pāṇṭiyaṉ ollaiyūrai veṉṟa ollaiyūr tanta pūtappāṇṭiyaṉ eṉpavaṉ āvāṉ. ollaiyūrc ciṟṟaracaṉ cōḻarukku uṭpaṭṭavaṉ. ātalāl cōḻaṉ pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭaiyeṭuttāṉ. ap pōril pūtap pāṇṭiyaṉ māṇṭāṉ. avaṉuṭalait tīyiliṭṭapōtu pūtappāṇṭiyaṉ kōpperuntēviyum uṭaṉkaṭṭaiyēṟiṉāḷ. 
aṭuttu aṟiyappaṭum pāṇṭiyaṉ pacumpūṇpāṇṭiyaṉ āvāṉ. ivaṉ potikai malaiyil aracāṇṭa āy aṇṭiraṉ tuṇaiyuṭaṉ koṅku nāṭṭiṉ kiḻakkup pakutiyaik kaippaṟṟiṉāṉ. ak kālattil koṅku nāṭṭiṉ naṭuvil takaṭūr iruntatu. ataṉaiyāṇṭa neṭumiṭal añciyaip pacumpūṇ pāṇṭiyaṉ tuḷuvanāṭṭiṉ mītu pōr toṭukkac ceytāṉ. appōril neṭumiṭal añci iṟantupaṭṭāṉ. ākavē, ippōril pāṇṭiyaṉ tōlvi kaṇṭāṉ. ēṉeṉil, takaṭūr neṭumiṭal añci pāṇṭiyaṉiṉ tāṉaittalaivaṉ āvāṉ. ip pāṇṭiyaṉukku irunta maṟṟoru tāṉaittalaivaṉ kaḷvar perumakaṉ teṉṉavaṉāvāṉ. 
āriyappaṭai kaṭanta neṭuñceḻiyaṉ 
kimu, iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭilirunta neṭuñceḻiyaṉai mutalām neṭuñceḻiyaṉ eṉṟōm. ivaṉ ki.pi. 2 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉiṭaip pakutiyil pāṇṭiya nāṭṭaiyāṇṭavaṉ .vaṭapulatti liruntu teṉṉakat tiṉ mītu paṭaiyeṭuttu vanta yavu tēyar eṉṉum kūṭṭattāriṉ āriyap paṭaiyait tōṟkaṭittut turattiyatāl ivaṉ 'āriyappaṭai kaṭanta neṭuñceḻiyaṉ" eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭukiṟāṉ. ivaṉ ciṟanta aṟiñaṉ. kalvi kaṟṟaliṉ payaṉaippuṟam.183ām pāṭalil ciṟappuṟap pāṭiyuḷḷāṉ. 
ivaṉ araciyal poṟuppukaḷ aṉaittaiyum tam ūḻiyar kaṉaiyē nampi viṭṭuviṭṭu nēraṭik kaṇkāṇippuc ceyyāmal poṟuppiṉṟik kiṭantāṉ. oru camayam vārttikaṉ eṉṉum pārppaṉa ṉuṭaiya makaṉ tiruṭiyatāka ūḻiyarkaḷ mūlam kēṭṭut tīra ucāvāmal avaṉaic ciṟaiyil aṭaittāṉ; vārttikaṉ maṉaivi kārttikai eṉpavaḷ koṟṟavaikkōyil muṉ muṟaiyiṭṭāḷ. ak kōyil katavu tāṉākavē mūṭikkoṇṭatu. tavaṟṟaiyuṇarnta pāṇṭiyaṉ pārp paṉaṉai viṭuvittu maṉṉippukkēṭṭatōṭu taṉ kuṟṟattiṉ kaḻuvāyāka nilaṅkaḷaiyum avaṉukku aḷittāṉ. 
itaip pōlattāṉ taṉ nakarukkup piḻaipput tēṭic cilampu viṟka vanta kōvalaṉaik kaḷvaṉeṉṟu kūṟiya araṇmaṉaip poṟkollaṉ pēccai nampit tīra ucāvāmal kōvalaṉaik kolaiceyyumpaṭi āṇaiyiṭṭāṉ. piṉṉar kaṇṇaki muṟai yiṭṭuc cilampiṉ kataiyai viḷakkumpōtu maṉa atircciyāl aracuk kaṭṭivilēyē māṇṭāṉ. itaṉāl ivaṉ 'aracuk kaṭṭilil tuñciya neṭuñceḻiyaṉ' eṉap paṭṭāṉ. ivaṉuṭaiya muṟaiyaṟṟa ceyalaikkaṇṭu kotitteḻunta nakara makkaḷ araṇmaṉaiyait tīyiṭṭukkoḷuttiṉar. itaiyē kaṇṇaki maturaiyai yerittatākak kūṟuvar. 
tōrāyamākakipi.180-l ivaṉ iṟantirukkalām. ivaṉ kālattil cēranāṭṭaiyāṇṭavaṉ cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ. ic ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ pāṇṭiyar tāṉait talaivaṉ mōkūrppaḻaiyaṉ māṟaṉ taṉ naṇpaṉ aṟukaiyeṉṉum aracaṉaippōr ceytu koṉṟāṉ eṉpataṟkāka mōkūrmītu paṭaiyeṭuttu, paḻaiyaṉ māṟaṉuṭaiya kāval maramāṉa vēppa marattai veṭṭi, avaṉaiyum koṉṟu, anta vēppamarattai avaṉuṭaiya uvaḷakap peṇkaḷai moṭṭaiyaṭittuk kūntalāl tiritta cuyiraik koṇṭu piṇaittu, yāṉaiyāl iḻuttu varacceytāṉ. ip pōr cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ iḷavaracaṉāka irukkum pōtu naṭantatu. ic cēraṉē piṉṉar kaṟputtēvatai kaṇṇakikkuc cilai yeṭuttavaṉ āvāṉ cāttaṉārum ivviru maṉṉarkaḷuṭaiya kālattavarē āvār.
pāṇṭiyaṉ naṉmāṟaṉ 
ivaṉuṭaiya iyaṟpeyar veṟṟivēṟ ceḻiyaṉ eṉpatu. avaṉ koṟkaippaṭṭiṉattil iḷavaracaṉāka neṭuñceḻiyaṉukkut tuṇai yāka āṇṭu vantāṉ. neṭuñceḻiyaṉ iṟantapiṉ aracukkaṭṭil ēṟiyatum 'pāṇṭiyaṉ naṉmāṟaṉ' eṉṟu peyar pūṇṭāṉ. kaṇṇaki vaḻakkuraikka vantu iv vaḻakkil neṭuñceḻiyaṉ tīrppukkūṟumuṉ tāṉē iṟantāṉ. kaṇṇakiyum maturaiyai viṭṭu veḷiyēṟiṉāḷ. ākavē, aracu muṟaippaṭi av vaḻakkai ucāvit tīrppuk kūṟum poṟuppai aṭuttu vanta ip pāṇṭiyaṉ ēṟṟāṉ. kōvalaṉaik kuṟṟavāḷiyākki avaṉ kolaikkuk karaṇiyamāyirunta araṇmaṉaip poṟkollaṉaiyum, avaṉukku uṟutuṇaiyāka niṉṟa poṟkollar palaraiyum kolaicceyalukkut tūṇṭiya taṟkākavum, aracamātēviyiṉ cilampait tiruṭiyataṟkākavum, ak kāl muṟaippaṭi avarkaḷiṉ talaikaḷai veṭṭumpaṭi tīrppaḷittāṉ. neṭuñceḻiyaṉ tāṉē it tīrppukku āḷāki ēṟkeṉavē māṇṭatāl tappiṉāṉ. 
cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ vaṭapulam ceṉṟu, cuṉāvicayarai veṉṟu cuṇṇakikkuc cilaiyeṭuttu mīṇṭāṉ. piṉ, ak kaṉaka vicayaraip pāṇṭiyaṉ naṉmāṟaṉukkuk kāṭṭumpaṭi aṉuppiṉāṉ. avarkaḷait tuṟavi vēṭattil kaṇṭa naṉmāṟaṉ pōrkkaḷattil tōṟṟut tuṟavi vēṭam pūṇṭavaraic ciṟaippiṭittu vantatu tamiḻar paṇpukkup putumai yāṉatueṉṟāṉām! 
ivaṉ aracukkaṭṭilēṟa tāyamuṟaiyiṉar ceyta cūḻcci kaḷaiyum, kalakaṅkaḷaiyum veṉṟu kālam tāḻttiyē muṭi cūṭṭik koṇṭāṉ, ivaṉ talaiciṟanta vīraṉ eṉpataik kārikkaṇṇaṉār, marutaṉ iḷa nākaṉār mutaliya pulavarkaḷ pāṭiya puṟanāṉūṟṟup pāṭalkaḷāl aṟiyalām. ivaṉ ilavantikaip paḷḷi allatu kaṭakāram eṉṉum māḷikaic cōlaiyil tuñciṉāṉ.cāttaṉār pāṭiya pāṭaliṉ kīḻuḷḷa kuṟippil ivaṉ cittira māṭattil tuñciyavaṉ' eṉavum aṟiyappaṭu kiṟāṉ. 
talaiyālaṅkāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉ 
ivaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ naṉmāṟaṉukkuppiṉ aracukkaṭṭil ēṟiyavaṉ. 'neṭuñceḻiyaṉ' eṉṉum varicaiyil ivaṉ mūṉṟām neṭuñceḻiyaṉ āvāṉ. ivaṉ ciṟanta pulavaṉākavum iruntāṉ, ivaṉ māṅkuṭi marutaṉāriṭam muṟaippaṭi tamiḻ payiṉṟāṉ. taṉṉai etirttup pōriṭṭacōḻaṉaiyum, cēraṉaiyum, avarkaḷukkut tuṇaiyāka vanta titiyaṉ, eḻiṉi, erumaiyūraṉ, iruṅkōvēḷ, porunaṉ ākiya aintu aracarkaḷaiyum talaiyālaṅkāṉam eṉṉum ūril naṭanta pōril veṉṟu, 'talaiyālaṅkāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉ' āṉāṉ. iv veṟṟiyaip pukaḻntu māṅkuṭi marutaṉār maturaikkāñci' pāṭiṉār. nakkīrar, iṭaikkuṉṟūrkkiḻār, kallāṭaṉār, potumpil kiḻār makaṉār veṇkaṇṇiyār ākiyōrum ivaṉ veṟṟiyaip pukaḻntu pāṭiyuḷḷaṉar. 
piṟa veṟṟikaḷ 
talaiyālaṅkāṉattu veṟṟikkuppiṉ vēḷeṉvi eṉpavaṉuṭaiya miḻalai nāṭṭaiyum, muttāṟu eṉṉum ūraiyum, aḻumpil vēḷ eṉpavaṉuṭaiya aḻumpil eṉṉum palaraiyum veṉṟāṉ. koṅku nāṭṭuc cēra maṉṉaṉ yāṉaikkaṇcēy māntarañcēral irumpoṟai eṉpavaṉaip pōril veṉṟu ciṟaippiṭittāṉ. piṉṉar ic cēraṉ payampuk kuḻiyil akappaṭṭa yāṉai taṉ muyaṟciyiṉāl kuḻiyiliruntu tappiōṭiyatu pōl ciṟaiyiṉiṉṟu tappi ōṭiviṭṭāṉ. 
cōḻavēntaṉ kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ, cēraṉ yāṉaikkaṇcēy māntarañ cēral irumpoṟaiyai veṉṟāṉ. piṉṉar pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉmītu paṭaiyeṭuttāṉ. kūṭalukkarukil avaṉ paṭaikaḷ neruṅkiyatum neṭuñceḻiyaṉiṉ tāṉait talaivaṉ mōkūrppaḻaiyaṉ māṟaṉ avaikaḷaic citaṟaṭittāṉ. cōḻaṉ paṭaikaḷaip pōrkkaḷattilēyē viṭṭuviṭṭu ōṭiṉāṉ. avaṟṟait tāṉait talaivaṉ kaippaṟṟiṉāṉ. 
neṭuñceḻiyaṉ cēra nāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭaiyeṭuttuk kuṭṭuvaṉ cēraliṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ makaṉ tuṟaimukamākiya muciṟiyai muṟṟukai yiṭṭu, aṅkirunta teyvac cilaiyaik kavarntu vantāṉ. ivaṉ āṇṭa kālattai nammāl kaṇikka muṭiyavillai. 
ukkirap peruvaḻuti 
talaiyālaṅkāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉukkup piṟaku pāṇṭiya nāṭṭaiyāṇṭavaṉ ukkirap peruvaḻuti āvāṉ. ivaṉaip paṟṟi aṟivataṟku virivāṉa cāṉṟukaḷillai, ivaṉ cōḻanāṭṭuk kōṭṭaikaḷai veṉṟāṉ. attakaiya oru kōṭṭaikāṉappēreyil eṉpatākum. eṉavē, ivaḷ "cāṉap pēreyil kaṭanta ukkirap peru kivaḻuti' eṉa aḻaikkappaṭukiṟāṉ. ivaṉ ciṟanta pulavaṉum āvāṉ. akanāṉūṟṟaiyum, naṟṟiṇaiyaiyum tokuppittāṉ. ivaṉ kālattil nallantuvaṉār, marutaṉ iḷanākaṉār mutaliya pulavarkaḷ iruntaṉar. kaṭaic caṅkattil vīṟṟirunta pulavar nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmaril ivaṉum oruvaṉ. eṉavē, ivaṉ kaṭaikkaḻakattiṉ puravalaṉākavum, pulavaṉākavum tikaḻntāṉ. kipi. 3ām nūṟṟāṇṭil iv vukkirap peruvaḻutiyiṉ kālattiltāṉ pāṇṭiya aracum, kaṭaikkaḻakamum kulaintaṉaveṉṟu karutap peṟukiṉṟatu. pāṇṭiya vēntaril ivaṉē kaṭaiciyaracaṉ āvāṉ. ivaṉukkup piṉ pāṇṭiya nāṭṭaik kaḷappirar kaippaṟṟikkoṇṭa ṉar. kaḷappirar āṭciyi. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭuvarai nilaittiruntatu. kipi. ēḻām nāṟṟāṇṭil mīṇṭum pāṇṭiyariṉ āṭci ēṟpaṭṭatu. 
(ī) kaḻakakkālak kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷ
kaḻakakkālattil cēranāṭu, cōḻanāṭu, pāṇṭiyanāṭu koṅku nāṭu, tuḷunāṭu, toṇṭaināṭu aruvānāṭu eṉṟu tamiḻakam pala pakutikaḷāka aṟiyappaṭṭālum cēranāṭṭilum, koṅku nāṭṭilum cēra vēntar māṟimāṟiyum, orē camayattilum āṇṭataippōlavē piṟa muṭiyuṭai vēntarkaḷum tuḷu nāṭṭilum, toṇṭai nāṭṭilum āṇṭuḷḷaṉar. ākavē, yāryār ententa nāṭukaḷai evvep poḻutu āṇṭaṉar eṉpatai varaiyaṟuttuk kūṟamuṭiyātu. āyiṉum, cēraraip paṟṟiyaṟiyum pōtu cēranāṭṭaiyāṇṭavar, koṅku nāṭṭaiyāṇṭavar eṉappaṭittaṟiya ēntāka avvāṟu pirittuk koṇṭōm. uṇmaiyil aḵtoru cariyāṉa muṟaiyēyaṉṟu. kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷiṉ nāṭukaḷum, nāṭṭiṉ ellaikaḷumkūṭa varaiyaṟuttuk kūṟamuṭiyātavaiyē. āyiṉum ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kaṇṭavāṟu avarkaḷaippaṟṟik kūṟalām. 
kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷ vaḷḷalkaḷākavum, peruvēntarkaḷaip pōl ciṟappeytiyavarkaḷākavum, makkaḷiṉ matippaip peṟṟavarkaḷāka vum tikaḻntārkaḷ. par. vi. ē. cimittiṉ kaṇakkuppaṭi caṅkakkālattil tōrāyamāka nūṟṟuyirupatukkum mēṟpaṭṭa kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷ irunta tākkūrappaṭukiṟatu. avar cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiyar koṭivaḻiyiṉaraiyum cērttuk kaṇakkiṭṭirukkiṟār. namatu kaṇakkiṉpaṭi ēṟattāḻa eḻupat tāṟu kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷē kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷil orucilaraip paṟṟi maṭṭum kūṟuvōm. 
kaṭaiyēḻu vaḷḷalkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyalait tokuttuk kūṟum peruñcittiraṉār, nattattaṉār ākiya pulavarkaḷ avvēḻu vaḷḷal kaḷaiyum kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷākavē kāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. ākavē, kaṭaiyēḻu vaḷḷalkaḷ eḻuvarum kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷē. paṟampu malaip pakutiyai yāṇṭavaṉum, mullaikkut tērntavaṉumāṉa pāri, malaiyamāṉ nāṭu muḷḷūrp pakutiyaiyāṇṭakāri, ceṉṉi malaip pakutiyaiyāṇṭari, kaṇmaramalaip pakutiyaiyāṇṭanaḷḷi, paḻaṉimalaip pakutiyaiyāṇṭa pēcuḷ, potiya malaippakutiyai yāṇṭa āy, takaṭūrppakutiyaiyāṇṭa atiyamāṉ ākiya kaṭaiyēḻu vaḷḷalkaḷum caṅka kālakkuṟunila maṉṉar kaḷē. ivarkaḷ ovvoruvarum koṭaic ceyalil pukaḻpeṟṟavarkaḷ. 
mutiramalaippakutiyaiyāṇṭa kumaṇaṉ, ciṟukuṭiyil vāḻnta paṇṇaṉ, valvār eṉṉum nakaril vāḻnta paṇṇaṉ, cantūr pakutiyil vāḻantatōyaṉ māṟaṉ, ōymānāṭṭu nalliyakkōṭaṉ, kōṭaikkāṉal pakutiyaiyāṇṭapaṇṇi, nāñcilnāṭṭu vaḷḷuvaṉ toṇṭai nāṭṭu iḷantiraiyaṉ, potiyamalait titiyaṉ, karuvūraiyaṭutta tāṉtōṉṟi malaippakutiyaiyāṇṭa tāmāṉ tōṉṟikkōṉ, cōḻanāṭṭup pūccāṟṟūril vāḻnta pārppaṉaṉ viṇṇattāyaṉ, vēṅkaṭamalaip pakutiyaiyāṇṭa ātaṉuṅkaṉ, karumpaṉūrp pakutiyai yāṇṭa curumpa tūrk kiḻāṉ, ōymāṉ nāṭṭu villiyātaṉ, maṉōkkattaic cūḻnta pakutiyai yāṇṭa aviyaṉ, kutiraimalaip pakutiyaiyāṇṭa piṭṭaṉ, teṉṉāṭṭuc ciṟukuṭiyeṉṉum ūril vāḻnta vāṇaṉ, irumpai eṉṉum ūril vāḻnta virāṉ, ampareṉṉumūril vāḻnta aruvattai, tarumaputtiraṉ mutaliya piṟa vaḷḷalkaḷum kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷē, 
potiyamalaip pakutiyaiyāṇṭatitiyaṉ, vākaiyaik kōnakara mākak koṇṭu vēṇāṭṭaiyāṇṭa āyeyiṉaṉ, mōkūrppakutiyai yāṇṭa paḻaiyaṉ māṟaṉ, kāviriyāṟu pāyum pōr eṉṉum karp pakutiyaiyāṇṭa paḻaiyaṉ, maicūrppakutiyai yāṇṭa maiyūrkkiḻāṉ, cellūrp pakutiyai yāṇṭa ātaṉ eḻiṉi, viḷaṅkil pakutiyaiyāṇṭa cuṭavaṉ ākiyōr tamiḻakattiṉ mēlaippakutiyil teṟkiliruntu vaṭakku varaiyilirunta kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷ āvar. vēṅkaṭattaiyāṇṭa pulvi, cōṇāṭṭup pakutiyilirunta ārkkāṭṭaiyāṇṭa aḻici , avaṉ makaṉ cēntaṉ, malliyaiyāṇṭa kāriyāti, paccai malaiyaiyāṇṭa viccikkō , nīṭūrp pakutiyaiyāṇṭaevvi. koṭumpāḷūrppakutiyai yāṇṭa pulikaṭimāl ākiyōr vaṭakkiliruntu teṟkē nōkkit tamiḻakattiṉ kiḻakkup pakutiyilirunta kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷ āvar. 
naṉṉaṉ eṉpavaṉum kuṟunila maṉṉaṉē. ip peyaril nālvar iruntatāka aṟikiṟōm. avarkaḷ kōcar kuṭiyaic cērnta pāḻiyait talainakarākak koṇṭu āṇṭa naṉṉaṉ, vēḷir kuṭiyaic cērnta viyārait talainakarākak koṇṭu āṇṭa naṉṉaṉ vēṇmāṉ, āykuṭiyaic cērnta paṟampumalaip pakutiyaiyāṇṭa naṉṉaṉ āy, cēykuṭiyaic cērnta nākarkuṭi ceṅkaṇmā pakutiyaiceṅkam yāṇṭa cēy naṉṉaṉ ākiyōr āvar. ivarkaḷil pāḻiyait talainakarākak koṇṭu āṇṭataṉṉōṭu kūṭṭuc cērntu, muṭiyuṭai vēntarōṭu pakaimai koṇṭu etirttavarkaḷ aṟuvar. avarkaḷ ēṟṟai, atti, cuṅkaṉ, cuṭṭi , pūntuṟai puṉṟuṟai), cuṇaiyaṉ eṉpōr āvar. ivarkaḷ tamiḻakattiṉ vaṭakkellai yōraṅkaḷil āṭci celutti vantaṉar. 
mūvēntarkaḷiṉ tuṇaivarkaḷāy viḷaṅkiya palarum kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷē. ivarkaḷ ivvēntarkaḷukkut tāṉaittalaivar kaḷāyp payaṉpaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷil kuṟippiṭattakkavarkaḷ cōḻaṉukku utaviya paḻaiyaṉum, pāṇṭiyaṉukkutaviya paḻaiyaṉ māṟaṉ, nākaṉ, atikaṉ ākiyōrum, teṉṉavaṉukku utaviya kantaṉ, paṇṇi eṉpōrum, vāṉavaṉukku utaviya piṭṭaṉ, aṟukai ākiyōrum āvar. maṟṟum cōḻariṭattil 'ēṉāti' paṭṭam peṟṟa ēṉāti tirukkiḷḷi, cōḻiya ēṉāti tirukkuṭṭuvaṉ, malaiyamāṉ cōḻiya ēṉāti, tirukkaṇṇaṉ mutaliyōrum kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷaip pōl iruntaṉar. 
"kiḻār' eṉṉum peyar peṟṟirunta palarum makkaḷiṉ cel vākkaip peṟṟuc ciṟappuṭaṉ vāḻntaṉar. ivarkaḷai maṉṉariṉ aluvalāḷar eṉak kūṟuvataiviṭa makkaḷ talaivar eṉakkūṟuvatu mikavum poruttamāṉatu. āvūr kiḻār, karuvūr kiḻār, kūṭalūr kiḻār, cellūr kiḻār, ciṟukuṭikiḻāṉ paṇṇaṉ, vallārkiḻāṉ paṇṇaṉ, curumpalūr kiḻāṉ, maiyūr kiḻāṉ, ampar kiḻāṉ, īntarar kiḻāṉ,ollaiyūr kiḻāṉ, koṇkāṉaṅ kiḻāṉ mallikiḻāṉ, nālai kiḻavaṉ, ilaṅkai kiḻavōṉ, nīṭūr kiḻavōṉ, ampar kiḻavōṉ, vēṅkaṭaṅ kiḻavōṉ, vallaṅ kiḻavōṉ, kāvirik kiḻavaṉ, poṟaiyāṟṟuk kiḻāṉ, malaik kiḻavaṉ, varaikkiḻavaṉ mutaliyōr mukāmaiyāṉavarkaḷ āvar. 
(u) kaḻakakkāla āṭcimuṟai
kaḻakakkālattil tamiḻakattaiyāṇṭa cērar, cōḻar, pāṇṭiyar ākiya muṭiyuṭai vēntarkaḷuṭaiyaṉavum, maṟṟum kuṟunila maṉṉarka ḷuṭaiyaṉavumāṉa āṭcimuṟaiyē īṇṭuk kaḻakak kāla āṭci muṟai yākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. mūvēntarēyaṉṟit tōrāyamāka 120 kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷum kaḻakak kālattil āṇṭaṉar. āyiṉum, tamiḻkūṟu nal lulakattil ivarkaḷ naṭattiya āṭcimuṟai orē mātiriyākavē iruntatu. 
piṟaṅkaṭaiyurimai 
paṇṭaikkālattil piṟanāṭukaḷil tōṉṟiyataippōlavē tamiḻakattilum aracappatavi tōṉṟiyatu. mūttavaṉ aracaṉāka vēṇṭumeṉṟa marapuvaḻimuṟai nīṭittatu. tantaikkup piṉ mūtta makaṉ, avaṉukkup piṉ aṭutta mūttamakaṉ eṉṉum muṟaiyilē kaḻakakkāl maṉṉarkaḷ aracuk kaṭṭil ēṟiṉar. ōr aracaṉukku iruvēru maṉaiviyarukkup piṟanta mūṉṟu piḷḷaikaḷ iruntālum avarkaḷiṉ akavaippaṭi mūtta vaṉukkup piṉ iḷaiyavaṉ eṉṟa muṟaiyil muṭicūṭik koṇṭaṉar. piṟaṅkaṭaiyurimaippaṭi mūtta makaṉukkuppiṉ avaṉuṭaiya mūtta makaṉ! aracurimai illai . karikālaṉait tavira vēṟu enta maṉṉaṉum makkaḷāl tērn teṭukkappaṭṭatākat teriyavillai. mūttavaṉ āḷumpōtu iḷaiyavarkaḷ piṟapakutikaḷukku iḷavaracarkaḷāyiruntu mūttōṉukku uṟutuṇaiyāyiruppar. itaṉāl avarkaḷukku. āḷum nāṭṭup pakuti kaḷaik koṇṭē virutuppeyarkaḷum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. tāṉait talai varkaḷ taṉinakaraṅkaḷil vāḻntaṉar. mōkūr eṉṟa ūril vāḻnta cōḻar tāṉaittalaivaṉ “paḻaiyaṉ" eṉṟum, cēramaṉṉariṉ tāṉaittalaivaṉ 'paḻaiyaṉ māṟaṉ' eṉṟum aḻaikkappeṟṟaṉar. potuvāka ivarkaḷai *vayavar perumakaṉ' vīrarkaḷiṉ talaivaṉ) eṉṟē aḻaippar. ittakaiya vayavar perumāṉākavum iḷavaracarkaḷ iruppatuṇṭu. nāmaṟintavarai kaḻakak kālattil makaḷir aracāṇṭatākac cāṉṟillai. pāṇṭiya nāṭ ṭil taṭātakaip pirāṭṭiyār āṇṭatākak kūṟappaṭuvatu toltaic ceytiyē. 
aracavai 
pāṇṭiya, cōḻavēntar taṅkaḷai matikkulam cantirakulam), katirk kulam cūriyakulam ākiyavaṟṟaic cārntavareṉṟum, itikācavīrarkaḷiṉ vaḻivantavareṉṟum kūṟikkoḷvatu akkāla vaḻakku muṭicūṭṭu viḻā naṭakkumpōtu mūttōr avaiyiṉarum, piṟarum kūṭi am maṉṉaṉai ēṟpatāy oppuvar. maṉṉaṉum aṟavaḻi naṭantu kuṭimakkaḷait taṉ makkaḷ pōl kāppāṉ, āyiṉum avaṉukku aṟivurai kūṟap pulavarkaḷum, amaiccarkaḷum, pūcārikaḷum iruppar. potuvāka aracavaiyil iruppavar kuṭi, kalvi, vāymai, tūymai, naṭunilaimai, taṉṉalamiṉmai ākiya paṇpukaḷ niṟainta cāṉṟōrākattikaḻvar. ivarkaḷ aracaṉukku aṭikkaṭi aṟattai valiyuṟutti nayaṉmaiyiṉ kāvalaṉāka irukkumpaṭi ceyvar. aracaṉ aṉṟāṭam aracavaiyaik kūṭṭi, kāṭcik keḷiya ṉākik kuṭimakkaḷaiyum aracavaiyil amarttik kuṟaikēṭṭu muṟai vaḻaṅkuvāṉ. ayalakat tūtuvarkaḷum, ayalaka viruntiṉarum aracavaiyil varavēṟkappaṭṭaṉar. tāymoḻiyām tamiḻaik kaṟṟaṟinta aṟiñar perumakkaḷai vāḻttiyum, pōṟṟiyum paricil vaḻaṅkiyum, aracavaiyil camamāka irukkaiyaḷittum, perumaippaṭuttum marapuvaḻakkam tamiḻaracar yāvariṭattilum iruntatu. aimperuṅkuḻuvum eṇpērāyamum aracamaippiṉ mukāmaiyuṟuppukaḷākum. 
ki.pi. 7 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻunta tivākara nikaṇṭil amaiccar, purōkitar, tāṉaittalaivar, tūtuvar, cāraṇar ākiyōr aimperuṅ kuḻuvil iṭampeṟṟiruntaṉar eṉṟum, karaṇattiyalavar, karuma vitikaḷ, cuṉacuc cuṟṟam, kaṭaikkāppāḷar, nakaramāntar, paṭait talaivar, yāṉai vīrar, kutirai maṟavar ākiyōr eṇpērāyatti liruntaṉar eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. aimperuṅ kuḻuviṉ mantira moḻikaḷai aracaṉ ēṟṟāṉ. itu makkaḷālum ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭa amaippākumeṉṟu oru cārār karutukiṉṟaṉar. itu maṉṉaṉ taṉṉiccai pōl māṟāmal taṭuttu niṟuttum kuḻuveṉpatu cimittiṉ karuttākum, eṇpērāyam araca ṉuṭaiya āṭci muṟaikku uṟutuṇaiyāka niṉṟa ceyaṟkuḻuvāka irun tirukkalām. 
ivarkaḷait tavira tiṟampaṭaitta, nuṇṇaṟivuḷḷa amaiccar kaḷaikkoṇṭa kuḻuvoṉṟum iruntatu. aracaṉai neṟippaṭuttavum, ceyalpaṭāmal taṭuttu niṟuttavum inta amaiccaravaikku atikāram iruntatu. ciṟanta amaiccarkaḷukku virutup peyarkaḷum vaḻaṅkap paṭṭaṉa. 
amaiccarkaḷukku aṭuttapaṭiyāka araciyalil uṟuppāṇmai peṟṟōr tūtuvarkaḷē. ivarkaḷ aracaṉiṉ uḷḷamāka iruntu ataṉai etirolittaṉar. ayalakam ceṉṟu aracariṉ karuttukaḷaik kūṟavum, ayalacu aracariṉ patilkaḷaip peṟṟu vantu kūṟavumāṉa mukāmaic ceyal cuḷil īṭupaṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷilum mūvakaip piriviṉar iruntaṉar. pulavarkaḷ tāmē perumpālāṉa camayaṅkaḷil tūtu ceṉṟa nikaḻcci cuḷaiyum pārkkiṟōm. 
oṟṟar eṉpōr tūtuvariṉum vēṟupaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ vēvu allatu oṟṟup paṇiyaic ceytaṉar. aracaṉiṉ kaṇṇākavum, kātākavum iruntu ceyalpaṭṭa. oṟṟarkaḷ pakaivaraip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷai muṉkūṭṭiyē terintu aṟivippar. ivarkaḷ kamukkak kuṟikaḷaik kaiyāṇṭaṉar. oṟṟarkaḷ mūvakaippaṭuvar eṉṟum, oruvaṉ kūṟiyatai maṟṟa iruvaraik koṇṭu uṟuti ceyta piṉṉarē aracaṉ ceyalpaṭavēṇṭumeṉṟum vaḷḷuvar kūṟukiṟār. tūtuvaṉai araca ṉukku īṭāka matittu naṭatta vēṇṭum. āṉāl, oṟṟaṉaik koṉṟu viṭalām eṉpatu kaḻākkāla marapu. 
paṭai 
paṭai, koṭi, ariyaṇai, vāṉ, veṇcāmaram (kavari), muracu mutaliyavai araciṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāta poruḷkaḷāy matikkap peṟṟaṉa, paṭaiyaṟṟa aracaṉ nilaiyaṟṟa kumiḻiyākak karutappaṭṭāṉ. 'vallāṉ vakuttatē vaḻi" eṉṉum kōṭpāṭu nilaviya ak kālattil paṭai yillāmal enta nāṭum, araciyalum iyaṅka muṭiyātu. ākavē, tamiḻaka maṉṉarkaḷum paṭaikaḷai vaittē makkaḷaik kāttum, piṟa nāṭukaḷaik kavarntum avarkaḷukku ākkamaḷittum vantaṉar. yāṉai, kutirai tēr paṭaikaḷum, kālāḷ paṭaikaḷum ovvoru maṉṉariṭamum iruntaṉa. peruvēntarkaḷiṭam kappaṟpaṭaiyum iruntatu. tamiḻ maṟavar yāvarum vēṟupāṭiṉṟip paṭaiyil cērttukkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉar. vaṭapulatti viruntataip pōl kuṟippiṭṭa cattiriyak kulattavar maṭṭumē paṭaiyil irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟa neṟiyait tamiḻakap paṭaiyil kāṇamuṭiya villai. aracaṉatu meykkāval paṭai avaṉuṭaiya uṭaluṟuppukaḷaip pōl ceyalpaṭṭatu. 
paṭaivīrarkaḷ paṭam kuppāyam, meymmaṟai, vaṭṭuṭai mutaliya āṭaikaḷaiyum, vīrakkaḻalaiyum, kāluṟaiyum aṇintaṉar. vīram ivarkaḷiṉ mūccākavum, vīrap paṇpu ivarkaḷiṉ kurutiyākavu miruntatu. pōrkkaḷattil māḷvōr vīrattuṟakkam aṭaivareṉṟa kōṭpāṭ ṭil ivarkaḷ nampikkaiyuṭaiyavarkaḷ.vīrappiḷḷaikaḷaip peṟṟatāl tāymārkaḷ perumita maṭaintaṉar. peṟṟa vayiṟṟaip pulikkukai yeṉṟum, vīraṉaip puliyeṉṟum kūṟiṉar. pōril māṇṭavarukku vīrakkalnāṭṭi, malarcūṭṭi vaṇaṅkiṉar. avar piṟaṅkaṭaiyarai vīrar vaḻivantōr eṉap pōṟṟiṉar. vīrarkaḷukku aṉṟāṭam muṟaiyāṉa payiṟcikaḷ aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa. ivarkaḷ piṟaviyilēyē vīrarkaḷāyi runtaṉar. muṉvaitta kālaip piṉvaikkātavarum, mārpil ampupaṭṭu māḷpavarum, taṉ talaivaṉukkut taṉṉaiyē paṭaippavarum perum vīrarkaḷākak karutappaṭṭaṉar. nāṭṭuppaṟṟum, ceñcōṟṟuk kaṭṭaṇumē ivarkaḷiṉ kaṭamai varalāṟākum. 
kollarkaḷāl vaṭittaḷikkappaṭṭa vēl, vāḷ, kōl mutaliya tākkum paṭaikaḷaiyum, kēṭayam, kavacam mutaliya kākkum paṭaikaḷaiyum ikkāla vīrarkaḷ kaiyāṇṭatāka aṟikiṟōm. vilvīrarum, vēl vīrarum ik kālattil pukārpaṭap pēcappaṭṭaṉar. tōlālāṉa nakaiyuṟaikaḷum, kāluṟaikaḷum iruntaṉa, vāḷ, vēl, ampu mutaliyaṉa vaikkavum tōluṟaikaḷ iruntaṉa. yāṉai kaḷukkum, kutiraikaḷukkum kūṭattākkum paṭaikaḷum, kākkum paṭaikaḷum poruttappaṭṭaṉa. palavakaippaṭṭa vāḷkaḷum, vēl kaḷum paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷaik kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷi liruntu aṟikiṟōm. 
pōr 
pōrmēṟ celvataṟku muṉ pōr vīrarkaḷ puṉita nāḷil paṭaikaḷai nīrāṭṭi, pūccūṭik kālakkaṇakkar kuṟitta nērattil pōrukkup puṟappaṭuvar. puṟappaṭumuṉ vīrarkaḷum, aracaṉum pakaivarai veṉṟu tirumpuvōm - iṉṟēl cettu maṭivōm' eṉac cūḷuraippar. itaṉai 'vañciṉam' kūṟutaleṉṟu aḻaippar. vāḷ, kuṭai. muracu ākiyavaṟṟuṭaṉ pōr muracam koṭṭa aṇivakuppu ūrvalam naṭakkum, vīrarkaḷ veḷḷāṭaiyaṇintu pōrukkup puṟappaṭuvar. taṅkaḷiṉ paṭaiyeṭuppai muṉkūṭṭiyē pakaiyaracaṉukku aṟivippar. mūppaṭain tōr, kuḻantaikaḷ, ūṉamuṟṟōr, iyalātōr, peṇṭir mutaliyōrai yum, āpacukkaḷaiyum, pārppaṉaraiyum, naṉmakkaṉaiyum veḷiyēṟum paṭi ceyvar. maṟavaṉ maṟavaṉōṭutāṉ mōta vēṇṭum eṉpatu marapup paṇpu. paṭaivīrarkaḷtāṉaittalaivarkaḷaip piṉtoṭarntu celvar, talaivarkaḷum, aracaṉum yāṉai mītamarntu meykkāppāḷaruṭaṉ celvar, avarkaḷ amarntuḷḷa yāṉaikaḷiṉ mītuḷḷa ampārikaḷiṉ mēl koṭikaḷ paṟakkum. ākavē, pakaivar paṭait talaivaraiyum aracaṉaiyum tolaiviliruntē aṭaiyāḷam kaṇṭukoḷvar. 
pōr, perumpālum kōṭaikkālattil camataḷattil naṭakkum. iruvaruṭaiya ellaikaḷaik kuṟikkum kōṭukaḷ allatu iyaṟkai ellaikaḷ amaikkappaṭum. kuṟippiṭṭa nērappaṭiyē pōr toṭaṅkum. tol kāppiyattil pōriṉ aintu nilaikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. avai veṭci, vañci, uḻiñai, tumpai, vākai eṉpaṉavām. pakaivaṉ nāṭṭait tākkum muṉ avaṉuṭaiya kālnaṭaikaḷaik kavarvatu vaḻakkam. itaṟku ānirai kavartal" eṉṟu peyar, ānirai kavarvōr veṭci mālaiyaic cūṭic celvar ivarkaḷ cakuṉam pārttuc celvar. taṟkāppu muyaṟcil īṭupaṭṭavarum, paṭaiyeṭuttuc celvōrum vañci mālaiyaic cūṭiṉar. kōṭṭaiyai muṟṟukaiyiṭuvōr uḻiñai mālaiyaiyum, muṟṟukaiyai uṭaittu veṟṟi peṟuvōr tumpai mālaiyaiyum cūṭuvar. potuvākap pōrk kaḷattil veṟṟi peṟṟōr 'vākai' mālaiyaic cūṭuvar. potuvāka oru nāṭṭaimaṇṇaik cuvarum nōkkattuṭaṉ toṭukkum pōrukku veṭcippōr' eṉṟum, paḻivāṅkum nōkkattōṭu allatu aṭakkiyāḷum nōkkattōṭu veṟṟikku maṭṭum toṭukkumpōrukku vākaip pōreṉṟum peyar. 
pōril puṟamutukiṭṭu ōṭum pakaivaraiyum, āṭaiyiḻantu niṟpōraiyum, mēyccal nilattil vāḻvōraiyum, nīril pāyntōrai yum, paṭaikkalaṅkaḷiṉṟit tavippōraiyum, puṇpaṭ ṭōraiyum, mutiyōr, peṇṭir, kuḻantaikaḷ mutaliyōraiyum pōrkkaḷattil etirttup pōriṭamāṭṭār. muṟṟukaikku uṭpaṭṭavaṉ kōḻaittaṉat tāl uḷḷirukkac ceyvatu mikat tāḻvāka matikkappaṭṭatu. pōrti talaivaṉ allatu aracaṉ pōrkkaḷattil iṟantāl avaṉuṭaiya paṭaikaḷ toṭarntu pōriṭā. pōril kaiyāḷappaṭṭavalakkāraṅkaḷ, uttikaḷ mutaliyaṉa paṟṟiyum aṟikiṟōm. pōrt toṭarntu naṭakkum pōtu vīrarkaḷum, talaivarkaḷum, aracaṉum pācaṟaikaḷamaittu avaṟṟil taṅkuvar. ap pācaṟaikaḷiṉ amaippup paṟṟiya virivāṉa viḷakkaṅkaḷ puṟanāṉūṟṟiliruntu aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ip pācaṟaikaḷ ellā ēntukkaḷnaṭaṉum amaikkappaṭṭu ōr ūraip pōlavē irukkum, itaṉāl pācaṟaikaḷ aṭaṅkiya iruppaik kaṭṭūr' eṉṟaṉar. aracaṉatu pācaṟaiyiṉ kūraiyil avaṉuṭaiya koṭi paṟakkum. aracaṉiṉ pācaṟai ceṅkaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭappeṟṟup pal aṟaikaḷākap pakukkappaṭṭiruntatu.' pakalil maṭṭumē pōr naṭakkum, iravil ōyvu koḷḷumpōtum aracaṉ vīrarkaḷaik kaṇṭu ūkkamūṭṭuvāṉ; maṟunāḷ naṭakkappōkum pōraiyeṇṇiyavāṟu viḻittiruppāṉ. veṟṟiyaṭaintavar viḻāk kōlam pūṇṭu āṭi makiḻvar. vīrarkaḷ takkavāṟu paricaḷikkappaṭuvar. matuvarunti, pēruṇṭimaṇ uṇavu uṇṭu, tuṇaṅkaiyāṭik kaḷippar; koḷḷaip poruḷkaḷ koṇṭip poruḷ, vīrarkaḷukkup pakirn taḷikkappaṭum; cilapōtu kuṭimakkaḷukkum itu pakirntaḷikkappaṭum; vīrarkaḷukkup poṉmalar paricaḷikkum camayaṅkaḷum uṇṭu. tōlvi kaṇṭavarkaḷ palvēṟu tuṉpaṅkaḷukkum āḷāvar. avarkaḷai iḻivāka naṭattuvatum uṇṭu. kaiyil vilaṅku māṭṭutal, talaiyil neyyūṟṟutal - mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkum āḷāvar. kaṉakavicayar tuṟavi vēṭamiṭṭu ōṭiyum, viṭātu piṭittuk kaṇṇakiyiṉ cilaiyaic cumakkac ceytatu īṇṭu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. pakaivariṉ kāval marattai veṭṭi, avaṉuṭaiya uvaḷakattār antappuṟattup peṇkaḷiṉ kūntalai iḻuttu, kayiṟu tirittu, akcayirāl ammarattiṉ aṭippākattaik kaṭṭiyāṉaiyaik koṇṭu iḻuttu vantu pōr muracam ceyvatum, tōṟṟa aracaṉiṉ kōṭṭai kaḷaiyum, piṟa kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷaiyum iṭittu niravik kaḻutai kaḷaik kaṭṭi ēr uḻutalum, ūrkaḷait tīyiṭṭuk koḷuttuvatum ak kāla vaḻakka mākum. 
pōrkkaḷaṅkaḷilum pulavarkaḷ nuḻaintu irutarattārukkum aṟivurai kūṟip pōrait taṭuttuḷḷaṉar. itaip pōlavē pōrait toṭaṅkāmal kāttatum uṇṭu. pakaivaṉ paṇintāl maṉṉittu avaṉ nāṭṭaiyum, uṭaimaikaḷaiyum avaṉukkē tiruppit taruvatum uṇṭu. pōrilum aṟavaḻiyē kaṭaipiṭikkappaṭṭatu, 
muṟai vaḻaṅkutal 
paṭaikkum nayaṉmaikkum nītikkum) aracaṉē talaivaṉ. araca ṉuṭaiya kolumaṇṭapamē aṟanītimaṉṟam, avaṉuṭaiya avaiyil evarum muṟaiyiṭṭut tīrppup peṟalām. perumpālāṉa cuḻacukkāl maṉṉarkaḷ paṉṉūṟ paṇṭitarkaḷāyiruntatāl nīti nūlkaḷiṉpaṭiyē tīrppu vaḻaṅkuvar. maṉaccāṉṟē avarkaḷiṉ tīrppukku aḷavukōl. āyariṉum, aṟivurai kūṟa aṟiñarkaḷum iruntaṉar, avarkaḷaik kalakkāmalē aracaṉ tīrppu vaḻaṅkiyatum uṇṭu. āṉāl aracaṉ illāmal avaiyiṉar tīrppu aḷippatillai. iṟaivaṉaic cāṉṟiṭṭu vaḻakkukaḷ ucāvappaṭṭaṉa. aracaṉ nayaṉmaiyil piḻaiceytāl nāṭṭil paruvamaḻai poykkum. kaṟpuṭaiya makaḷir arukuvar; tavam keṭum eṉṟu makkaḷ nampiṉar. nayaṉmaiyil piḻai ceyta aracaṉ vaḻittōṉṟalkaḷai naṉmakkaḷ matiyār, pulavarkaḷ pāṭamāṭṭārkaḷ.' aracaṉ tāṉē kuṟṟavāḷiyeṉatterintālum taṉakkuttāṉē taṇṭaṉai yaḷittuk koḷvāṉ. kuṟṟavāḷi taṉ makkaḷāyiṉum, uṟaviṉarāyiṉum allatu naṇparkaḷāyiṉum tappāmal taṇṭippāṉ. koṭukkal, vāṅkal, uṭaṉpaṭikkai, oppantaṅkaḷ mutaliya poruḷiyal kuṟṟaṅkaḷum, aracurimaikaḷukku aḻivutēṭal, pārppaṉaṉaik kollutal, āppaka) maṭiyaṟuttal, karukkalaippu, mānta uyirkkolai, mutaliya peruṅ kuṟṟaṅkaḷum ak kālattil naṭantaṉa. piṉkūṟappaṭṭa payaṅkarak kuṟṟaṅ cuḷukkuk kolaittaṇṭaṉai talaiyai veṭṭutal, yāṉaik kāvil iṭṭuk kollutal mutaliyaṉa) vitikkappaṭṭaṉa. piṟaṉil viḻaiyum kuṟṟattiṟkum, māṟṟāḷ kaṇavaṉai virumpum kuṟṟattiṟkum uṟuppu kaḷaic citaikkum taṇṭaṉaiyum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. tiruṭṭuk kuṟṟattiṟ kuttaṇṭamum, ciṟaittaṇṭaṉaiyum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. kappalil tuṭupput taḷḷutal, kōyilukku viḷakkēṟṟutal, potuppaṇikaḷaic ceytal mutaliya taṇṭaṉaikaḷum vitikkappaṭṭaṉa. poṉ, yāṉai, kutirai, māṭu, āṭu mutaliyavaṟṟai iḻappīṭākap peṟum taṇṭaṉaikaḷum vaḻaṅkap paṭṭaṉa. 
- kari cāṭcikaḷ ucāvappaṭuvatum, pāmpukkuṭattil kaiviṭumpaṭi ceytu kuṟṟavāḷiyai aṟitalum uṇṭu terintē cāṉṟu kūṟa maṟuppava rum, poyc cāṉṟu kūṟuvōrum taṇṭaṉai peṟṟaṉar. kātalittuk kaiviṭṭa oruvaṉaip piṭittu avaiyārē tirumaṇam ceyvitta nikaḻcciyum uṇṭu. aracukkuc celutta vēṇṭiya vari kaḷaic celuttāta varkaḷum taṇṭaṉaip peṟṟaṉar. kaḻakakkālattil ciṟaikaḷ nilaiyāṉa kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷākavē iruntaṉa. ciṟaiyil kuṟṟavāḷikaḷ toṭari caṅkilira yāl piṇaikkap paṭṭiruntaṉar. kuṟṟavāḷikaḷukku nīr koṭukkāmalum, uṇaviṭāmalum, cittiravatai ceyyappaṭuvatuṇṭu. āyiṉum, avarkaḷai nōyāḷikaḷaippōl aṉpāka naṭatta vēṇṭumeṉpatu akkāla neṟi. 
nakaraṅkaḷum mārkaḷum 
nakaraṅkaḷilum ūrkaḷilum taṉittaṉi maṉṟaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. ivai taṅkaḷukkuṭpaṭṭa niruvākattaiyum, muṟaivaḻaṅkum tiṭṭaṅkaḷaiyum kavaṉittaṉa. perumpālāṉa ūr maṉṟaṅkaḷ marattaṭiyilum, kuḷak karaiyilum allatu potukkaṭṭaṭattilum kūṭiṉa. oḻukkam, kalvi, pakti ākiyavaṟṟai kāril vaḷarkkum talaimaiyirukkaiyē inta harmaṉ naṅkaḷ. ivaṟṟil akavai mutirntōrum, kalvi kēḷvikaḷil ciṟantō rum paṅkēṟṟaṉar. nāṭu kōṭṭaṅkaḷākavum, ūṉarkaḷākavum pirikkappaṭ ṭiruntatu. kōṭṭaṅkaḷ iḷavaracarkaḷāl iḷaṅkō) āḷappaṭṭaṉa. tuṟaimukappaṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ - vaṇika nakaraṅkaḷ, kōṭṭat talainakaraṅkaḷ, aracaṉiṉ kōnakaram, paṭaittalaivar vāḻnta nakaraṅkaḷ eṉṟu nakaraṅkaḷ palavakaiyāy amaintu nakara vāḻkkai ciṟantu kāṇappaṭṭatu. ivai akaḻiyālum cūḻappaṭṭiruntaṉa. araṇmaṉai kōyil' eṉappaṭṭatu. itaṉait taṉikkāvalarkaḷ kāttaṉar. ivarkaḷ porunar, pulavar, paraṇar, pārppaṉar ākiyōrai maṭṭumē taṭaiyiṉṟi uṭcella icaivar. āṉāl, araciyāriṉ irukkaikku araṇmaṉaiyaic cērntavar tavira piṟa āṭavarcella icaiyār. araṇmaṉaikkuḷḷēyē uvaḷakam iruntatu. itilirunta aḻakiya makaḷir urimai makaḷir' eṉappaṭṭaṉar. 
varuvāy 
kaḻakakkālattil āṭciyaic cevvaṉē naṭatta varuvāy tēvaip paṭṭatu. itaṟkākap pala vitikaḷiṉ varikaḷ taṇṭappaṭṭaṉa. koṭuppō rukkut tuṉpam ēṟpaṭātavāṟum, avarkaḷē maṉamuvantu koṭukku māṟum varittaṇṭal ceytaṉar. viḷaiccalil āṟil oru paṅku variyākac celuttappaṭṭatu. cila camayaṅkaḷil varittaḷḷupaṭi ceytalum uṇṭu. aracaṉukku nilavariyait tavira ulku vari, pōril kiṭaitta koṇṭip poruḷ, 'ciṟṟaracarkaḷum, tamakku aṭaṅkiyāḷvōrum celuttiya tiṟaikaḷ, urimaiyaṟṟu niṟkum cottukaḷ mutaliyaṉavum varuvāyāka vantaṉ.vāṇipam ceyvōr taṉippaṭṭa muṟaiyil vaṇikavaricelutti ṉar. nayaṉmainīti maṉṟaṅkaḷil vitikkappaṭum taṇṭaṅkaḷum, ēṟṟumati, iṟakkumatip poruḷkaḷiṉ mītu vitikkappaṭṭa varikaḷum, aṉpaḷippāka aḷikkappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷum varuvāykaḷē. 
aḷavaikaḷ 
kaḻakakkālattil nīṭṭalaḷavai, mukattalaḷavai, eṭuttalaḷavai, eṇṇal aḷavai ākiyavaiyum vaḻakkattil iruntaṉa. cuḻiccikkāy (cuḻaṅku eṉpatu eṭai aḷavākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu. toḷḷāyiram, āyiram, nūṟāyiram, veḷḷam, āmpal, tāmarai mutaliya eṇṇap peyarkaḷum, kāṇi, muntiri mutaliya piṉṉaṅ kaḷum vaḻakkil iruntaṉa. kaṉṉattaik kuṟiccup pāḻ' eṉṟa col payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. mā, vēli mutaliyaṉa nila aḷavaik kuṟittaṉa. ampaṇam eṉpatu mukatta vaḷavaiyākum. itu oru marakkālukkuc camamākalām. taṅkam tulāk kōliṉāl niṟukkappaṭṭatu. atu 'cā' eṉappaṭṭatu tāṭi' eṉpatu eṭaiyil mikac ciṟiya aḷavāka irukkalām, kā, kāṉam, poṉ mutaliyaṉa poṉṉiṉ eṭaiyaik kuṟittaṉa. ivaipoṟkācukaḷākavum irukkalām. ēṉeṉil kaḻakak kālattil tamiḻakattuṭaṉ vāṇipam ceyta urōmar, poṟkācukaḷaiyē kaiyāṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. ākavē, ivarka ḷum poṟkācukaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttiyirukkalām, ākap paṇṭamāṟṟa muṟaikkum mēmpaṭṭa kācup paḻakkattaiyuṭaiya ciṟanta nākarikattaik kaḻakakkālat tamiḻarkaḷ peṟṟiruntaṉar. - 
(ū) kaḻakakkālap poruḷiyalum vāṇikamum
vēḷāṇmai 
kaḻakakkālak kumukāyattil ēṟṟat tāḻvukaḷ iruppataik kaṇṭōm. avarkaḷ vāḻnta iṭaṅkaḷum ceyta toḻilkaḷumē iv vakai ēṟṟat tāḻvukaḷukkuk kāraṇiyaṅkaḷāyamaintaṉa. vēḷāṇmai ik kālattil ciṟantu viḷaṅkiya toḻil, intu uyirnāṭiyāṉatum ākum. ūrppuṟattu makkaḷēyaṉṟi nakaraṅkaḷil vāḻnta makkaḷil oru cārārum it toḻil ceytaṉar. ittoḻilukku iṉṟiyamaiyāta māṭukaḷaip peṟṟiruntaṉar. vaikkōlāl vēynta vīṭukaḷilē vāḻntārkaḷ eṉpatai marutanila vāḻkkaiyāl aṟikiṟōm. ivarkaḷiṭam irunta kāḷaikaḷ, kalappaikaḷ, ivaṟṟiṟkāṉa koṭṭilkaḷ mutaliya vaṟṟaik koṇṭu oru kalappai uḻavaik koṇṭavarum, pala kalappai uḻavaik koṇṭavarkaḷum iruntaṉar eṉa aṟikiṟōm. maruta nilattu uṟavumuṟai ēṟakkuṟaiya iṉṟaiya tamiḻnāṭṭu uḻavu muṟaiyaip pōlavē iruntatu. cēṟṟil nāṟṟu naṭuvatu, puḻutiyil vitaippatu, aṟuttal, aṭittal, kuvittal yāvum iṉṟupōl aṉṟum naṭantaṉa. 'veṇṇel" cen nel' eṉṟu palavakaiyāṉa nel vakaikaḷum payiriṭappeṟṟaṉa. karumpup payirpaṟṟiyum, karumpālai paṟṟiyum aṟikiṟōm. nellai yaṟuttavuṭaṉ uḷuntuppayirum piṉṉar curumpum payiriṭappeṟṟaṉa. nañcaikku āṟu, kuḷaṅkaḷiliruntu nīrp pāyccappaṭṭatu. maruta nilattil perumpālum naṉceyp payirkaḷum piṟa nilaṅkaḷil puṉceyp payirkaḷum payiriṭap peṟṟaṉa, varaku, tiṉai, eḷ, avarai mutaliya puṉceyta tavacaṅ kaḷum, malainellumaivaṉamtōrai eṉappaṭum oruvakai nellum, iñci, aiyavi kaṭuku, kavalaik kiḻaṅku mutaliyavaiyum kuṟiñci mullai nilaṅ kaḷil viḷaivikkappeṟṟaṉa. karumpum, vāḻaiyum mullaiyilum viḷain taṉa. mūṅkil nellum, nāvaṟkaṉiyum, uyavaik koṭiyum, kūvaik kiḻaṅkum, mā, palā, teṉṉai mutaliyaṉavum iyaṟkaiyāka viḷaintaṉa. kōṭaikkālattil oru vakaip parutti payiriṭappeṟṟatu. vēḷāṇmai yil īṭupaṭṭōr ōraḷavu taṉṉiṟaivōṭu vāḻntaṉar. āyiṉum iṉṟupōl aṉṟum ivarkaḷiṉ allal tīravillai. 
āyar toḻil 
vēḷāṇmaiyaiyaṭuttuk kaḻakakkālattil āṭu, māṭu vaḷarttal oru mukāmaittoḻilāka iruntatu. māṭukaḷ vēḷāḷariṉ kūṭṭāḷikaḷ, āṭukaḷ pālukkum iṟaiccikkum piṟavaṟṟiṟkum payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. āpacu), kāḷai, erumai, nāku mutaliya māṭukaḷiṉ ciṟappukaḷum, kuṟumpai , varuṭai mutaliya āṭukaḷiṉ ciṟappukaḷum, ivaṟṟai vaḷarkkum muṟaikaḷum piṟavum aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. 
piṟa toḻilāḷarkaḷ 
vēḷāḷarukku uṟutuṇaiyāyirunta caṅkaṟuppōr, maṇikaḷai tuḷaippōr, poṉṉakai ceyvōr, poṉṉai māṟṟuk kāṇpōr, tuṇi, cempu, curālai(cāmpirāṇi) mutaliyaṉa viṟpōr, ōviyar 
mutalāṉa yāvaraiyum cummiyar eṉṟē maturaikkāñci kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. kollaṉum, taccaṉum vēḷāṇmaikkum, vīṭu kaṭṭutalukkum, tēr ceytalukkum, araṇmaṉai yamaittalukkumāka makkaḷ nākarikattiṉ mutukelumpāy niṉṟārkaḷ. karuvikaḷum, paṭaikaḷum ivarkaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. ākavē, ivarkaḷaip paṟṟic caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ palapaṭap pēcukiṉṟaṉa. cempu, veḷḷi ākiyavaṟṟālum ēṉaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. kuyavar maṇpāṇṭam ceytaṉar. poṟkollar nakaikaḷ ceytaṉar. parutti, paṭṭu āṭaikaḷai neytavarkaḷaip paṟṟiyum, kacaṅkāta āṭaikaḷai neytavarkaḷaip paṟṟiyum aṟikiṟōm, eli mayiraik koṇṭu tāvari kampaṉ) āṭaikaḷ neyyappaṭṭaṉa. paruttiyaip patappaṭutti nāṟṟal peṇkaḷukkum, neytal āṇkaḷukkum uriya parikaḷāyiruntaṉa. kaṭaṟkaraippakutiyil vāḻntōr mīṉpiṭikkum toḻil ceytaṉar. ularttiyum uppiṭṭum mīṉkaḷaip patappaṭuttum muṟaikaḷaiyum ivarkaḷ aṟintiruntaṉar. uppu eṭukkum toḻilum kaṭalōraṅkaḷil naṭantatu. muttuk kuḷittal, caṅkaṟuttal, tōl pataṉiṭutal mutaliya toḻilkaḷum naṭaipeṟṟaṉa. naṟṟiṇaiyil tōl vēlai ceypavaṉ iḻintavaṉākakkuṟippiṭappaṭuvatāl toḻil vaḻic cātikaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa eṉpatai aṟiya muṭikiṟatu. 
vāṇikam
uḷnāṭṭu vāṇikam 
kaḻakakkālattil uḷnāṭṭu vāṇikamum, ayalnāṭṭu vāṇikamum naṭantaṉa. kaḻaka ilakkiyak kuṟippukaḷ perumpālum uḷnāṭṭu vāṇikattaip paṟṟiyum, kirēkkak kuṟippukaḷ ayalnāṭṭu vāṇikattaip paṟṟiyum aṟiya utavukiṉṟaṉa. uḷnāṭṭu vāṇikattiṟku iṉṟiyamaiyāta pōkkuvarattup pātaikaḷum, piṟatuṇaikkāraṇikaḷum akkālattil atikamillai . āyiṉum vaṇṭikaḷiṉ mītu tavacaṅkaḷaiyum uppu mutaliya vaṟṟaiyum ēṟṟicceṉṟu viṟṟaṉar. vaṇikarukkut tuṇaiyākak kāvalarkaḷum ceṉṟaṉar.uppuvaṇikar umaṇar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷ paratavar allar. ivarkaḷ nāṭōṭikaḷākat tirintavarkaḷ. kūṭṭam - kūṭṭamāy vaṇṭikaḷil uppai ēṟṟikkoṇṭu ōr ūrē nakaruvataip pōl kuṭumpaṅkaḷuṭaṉ ūr ūrākac ceṉṟu ivarkaḷ uppu vaṇikam appuṟam ceytaṉar. kuṟippiṭam viṟpōrum, ceytaṉar. āṟalaik kaḷvarālum, maḻaiyālum ivarkaḷiṉ vaṇikap poruḷukkut tīṅku nēriṭṭatu. kaḻutaikaḷiṉ mītum ivarkaḷ uppai ēṟṟicceṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷaippōlavē tavaca vāṇikarkaḷum, malaip poruḷkaḷaiyum kaṭaṟporuḷkaḷaiyum viṟpōrum, paṇṭamāṟṟu muṟaiyil vāṇikam ceytaṉar. kuṟippiṭṭac cālaikaḷ cērumiṭaṅkaḷi lum, ellaip puṟaṅkaḷilum cuṅkaccāvaṭikaḷ iruntaṉa. ic cāvaṭikaḷē ittakaiya vaṇikarkaḷukku paṇṭamāṟṟum nilaiyaṅkaḷākavum payaṉ paṭṭaṉa. ciṟṟūrkaḷilum, pērūrkaḷilum ittakaiya poruḷkaḷai viṟkum aṅkāṭikaḷ iruntaṉa, terukkaḷil āymakaḷ tayir, ney mutaliyavaṟṟai viṟṟuviṭṭu nellaiyum, uppaiyum vāṅkic ceṉṟāḷ. pāṉmakaḷ mīṉavap peṇ) itaip pōlavē mīṉaiviṟṟu uṇavut talacaṅkaḷai vāṅkic ceṉṟāḷ. uḻavappeṇ putumalarkaḷait terukka ḷil viṟṟāḷ. ivvāṟu, mottamāka ūr ūrāka vantu viṟpōrum, kuṟippiṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷil cantai māṟṟik koḷvōrum, ūr aṅkāṭi vaṇikarum, teruvil viṟpōrum ak kālap poruḷ naṭamāṭṭattiṟku ūkkamaḷittaṉar. '
nakara aṅkāṭikaḷ 
maturai. pūmpukār mutaliya nakaraṅkaḷilirunta nāḷaṅkāṭi avlaṅ kāṭi mutaliya kaṭaivītikaḷaip paṟṟiyum, avaṟṟil viṟkappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷaip paṟṟiyum ēṟkeṉavē kūṟiviṭṭōm. ākavē, nakaraṅ karil naṭanta vāṇikattaippaṟṟi vivarikkat tēvaiyillai, 
ayalaka vāṇikam 
tamiḻakattil mūṉṟu pakkattilum nīr cūḻntiruppatāl kaṭal kaṭantum vaṇikat toṭarpukaḷ toṉṟutoṭṭē iruntuvantaṉa. uppu eṭuttal, muttuk kuḷittal, kappal kaṭṭutal, kaittoḻilkaḷ ākiyavai ayalaka vāṇikattiṟku uṟutuṇaiyāyiruntaṉa. muṭiyuṭai vēntariṉ kaṭal āḷumaiyum, ayalakaṅkaḷil tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ celvākku ōṅki vaḷarak karaṇiyamākiyatu. tamiḻarkaḷ palatarappaṭṭa kalṅkaḷ kaṭṭik kaṭal vāṇikattaiyum, kaṭal āḷumaiyum peṟṟaṉar eṉpataik kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntum, ayalakak kuṟippukaḷiliruntum aṟikiṟōm. vaṅkam, nāvāy eṉṉum kalaṅkaḷ peruṅkaṭalil neṭuntolaivu ceṉṟaṉa. ivaiyāṉaikaḷ pōṉṟu periyavai, kaṭalil niṟkum kappal kaḷiliruntu carakkukaḷai iṟakkuvataṟkum allatu ēṟṟuvataṟkum payaṉ paṭṭavai tōkarikaḷeṉṟum, ivai kaḻikaḷ mūlam curaikaḷaic cēru meṉṟum, kaṭaṟkarai ōraṅkaḷiluḷḷa ūrkaḷuṭaṉ vaṇikam ceyyap payaṉpaṭṭavai eṉṟum, paḵṟi eṉpatu oruvakaik kālam eṉpataiyum, ittakaiya paḵṟikaḷ kutiraikaḷaippōl varicaiyākakkaraikaḷil niṉṟu, koḷḷut tiṉṉum kutiraikaḷ talaiyai acaippataippōl ivai acaintu niṉṟaṉaveṉṟum aṟikiṟōm. ākavē, nāvāy, vaṅkam, tōṇi, paḵṟi mutaliya kalaṅkaḷum ampi, timil eṉappaṭum mīṉpiṭi kalaṅkaḷum ik kālattil iruntataiyaṟikiṟōm. kipi 75-l eḻutappaṭṭa *erittiriyak kaṭaṟcelvu' eṉṉum kirēkka nāvil pala maraṅkaḷaik koṇṭu kaṭṭappeṟṟa 'caṅkarā'(caṅkāṭam eṉṟa kalamum, mikap periya urukkoṇṭa kolāṇṭiyā' eṉṟa kalamum, karaiyōramākac cel lum nāṭṭuk kalamumāka mūvakaik kalaṅkaḷ tamiḻakattil iruntatākak kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṟatu. 
kalaṅkaḷaippaṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷaip pōlavē tamiḻakat tuṟai mukaṅkaḷaip paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷum ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum, kaḻaka ayalcuc cāṉṟukaḷilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa, mēṟkuk karaiyil muciṟi, pantar, koṭumaṇam mutaliya tuṟaimukaṅkaḷum, kiḻakkuk karaiyil koṟkai, maruṅkūrppaṭṭiṉam, kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam, eyiṟpaṭṭiṉam mutaliya tuṟaimukaṅkaḷum iruntaṉa. mēṟkūṟiya 'erittiriyak kaṭaṟcelavu" eṉṉum nūlil nakarai, tiṇṭicu ākiya tuṟaimukaṅkaḷ kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nakara eṉpatu kaṇṇaṉūrākavumnaṟavu), tiṇṭik toṇṭiyākavum irukkalām. mēlum, in nūlil ivai cēranāṭṭut tuṟaikaḷ eṉavum cuṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tālami eṉṟa kirēkka āciriyarum it tuṟaikaḷaiyum, yavaṉarkaḷil kappalkaḷ vantuceṉṟa vakaikaḷaiyum, vaṇikam ceyta poruḷkaḷaiyum paṟṟik kuṟippiṭukiṟāṉ. pakkaraiyeṉṟa tuṟaiyaip paṟṟip piḷaiṉi kuṟippiṭukiṟāṉ. itu vaikkaraiyākalām. nīrkkuṉṟam, vaikkarai ākiya ivai kuṭṭa nāṭṭilirunta tuṟaimukaṅkaḷ. itaṟkum teṟkē paraliyā' eṉṟa tuṟai iruntatākak kirēkkarkaḷ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷa ṉar. itu 'palita' (varkkalai eṉṉum tuṟaiyākalām. kīḻkkaraiyil kolki, kamara potukkē, cōpaṭmam mutaliya tuṟaimukaṅ kaḷiruntataik kaṭaṟcelavu nūl kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. ivai muṟaiyē koṟkai, kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉam, putuccēri, marakkāṇam ākiya tuṟaikaḷākalām. koṟkai yil muttukkuḷittalaip paṟṟi in nūlil vekuvākap pēcappaṭukiṟatu. iṉṟu putuvaikkarukil arikkamēṭṭil naṭattap peṟṟa akaḻvā rāycci in nūliṉ kūṟṟai meyppikkiṟatu. ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭai, perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭai ākiyavaṟṟil muṟaiyē eyiṟpaṭṭiṉam, nīrp peyaṟṟu eṉṟa tuṟaimukaṅkaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟil nīrp peyaṟṟu iṉṟaiya māmallapuram ākalām. 
paṇṭaiya tamiḻakattuṭaṉ kirēkkam, urōmap pēraracu, ekiptu, arēpiyā ākiya mēlai nāṭukaḷum, malaināṭu malēyā), kiḻakkintiyat tīvukaḷ, īḻam, cīṉam mutaliya cīḻaināṭukaḷum vāṇikat toṭarpu koṇ ṭiruntaṉa. kirēkkam teṉṉakattōṭu nēraṭit toṭarpu koṇṭiruntatu. alekcāntiriyā intiya vāṇikap poruḷkaḷāl ciṟappuṟṟatu. , urōmap pēraracu vāḻntataṟkum, vīḻntataṟkum, tamiḻakattōṭu atu koṇṭirunta vāṇikat toṭarpē karaṇiyam eṉiṉum mikaiyākātu. āṇṭoṉṟukku ēṟattāḻa 120 kappalkaḷ urōmāpurikkum teṉṉakat - tiṟkum iṭaiyē naṭamāṭiṉa. paṇṭaiya tamiḻakam mēlai nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ tāṉ atikamāṉa neruṅkiya vāṇikat toṭarpukoṇṭiruntatu, iḻai nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ avvaḷavu neruṅkiya toṭarpukaḷ illai, itai iṉṟaiya akaḻvārāyccikaḷ valiyuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa. poṉṉai yēṟṟi vanta yavaṉariṉ kalaṅkaḷ poṉṉaik koṭuttu miḷakaiyum, kaṭal viḷai poruḷkaḷaiyum, malaipaṭuporuḷkaḷaiyum ēṟṟic ceṉṟaṉa. 'puṭpa rākam' eṉṉum ariya maṇikkallum, melliya tuṇikaḷum, ōviya vēlaippāṭukaḷ niṟainta nārāṭaikaḷum liṉaṉ, añcaṉakkal, pavaḷam, kaṇṇāṭi, cempu, takaram, matuvakaikaḷ, tāḷakam aritāram, centakāḷam oruvakai maruntu mutaliyaṉavum iṟakkumati ceyyap paṭṭaṉa. palavakai muttukaḷ, yāṉai maruppu, paṭṭut tuṇi, kaṅkaip pakutiyil kiṭaitta ilāmiccai, marapattiram, kaṇṇāṭik kaṟkaḷ, vayiram, nīlam, caṅku mutaliya poruḷkaḷ iṅkiruntu mēlai nāṭukaḷukku ēṟṟumatiyāyiṉa, intiyāviṉ uḷnāṭṭup pakutikaḷiliruntu vanta poruḷkaḷum, kiḻakkintiyat tīvukaḷiliruntu vanta poruḷkaḷum tamiḻakat tuṟaimukaṅkaḷiliruntu ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. itaip pōlavē yavaṉarkaḷ arapu nāṭukaḷilum, naṇṇilak kaṭaṟpakutikaḷilum, piṟa airōppiya nāṭukaḷilum kiṭaitta poruḷkaḷai iṟakkumati : ceytaṉar. ivarkaḷukkeṉṟu paṇṭaka cālaikaḷum, kuṭiyiruppukaḷum tamiḻakattil ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. ittakaiya ēṟṟumati, iṟakkumatikaḷāl aracukkuc cuṅkavari atikam kiṭaittatu. māmallapurattil nīrppeyaṟṟu) uyarnta māṭi vīṭukaḷ iruntaṉa. vāṇikac ceṭṭikaḷ (paratavar) irunta ṉar; veḷḷaik kutiraikaḷum, kaṅkaip pakutiyil viḷainta poruḷ kaḷum it tuṟaiyil atikam kāṇappaṭṭaṉa; iṅku uyarnta kalaṅkarai viḷakkamiruntateṉap perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭai kūṟukiṟatu. ēṟṟumati yāṉa poruḷkaḷil ēlakkāy, akil, cantaṉam mutaliya naṟumaṇap poruḷkaḷum, yāṉai maruppu, vayiram mutaliyavai kuṟainta aḷavilum, miḷaku muttu, paṭṭu, ariya kalvakaikaḷ periya aḷavilum iruntaṉa. iṟakkumatiyāṉa poruḷkaḷil poṟkācukaḷum, kutiraikaḷum, matuvakai kaḷum atikamāka iruntaṉa. ittakaiya vāṇikap poruḷkaḷāl ciṟappaṭainta tamiḻaka nakaraṅkaḷum, ayal nāṭṭu nakaraṅkaḷum piṟkālattil varalāṟṟilum, ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum iṭampeṟṟaṉa. 
(e) kaḻakak kālak kumukāyam
meypporuḷ vāḻvu 
cuḻacukkālat tamiḻkkumukāyam vāḻkkai neṟiyaic ciṟanta paṭṭāṅkukaḷiṉ tattuvaṅkaḷiṉ mēl kaṭṭiyiruntatu. taṉakkeṉa vāḻāt takaimaiyāḷarē kaḻakakkālat tamiḻar. taṉṉaiyum, taṉṉuṭaiya āṟṟavaiyum vaḷarttu ataṉvaḻi taṉṉaic cārnta kumukāyattiṟkum, nāṭṭiṟkum, moḻikkum vaḷarcci tantu, aṟaneṟi niṉṟu, vāḻvāṅku vāḻum neṟiyē aṉṟaiya tamiḻkkumukāya neṟiyākum. ākavē, paṇṭaiya kirēkkaraip pōl – araciyaluk kākavē vāḻum kōṭ pāṭō, ivvulaka iṉpattai maṭṭum nukarum kōṭpāṭō, tuṟakkat taiyē yeṇṇi ulaka iṉpaṅkaḷait tuṟakkum camayak kōṭpāṭō taṉittu ivarkaḷiṭam kāṇappaṭa villai. tām vāḻum iṭam, kālam ākiyavaṟṟai mutaṟ poruḷākavum, uyiriṉaṅkaḷaiyum ulakap poruḷ kaḷaiyum karup poruḷākavum koṇṭatāl tamiḻar ulakiyal vāḻkkaiyai maṉamāra nukarntu. tāmum vaḷarntu, ulakaiyum vaḷarttuc. ciṟanta toru nākarikattait tam aṭiccuvaṭāka viṭṭucceṉṟaṉar. tām vāḻnta iṭattaik kuṟiñci, mullai, marutam, neytal, pālai eṉṉum aintiṇaikaḷākavum, kālattaik kālai, naṇpakal, ēṟpāṭu, mālai, yāmam, vaikaṟai eṉṉum ciṟupoḻutukaḷākavum, noṭi, nāḻikai, nāṉ, tiṅkaḷ, āṇṭu eṉavum, taṭpaveppattiṟkēṟpak kār, kūtir, muṉpaṉi, piṉpaṉi, iḷavēṉil, mutuvēṉil ākiyavai yākavum pirittu vāḻkkai yaik kālattiṟkēṟpak kaṇakkiṭṭu naṭattiṉar. kālam poṉ pōṉṟatu; kaṭamai kaṇ pōṉṟatu eṉak karutiya ivarkaḷiṉ vāḻkkai neṟiyāl kumukāyam eṇṇip pārkka muṭiyāta aḷavukku ēṟṟam peṟṟatil viyappillai . 
nāṉilattilum vāḻnta makkaḷ kātalāl oṉṟukūṭi vāḻum illaṟa vāḻkkaiyai 'akam' eṉṟum, ataṟkāṉa taḷaṉṟu kōlāyuḷḷa araciyalum, pōrum, poruḷīṭṭalum 'puṟam' eṉṟum koṇṭaṉar. aṟamum, poruḷumuṭaiya vāḻkkaiyē iṉpamāyiṟṟu. eṉavētāṉ, caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil perumpālāṉavai akapporuḷ paṟṟiyē pēcukiṉṟaṉa. āṇum peṇṇum kātalittu illaṟam mēṟkoḷḷum nilaiyil maṭṭum amaiyātu maṇṇum poṉṉum akavāḻvukku aṭippaṭaiyāka amaintatāl iv vāḻvukkup puṟattēyum avarkaḷ ceyalpaṭṭaṉar. kātal kaḷavākippiṉ kaṭimaṇa māka muṭiyum. piṉṉar kaṟpu neṟiyaik kaṭaippiṭittu oḻukutal ivarkaḷiṉ kaṭaimai yāyiṟṟu. pōr mēṟceṉṟu veṟṟi peṟutalum, tiraikaṭal ōṭip poruḷ tēṭalum āṇiṉ puṟa oḻukkamāyiṟṟu. avvāṟu pōr mēṟcollum pōtu vīramaraṇameytuvatum, veṟṟi peṟuvatum ciṟappeṉṟē karutap paṭṭaṉa.vīrac cāvu eytuvatum, ivarkaḷiṉ maṟuvulakak kōṭpāṭākum. tuka vāḻvu ciṟakka ayalakam ceṉṟu poruḷ tēṭaliṉ poruṭṭum, ōtaliṉ poruṭṭum, pōrkkaḷam pōtaliṉ poruṭṭum kātalaṉ kātaliyaip pirintu celvāṉ. pōr mēṟceṉṟavaṉ kār kālattil tirumpi varuvatāyk kūṟic celvāṉ. avaṉ varum kār kālattai iyaṟkaiyiṉ aṟikuṟikaḷ kātalikku niṉaivūṭṭum, kātali avaṉ varukaiyaiyeṇṇi vāṭi, melintu, vaḷaikaḻaṉṟu vaḻimēl viḻi vaittu nōkkuvāḷ. kātalaṉ veṟṟic celvattuṭaṉ tirumpuvāṉ. kuḷirkālam muḻuvatum kūṭik kaḷippuṟṟu vāḻvar. ivvāṟu kālam, iṭam (nilam) ākiyavaṟṟiṟkēṟpa avarkaḷ naṭattiya nāṉila vāḻkkai neṟi yiṉaic curuṅkak kūṟuvōm. 
potut taṉmaikaḷ 
paṇṭaiya tamiḻkkumukāyattil āṇum peṇṇum kūṭi vāḻum amaippākiya 'kuṭumpamē" mukāmai uṟuppākum. kuṭumpattalaivaṉ kiḻavaṉ eṉṟum, talaivi maṉaiyōḷ eṉṟum aṟiyappaṭṭaṉar. pala kuṭumpaṅkaḷ cērntu vāḻnta iṭam *cēri' eṉappaṭṭatu. pala cērikaḷaik koṇṭatu ōr uḷar. ovvōr paḷarilum toḻil vaḻi makkaḷ. aṟiyappaṭṭaṉar. itaṉ vaḻi avarkaḷ vāḻnta cēriyum aḻaikkap paṭṭatu. ovvoru toḻiṟkuḻuvukkumcērikkum talaivaṉ ēṟpaṭ ṭāṉ. it talaivarkaḷiṉ kīḻ kuḻu maṉappāṉmai vaḷarntu kumukāyak kaṭṭukkōppukkut tuṇaipurintatu. 
paṇṭait tamiḻar vāḻnta iṭaṅkaḷukkēṟpa mēṟkaṇṭavāṟu cērntu vāḻnta cērikaḷaṭaṅkiya ivarkaḷiṉ vāḻkkai neṟikaḷum vēṟupaṭṭaṉa. tamiḻakam kuṟiñci, mullai , marutam, neytal eṉṟu nāṉilamāka iyaṟkaiyiṉaṭippaṭaiyil pirittaṟiyappaṭṭatu. ivaṟṟil vāḻnta makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkai neṟikaḷ vāḻum nilattiṟkēṟpa amaiyap peṟṟatāl tamiḻakattil nila vaḻi, toḻil vaḻi palatiṟappaṭṭa kumukāya amaippukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. kālappōkkil ivarkaḷ taṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉōr kaḷiṉ kālvaḻiyē taṅkaḷaip pirittuk kūṟik koṇṭaṉar. ākavē, kuṭikaḷ(kulam) pala tamiḻakattil tōṉṟiṉa, avvāṟu tōṉṟiya kuṭikaḷuḷ kuṟavarkuṭi vēṭṭuvarkuṭi āyarkuṭi, kāṉavarkuṭi, uḻavarkuṭi, paratavarkuṭi, nuḷaiyarkuṭi eyiṉarkuṭi, maṟavarkuṭi mutaliyaṉa kuṟippiṭat takkaṉa. ivvāṟu, ivarkaḷ vāḻnta nilattiṟkum, ceyta toḻilukku mēṟpappala kuṭikaḷāy aṟiyappaṭṭaṉarēṉum ivarkaḷ yāvarum tamiḻk kuṭikaḷē; tuṭiyaṉ, pāṇaṉ, paṟaiyaṉ, kaṭampaṉ mutaliyōr vīrak kuṭiyiṉar. naṭukallaiyē vaṇaṅkiyavar. 
kaḻakakkālattil maṉṉarum, tāṉait talaivarkaḷum, atikāri cuḷum kōṭṭai kottaḷaṅkaḷuṭaṉ amaikkappaṭṭa nakaraṅkaḷil vāḻntaṉa reṉpatāl nakara vāḻkkaiyum, nāṭṭuppuṟa vāḻkkaiyum ōrē pōtu iruntaṉa veṉpataiyaṟikiṟōm. ākavē, nāṉilattil vāḻnta makkaḷaip paṟṟi yaṟivatōṭu, nakara vāḻkkai muṟaiyiṉaiyum aṟital vēṇṭum. 
nāṉil vāḻkkai 
kuṟiñci : malaiyum malaicārnta iṭamum kuṟiñci eṉappaṭum. kuṟiñcic ceṭikaḷ mikuntu kāṇappaṭṭatāl in nilam kuṟiñci eṉap paṭṭatu. kuṟiñci nila vaḷar kuṟicci eṉṟum, makkaḷ kuṟavar eṉṟum, kāṉavar eṉṟum aḻaikkappeṟṟaṉar. āṭavar kuṟavaṉ, kāṉavaṉ, veṟpaṉ, puṉavaṉ eṉṟum, makaḷir kuṟatti, koṭicci, eṉṟum aḻaikkap peṟṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ marakkiḷaikaḷil ciṟu kuṭilkaḷai yamaittu vāḻntaṉar. malainel (aivaṉam), tiṉai, vaḷḷik kiḻaṅku, vāḻaimutaliya vaṟṟaip payiriṭṭum, vilaṅkukaḷai vēṭṭaiyāṭiyum, paḻaṅkaḷaiyum, kiḻaṅkukaḷaiyum, tēṉaiyum, cuṉai nīraiyum uṇṭum, parukiyum vāḻntaṉar. paraṇ amaittu ataṉ mītamarntu payirkaḷaip pātukāttaṉar. peṇkaḷ paraṇkaḷ mītu amarntu pala ōcaikaḷaiyeḻuppi vilaṅku kaḷaiyum, paṟavai kaḷaiyum ōṭṭuvatum, iravil tīmūṭṭi vilaṅkukaḷai viraṭṭuvatum iṉiya kāṭcikaḷākum. ivarkaḷiṉ kaṭavuḷ cēyōṉ eṉṉum murukaṉākum. kuravaikkūttāṭi paṇṭaṅkaḷaip paṭaittu, kāvu koṭuttuc cēyōṉākiya murukaṉai vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. 
mullai : kāṭum, kāṭucārnta iṭamum mullai eṉappaṭum. iṅku mullaik koṭikaḷ atikamiruntatāl innilam mullai yeṉap paṭṭatu. iṅku vāḻnta makkaḷ kōvalar,iṭaiyar, āyar, āycciyar eṉappaṭṭa ṉar. ivarkaḷ kēḻvaraku, varaku avarai mutaliya puṉceyp payirkaḷaip payiriṭṭaṉar. āṭu, māṭukaḷaic cottākak koṇṭiruntaṉar. eṉavē, puṉceyt tavaca uṇavu kaḷaiyum, pāṟporuḷkaḷaiyum peritum uṇṭaṉar. maṇcuvar eḻuppiya ciṟu kuṭicaikaḷil vāḻntaṉar. āṭu, māṭukaḷai mēykkum ivarkaḷiṉ kōvalar vāḻkkai iṉimaiyāṉatu. kuḻalūti mantaiyai naṭattic cellum kāṭci iṉiyatu. pāṟporuḷ kaḷaiyum, tōṟporuḷkaḷaiyum viṟṟuttaṅkaḷukku vēṇṭiya vaṟṟaip peṟuvar. ivarkaḷ tiṉaiyarici uṇṭālum pālaic cērttē uṇpar. anta aḷavu pāl ivarkaḷukkup perumitamāyk kiṭaittatu. peṇkaḷ pāl, tayir, ney viṟkum kāṭciyum, kiḷikaḷuṭaṉ koñcum kāṭciyum piṟavum iṉiyaṉa. ivarkaḷ kaṭavuḷ māyōṉ āvāṉ. 
marutam kāṭṭukkum kaṭalōrattiṟkumiṭaippaṭṭa ceḻippunilam marutam eṉappaṭum. maruta maraṅkaḷ atikam vaḷarntatāl innilam marutam eṉappaṭṭatu. itu vayalum vayal cārnta iṭamumākum. iṅku vāḻnta makkaḷ uḻavar, kaḷamar, kaṭaiyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. peṇkaḷ uḻattiyar. kaṭaiciyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. in nilattut talaivaṉ makiḻnaṉ, ūṉaraṉ eṉṟum, talaivi maṉaivi eṉṟum aḻaikkap peṟṟaṉar, uḻutoḻil ceytu vāḻnta immakkaḷiṉ vāḻkkai ciṟappuṭaiyatum makiḻvuṭaiyatu mākum. palatarappaṭṭa neṟpayir kaḷaiyum, veṟṟilai, karumpu, vāḻai mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum payiriṭṭaṉar, āṭavar ciṟappum, kāḷai nōkkum peṇkaḷiṉ ciṟappum īṇṭu kaṇṭu kaḷikkattakkaṉavākum. ivarkaḷiṉ kaṭavuḷ vēntaṉ intiraṉ āvāṉ. 
neytal : kaṭal cārnta nilaṅkaḷum, kaṭalōrappakutikaḷum neytal nilappakutiyākum. maṇal niṟainta ip pakuti peru maṇal ulakam' eṉappaṭṭatu. in nilattil vāḻntavar paratavar (paravar eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷ vāḻnta pakuti pākkam, kuppam eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭṭatu. ivarkaḷ kaṭalil mīṉ piṭittaṉar. im makkaḷ nuḷaiyar eṉavum aḻaikkappeṟṟaṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ talaivaṉ cērppaṉ, pulampaṉ koṇkaṉ, tuṟaivaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭṭāṉ. kaṭaṟkaraikaḷaiyoṭṭi uppaṅkaḻikaḷum iruntaṉa. eṉavē, im makkaḷ mīṉaiyum, uppaiyum viṟṟē vāḻntaṉar. kaṭaṟkaraiyōraṅkaḷil paṉai, teṉṉai, puṉṉai, ñāḻal, cuṇṭal, tāḻai kaitai), aṭappaṅ koṭikaḷ mutaliyaṉa ceḻittu vaḷarntaṉa. puṉṉai marattaṭiyil ivarkaḷiṉ ūr maṉṟaṅkaḷ kūṭum. kaṭṭumarattil ivarkaḷ kaṭalmēṟ ceṉṟu mīṉ piṭikkum toḻil neṟiyum, kuppattil vāḻum vāḻkkai neṟiyum cuṟṟuppuṟac cūḻalkaḷukkēṟpa amaintaṉa. ivarkaḷiṉ vaḻipaṭu teyvam vāraṇaṉ (varuṇaṉ ākum, 
pālai: pālaiyeṉṟa nilappakuti tamiḻakattil nilaiyāka amain tirukkavillai. paruvamaḻai poyppatāl mēṟkūṟiya nilaṅkaḷ vaṟaṇṭu pālai pōl kāṭciyaḷippataiyē pālai eṉṟaṉar. āyiṉum, pālai eṉṟa aintāvatu nilattaiyum, ataṉ iyalpukaḷaiyum atil vāḻnta makkaḷaiyum nūlil kaṇṭavāṟu curuṅkak kūṟuvōm. 
pālaiyil vāḻnta makkaḷ vēṭṭuvar, eyiṉar, maṟavar eṉap paṭṭaṉar. cilar vaḻippaṟi ceytē vāḻntatāl 'āṟalaik kaḷvar' eṉappaṭṭaṉar. peṇkaḷ eyiṟṟiyar, maṟattiyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷ vāḻnta kārkaḷ kuṟumpu, paṟantalai eṉappaṭṭaṉa. ivarkaḷiṉ vaḻipāṭu teyvam koṟṟavai (yai)yākum ivarkaḷ vāḻnta pakutiyil iruppai, ōmai, nemai, pālai, yā eṉṉum maraṅkaḷ vaḷarntaṉa. ivarkaḷiṭam 'añcāmai' eṉṉum paṇpē atikam kāṇappaṭṭatāl "maṟakkuṭi' māṇpukku ivarkaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭāyiṉar. inta añcāmai peṇkaḷiṭamum kāṇappaṭṭatu. 
nakara vāḻkkai 
cuḻacukkālattu muṭivuṭai vēntarkaḷum avarkaḷaic cērnta araca kuṭumpattārum vāḻnta nakaraṅkaḷilum, piṟa kuṟunila maṉṉar kaḷum, tāṉait talaivarkaḷum vāḻnta nakaraṅkaḷilum pēratikāri kaḷum ayal nāṭṭavarum, celvarkaḷum, vaṇikarkaḷum vāḻntaṉar. ākavē, muṭiyuṭai vēntar mūvaruṭaiya kōnakarkaḷēyaṉṟip pala nakaraṅkaḷ caṅka kālattil iruntaṉa. āyiṉum, kaḻakakkāla nakara vāḻkkaiyaip potup paṭa viḷakku muṉ pāṇṭiyariṉ kōnakarāṉa kūṭalampatiyaip paṟṟiyum, cōḻariṉ kōnakarum, tuṟaimukappaṭṭiṉa mumāṉa pūmpukāraippaṟṟiyum kuṟippākak kūṟalām.
 kūṭal (maturai) nakara vāḻkkai 
nāḷaṅkāṭi : maturaiyil nāḷaṅkāṭi (pakaṟkaṭaitteru, alluṅ kāṭi (iravuk kaṭaitteru eṉṉum iruvakaik kaṭaitterukkaḷ muṟaiyē pakalilum, iravilum ceyalpaṭṭaṉa. vaikai āṟṟut tuṟai kaḷil uḷḷa cōlaippakutikaḷil perumpāṇ" makkaḷ vāḻntaṉar. nāṭṭai iḻanta maṉṉarkaḷum, kuṭiyiḻanta piṟanāṭṭu makkaḷum, pāṇṭiya nāṭṭai tākkit tōṟṟa maṉṉarkaḷum maturai nakariṉ kōṭṭaikkuḷ amaintirunta uyarnta māṭi vīṭukaḷil vāḻntaṉar. pāṇar nakariṉ akaṉṟa teruṅkaḷil āṅkāṅkē icaiyaiyeḻuppik koṇṭiruntaṉar. icaiyaik kēṭṭuc cilar makiḻcciyil āṭiṉar. viḻāk koṭi, maṉṉa ṉiṉ palvēṟu koṭikaḷ, kaḷḷukkaṭaiyil paṟakkum koṭi mutaliya pala koṭikaḷ akaṉṟa terukkaḷil iru maruṅkilum paṟantaṉa. kaḷḷuṇṭa maṟavar cerukkōṭu ceṉṟaṉar. yāṉaikaḷ, tērkaḷ, kutiraikaḷ vantu pōyk koṇṭiruntaṉa, palvakai malarkaḷ, malar mālaikaḷ, mēṉi maṇappoṭikaḷ, veṟṟilai, aṭaikkāy (pākku, cuṇṇāmpu mutaliya poruḷkaḷai viṟpōr paṭainaṭamāṭṭattiṟku añci māṭi vīṭukaḷiṉ niḻalil niṉṟu viṟṟaṉar. palvēṟu akavaiyiṉarāṉa peṇkaḷum kaṭai vītikku vantu vēṇṭiya poruḷkaḷaik kalaivēlaippāṭuṭaiya ceppukaḷil vāṅkic ceṉṟaṉar, paṇpaṟṟa cila āṇkaḷ ivarkaḷaik kaṇṭu nakaiyāṭi niṉṟaṉar. ittakaiya pōkkuvarattāl eḻunta āravāra olimaṉṉaṉiṉ veṟṟikkup piṉ ēḻu nāḷkaḷ naṭakkum peruñcōṟṟu viḻāvil eḻum āravārattaiyottiruntatu'. 
allaṅkāṭi 
muṉṉiravu : muṉṉiravil celvarkaḷ kutiraikaḷ pūṭṭiya tēril kāvalar piṉtoṭara ulā vantaṉar. makaḷir māṭaṅkaḷiṉ mītuḷḷa nilā muṟṟaṅkaḷil naṭamāṭiṉar. tāymārkaḷ kuḻantai kaḷaiyum, pūcaip poruḷkaḷaiyum ēntikkoṇṭu civaṉ kōyilukkuc ceṉṟaṉar.kuṉṟu kuṭaintaṉṉa antaṇar vīṭukaḷil maṟaiyoli kēṭṭatu. camaṇap paḷḷikaḷil pūvum, pukaiyum maṇantaṉa. pala poruḷkaḷaiyum viṟṟu muttum maṇiyum vilai yākap peṟṟut tirumpuvōrum, vilaiyuyarnta poruḷkaḷai viṟṟu vīṭu tirumpuvōrum, vēntaṉukku utaviya kalaiñar kaḷum piṟarum vītikaḷil kāṇappaṭṭaṉar. mā, palā, mutalāṉa kaṉikaḷum, kiḻaṅku kaḷum, taṉaṉ cōṟum piṟavum viṟkum kaṭaikaḷil avaṟṟai uṇṭa makkaḷ āravāram ceytaṉar. muḻu nilāvoḷiyil taṅkaḷaip palavāṟu oppaṉai ceytukoṇṭu potumakaḷir mēṉi miṉukki aṭittiḷaitta ṉar. poṅkaliṭṭup pūcai ceyta iḷam piḷḷait tācciyarum, cāmi yāṭum cāliṉiyum, veṟiyāṭumvēlarum kuravaik kūttāṭikkoṇṭu ceṉṟaṉar. cērikaḷil coṟpoḻivukaḷum, icaiyum, kūttum nikaḻnta ṉa. 
naḷḷiravu : naḷḷiravil viṟpaṉaik kaṭaikaḷ mūṭappaṭṭaṉa. aṭai, pāyacam (vicayam), koḻukkaṭṭai mōtakam) mutaliyavaṟṟaik kūvi viṟṟaṉar.viḻāvil mūṅkilai niṟuttik kaṭṭiya kayiṟṟiṉ mēl āṭiya vayiriyar tāṅkiṉar. tarkkāval paṭaiyiṉar naṭamāṭiya vaṇṇa miruntaṉar 
piṉṉiravu : vaikaṟaiyil antaṇar vīṭukaḷil maṟaiyoli kēṭṭatu yāḻ vallōr marutappaṇ pāṭiṉar; kaḷiṟukaḷukku uṇavu uruṇṭaikaḷ iṭappaṭṭaṉa; kaṭaikaḷ meḻukappaṭṭaṉa; aḷviṟpōr vilaikūṟi viṟṟaṉar; kaḷḷuṇṭōriṉ karakarappāṉa oli kēṭṭatu; maṉṉaṉ tuyileḻac cūtarkaḷ pāṭiṉar; viṇṇiyalar (mākatar) nērattai yaṟivittaṉar; vētakāḷi eṉṉum icaikkaruvi muḻaṅkiṟṟu: makaḷir katavukaḷait tiṟakkum olikēṭṭatu. 
tuyileḻunta maṉṉaṉ kaṭamaikaḷai muṭittukkoṇṭu kolu maṇṭapattil amarntu maṟavarukkum, pulavarukkum, kalaiñarukkum paricukaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ. poṟkalattil makaḷir tanta tēṟalaip parukiṉāṉ. 
yāvarum vāḻttum vāḻttolikaḷ kēṭṭaṉ. ivvāṟu aracaṉ aṟam purital, araciyal naṭattutal, iṉpamāyk kālaṅkaḻittaleṉa oru nāḷai mūṉṟākap pakirntu paṇiyāṟṟuvāṉ. 
mēṟkūṟiya muṟaiyilēyē pukār nakara vāḻkkaiyum amain tiruntataip paṭṭiṉappālaiyāl aṟikiṟōm. pukārcelvam koḻitta nakaram. atil vāḻnta celvarum vaṇikarum, poṉṉum maṇiyum viravap peṟṟiruntaṉar. paratavar pāṇar, yavaṉar cērikaḷilum makkaḷ makiḻntu vāḻntaṉar. palvakaittoḻilāḷar kaḷum, uḻavarkaḷum, kalaivallār kaḷum in nakaril vāḻntaṉar. caṅku aṟuttal, yāṉai maruppukaḷaik kaṭaital, poṉvēlai, maṉari vēlai, cempu vēlai, mārpukkaccai ceytal(vampu muṭital), ōviyam tīṭṭutal, mālai kaṭṭutal, cuṇṇam iṭittal, tiraiyal maṭittal pīṭā) mutaliya ciṟutoḻilkaḷum," veṇkala vēlai, necavuvēlai, civikai, vaṇṭi tēr meyppuk kavacam, yāṉaiyai aṭakkum tōṭṭikaḷ, kaiyil māṭṭik koḷḷum tōluṟaikaḷ, maruntukaḷ, cūḻiyam , veṇcāmaram, palavakaiyāṉa kēṭayaṅkaḷ ceyyum toḻilkaḷum maturaiyil naṭantaṉa. 
maturaiyilum, pukārilum palvēṟu tavacaṅkaḷai viṟkum vāṇikat terukkaḷum iruntaṉa. tavacam viṟpōr kūlavāṇikar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. kappal vāṇikattil īṭupaṭṭōr mācāttu vaṇikar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. uppu vaṇikarum, eṇṇey vaṇikarumkaḷ, tēṟal, tōppi, maṭṭu makiḻ, naṟavu, naṉai, ariyal mutaliya palatarappaṭṭa matuvakaikaḷai viṟpōrum, appam, piṭṭu paṇṇiyam, ūṉuṇavu mutaliya uṇavukaḷai viṟpō nam pū pūmālai, viraimāmēṉi maṇappoṭi), poṉ, muttukaḷ, pavaḻam, vairam mutaliyaṉa viṟpōrum, pūvēlaippāṭuṭaiya āṭaikaḷ viṟpōrum, palvakai āṭaikaḷ viṟpōrum, ōviyaṅkaḷ, pom maikaḷ, valaippiṉṉal, viḷaiyāṭṭupporuḷkaḷ viṟpōrum, iṉṉapiṟa eṇṇilā vaṇikarkaḷum maturai, pukār kaṭai vītikaḷil kāṇappaṭṭaṉar. 
kaṭal vaḻivanta ayalnāṭṭup poruḷkaḷum in nakaraṅkaḷil viṟkappaṭṭaṉa. kutiraikaḷ, miḷaku, maṇikkaṟkaḷ, akil, cantaṉam mutaliyaṉavum viṟkappaṭṭaṉa. ayalnāṭṭu vaṇikarum tamiḻaka vaṇikarum palvēṟu moḻikaḷil pēcikkoṇṭaṉar. maruvūrp pākkattilum ittaku vaṇikar iruntaṉar. 
araṇmaṉai vāḻvu | 
mēṟkūṟiya makiḻcci āravāraṅkaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya nakarattiṉ naṭuvil aracaṉ vāḻum kōyil araṇmaṉai irukkum. maṉṉaṉ vaikaṟaiyil tuyileḻuntu veḷutta āṭaiyaṇintu aṟam ceytu, ceṅkōlōc cuvatākak kūṟiṉōm. ivaṉ iravup poḻutai makaḷiroṭu iṉpamāyk kaḻittāṉ. poṉ maṇikaḷālāṉa aṇikaḷaiyum, palatarappaṭṭa āṭai kaḷaiyum aṇintu oppaṉai ceyyappaṭṭa araṇmaṉaiyil vāḻnta maṉṉaṉiṉ vāḻkkaiyum, uṟaiviṭamum cepputaṟkariya ciṟappuṭaiyaṉa. kaḻakakkāla maṉṉar peṇṭir palarai maṇakkum vaḻakkamuṭaiyavar. araciyārum aracaṉuṭaṉ koluvīṟṟiruntār. aracaṉ iṟantāl araci yum, paṭait talaivarum piṟarum uṭaṉ kaṭṭaiyēṟum vaḻakkam iruntatu. yāṉaiyēṟṟam, kutiraiyēṟṟam, vēṭṭaiyāṭutal, piṟa peṇ kaḷuṭaṉ peṇṭāṭṭikaḷ kūṭikkaḷittal mutaliya paḻakkavaḻakkaṅ kaḷum, maṉṉariṭamuṇṭu. 
makkaḷ 
ituvarai nām kaṇṭa kaḻakakkālattu nāṉila vāḻkkaimuṟai kaḷaiyum, nakara vāḻkkaimuṟaiyaiyum tuytta makkaḷ yāvarum tamiḻarkaḷē. ivarkaḷ iṭattāl, toḻilāl vēṟupaṭṭaṉarēyoḻiya, uḷḷattāl tamiḻarkaḷē. uḻavar, toḻilāḷar, kalaiñar, pulavar, vaṇikar mutaliya palatiṟappaṭṭavarkaḷaik kaṇṭōm. iṉi im makkaḷiṉ uṇavu, uṭai aṇikalaṉkaḷ, poḻutu pōkkukaḷ, camayak kōṭpāṭu, vāḻviyal vēṟupāṭukaḷ mutaliyaṉa paṟṟic curuṅkak kūṟuvōm. 
uṇavu 
ik kāla makkaḷ orēvakaiyāṉa uṇavai uṇṭaṉar eṉak kūṟa muṭiyātu. avaravar vāḻnta iṭaṅkaḷukkēṟpa uṇavu, uṭai, uṟai viṭaṅkaḷ amaintatai mēlē kaṇṭōm. āyiṉum perumpālāṉa makkaḷiṉ uṇavu aricic cōṟēyākum. varaku, cāmai mutaliya puṉceyta tavaca uṇavaiyum, mīṉ iṟaiccikaḷaiyum, kaṉi, kiḻaṅkukaḷaiyum uṇṭa makkaḷaiyum kaṇṭōm. miḷaku, puḷi, uppu mutaliyaṉavum uṇavil cērttuk koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. aracaṉ aḷikkum viruntukaḷilum, piṟa viruntukaḷilum uṇṭa uṇavukkum, taṉi māntaṉ uṇṭa uṇavukkum, ainnila makkaḷum, araṇmaṉaiyil vāḻntōrum uṇṭa uṇavukkum vēṟupāṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. kaimpeṇṭir vitavaikaḷ, tuṟavikaḷ, vīrarkaḷ mutaliyōriṉ uṇavu vakaikaḷ vēṟu vēṟāka iruntaṉa. aṟaccālaikaḷ pala iruntaṉa. palvēṟu ciṟṟuṇṭikaḷum ciṟṟuṇṭi viṟpōrum aṟiyappaṭu kiṉṟaṉar. palatarappaṭṭa paḻaccāṟukaḷaiyum, palatarappaṭṭa kaḷḷaiyum, cuvai nīraiyum, kuṭivakaikaḷaiyum ik kālattil kāṇkiṟōm, ūṉ uṇṇum vaḻakkam ellōriṭamum iruntatu. veḷḷāṭu, cemmaṟiyāṭu, māṉ, muyal, āmāṉ, mīṉ, naṇṭu, īyal, kōḻi, kāṭṭukkōḻi, kāṭai uṭumpu mutaliyavaṟṟai uṇṭaṉar. pālum, pāl poruḷum, paruppu vakaikaḷum uṇṇappaṭṭaṉa. nāṉ uṇavaip pōlavē kaḷḷuṇṇum paḻakkamum, tēṟalaip parukum paḻakkamum palariṭamum kāṇappaṭṭatu. piṟkālattil kaḷḷuṇṇu taliṉ kēṭukaḷaiyuṇarntōr kaḷḷuṇṇāmaiyai valiyuṟut tiṉar. 
uṭai 
kaḻakakkālac cāṉṟukaḷiliruntu akkāla makkaḷ taḻaikaḷālum, pūkkaḷālum, koṭikaḷālum āṉa āṭaikaḷaiyum aṇintaṉar eṉpatu pulaṉākiṟatu. nārālum, marappaṭṭaiyālumāṉa maravuri āṭaiyum iruntataip pattuppāṭṭiliruntu aṟikiṟōm. pulittōl, māṉtōl mutaliyavaṟṟaip paṭukkaikkum, irukkaikkum payaṉpaṭuttiṉārkaḷ. , paruttiyālāṉa meṉtukil, kaliṅkam, vaṇṇa āṭaikaḷ mutaliyaṉa aṇiyappaṭṭaṉa. veṇmaiyāṉa āṭaikaḷ tuṇṭutuṇṭāka iruppiṉ aṟuvaiyeṉṟum, mūṅkiliṉ mēl ōṭu pōlavum pālāṭai pōlavum iruppiṉ meṉtukil eṉṟum aṟiyappaṭṭaṉa. kaliṅka nāṭṭiliruntu koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa parutti yālāṉa āṭaiyē kaliṅkam eṉṟum, paṭṭāṭaikku vaḻaṅkiya peyarē kaliṅkam eṉṟum palavāṟu kūṟappaṭu kiṟatu. nālākkaliṅkam, oṇpūṅkaliṅkam eṉṟu kaliṅkattaip palavakai yākak kūṟiṉar. nurai pōṉṟa veṇmaiyāṉa tuṇi. mūṅkili liruntu urikkappaṭṭa veḷḷiyatōl pōṉṟa kaṇṇukkut teriyāta iḻaika ḷāl neyyappaṭṭa tuṇi ākiyavaiyē muṟaiyē nūlākkaliṅkam, oṇpūṅkaliṅkam eṉṟu aṟiyappaṭṭaṉa. maṇappeṇ aṇinta āṭai. kōṭikkaliṅkam eṉappaṭṭatu. curuṅkāta kaliṅkattai maṇamakaḷ aṇintāṉ eṉavum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. itiliruntu cucaṅkāta allatu curuṅkāta veḷḷiya āṭai kaliṅkam eṉappaṭṭirukkalāmeṉat teri kiṟatu. nīlam, civappu mutaliyavaṇṇaṅkaḷālāṉa āṭaikaḷum iruntaṉa. 
paṭṭālum, elimayirālum āṭaikaḷ neyyappaṭṭaṉa. āṭai kaḷil piṉṉal vēlaippāṭukaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. piṉṉal vēlai ceytavar cāliyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. āṇ, peṇ irupālarum palvakai yāṉa āṭaikaḷaiyum aṇintaṉar. nīṇṭa koycakam vaittu āṭai aṇiyum vaḻakkam kaḻakakkāla makaḷiriṭaiyē kāṇappaṭṭatu. ivarkaḷ "vampu'. 'vār' eṉṉum mārpuk kaccaikaḷai aṇintaṉar. maṟavarkaḷ veṭṭuṭaiyaṇintaṉar. āṇkaḷ ariyum caṭṭai 'paṭam' meyppai mutaliya peyarkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭṭaṉa. āṇum, peṇṇum oṉṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa āṭaikaḷai aṇintaṉar. talaippākai aṇivatum, mēl --- tuṇṭu pōṭuvatum uṇṭu. 
aṇikaḷ 
cuḻacukkālat tamiḻarkaḷ, aṇinta aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṇi, pūṇ, iḻai, kalam, toṭi, kuḻai, vaḷai mutaliya coṟkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. kāl mutal talai varai palvēṟu aṇimaṇikaḷai ivarkaḷ aṇintārkaḷ. āṇkaḷ maṟavar) kālil vīrakkaḻal aṇivar. peṇkaḷ taṇṭu, cilampu mutaliyavaṟṟaik kālil aṇintaṉar. kiṇkiṇi eṉṉum ciṟu cataṅkaiyum ciṟuvarkaḷukkukkālil aṇivikkappaṭṭatu. kaḻuttil āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum, muttu, poṉ mālaikaḷaiyum, kaṟkaḷ patitta mālaikaḷaiyum aṇintaṉar. āṇkaḷ tōḷil toṭi, kaḻaltoṭi, koṭi ākiyavaṟṟaiyum, kaiyil kaṭakattaiyum aṇintiruntaṉar. peṇkaḷ muṉkaiyil toṭiyum, ataṉaiyaṭuttu vaḷaiyum aṇintaṉar. kuṟuntoṭi, avirtoṭi, āytoṭi pūntoṭi, ceṟitoṭi eṉap palavakaiyāṉa toṭikaḷ iruntaṉa. celvamakaḷir tōḷukkuk kīḻ aṇinta oruvakai aṇi vanta" eṉappaṭṭatu. itaṉai vaṅki eṉalām. poṉṉālum, muttālumaṉṟic caṅkālum vaḷaiyalum, piṟavum ceytaṇiyappaṭṭaṉa. valampuric caṅkālāṉa vaḷai "valampurivaḷai' eṉappaṭṭatu. aṟuttuc ceyyappaṭṭa vaḷai elvaḷaiyeṉṟum iluṅkuvaḷaiyeṉṟum aḻaikkappaṭṭatu. makaḷir pūṅkuḻai polaṅ kuḻai, ciṟukuḻai, oṇkuḻai, vārkkuḻai, kaṉaṅkuḻai, pollāṅkuḻai, makarakkuḻai, varikkuḻai mutaliya palatarappaṭṭa kātaṇikaḷai aṇin tiruntaṉar. ivaṟṟiliruntu poṉṉāl ceyta pūppōṉṟa kuḻaiyum, cumpi pōṉṟa kuḻaiyum, vārppuk kuḻaiyum, paccaikkal patitta kuḻaiyum allatu taḻai, koṭi pōṉṟa kuḻaiyum piṟavum ivarkaḷāl aṇiyap paṭṭaṉaveṉpatai aṟikiṟōm, 
peṇkaḷ kūntal oppaṉaikkuppiṉ talaiyil poṉṉālāṉa talaiyaṇi, curitakam mutaliya aṇikaḷaiyum aṇintaṉar. aracarkaḷiṉ muṭicuḷ nīlamaṇikaḷ patittu aṇiceyyappeṟṟiruntaṉa. avarkaḷ mārpilum, kaḻuttilum, iṭuppilum, tōḷilum kaiyilum palvēṟu aṇikaḷaiyaṇintaṉar. ivvāṟu nōkkumpōtu caṅkakālattil āṇ, peṇ, ciṟuvar, ciṟumiyar yāvarumē ēḻai, celvantar eṉṟa vēṟupā ṭiṉṟi aṇimaṇikaḷai aṇintaṉar eṉa aṟikiṟōm. muttu, maṇi, poṉ, koṭi, vārai mūṅkil, caṅku mutaliyavaṟṟālāṉa aṇikaḷ iruntaṉaveṉpataiyum aṟikiṟōm. kuṟum poṟi, cilkāḻ, parumam, palkāḻ, vaikkāḻ mutaliyaṉa iṭaiyaṇikaḷākum. mārpil aṇinta aṇi "matāṇi' eṉappaṭṭatu. yāṉaikku mukapaṭām kuḻi) eṉṉum poṉṉaṇi pōṭuvatuṇṭu. ciṟuvarkaḷukkup pulippal kōtta tālikaḷaiyum, aimpaṭait tālikaḷaiyum aṇivippar. kūntalaic caṭaiyākap piṉṉi atil tiruciyaṇiyappaṭum aṇi curitakam eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭṭatu. talaimayirai vārivakiṭu eṭuttu oṉpatu maṇikaḷaip patitta oruvakai aṇiyai aṇivārkaḷ. ataṟkut 'toyyakam' eṉṟu peyar. itaṉait talaippāḷai eṉṟum aḻaippar, makarappaṭuvāy eṉpatu cuṟāmīṉ vaṭivilāṉa oruvakai aṇiyākum. cuṟā vaṭivilāṉa maṇipatitta poṉvaḷaiyam 'makaravaḷaiyam' eṉappaṭṭatu. nīlamaṇi patitta poṉṉaṇi 'kuṇimpai' eṉappaṭṭatu. vāku vaḷaiyam, nittilamatāṇi mutaliya aṇikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum toṉmaṇikaḷāl (navamaṇikaḷ) āṉa nakaikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. 
naṟumaṇap poruḷkaḷ 
naṟumaṇap poruḷkaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttum vaḻakkam ak kāl makkaḷiṭam kāṇappaṭṭatu. malarkaḷ, kāykaḷ, marakkaṭṭaikaḷ, vilaṅkup poruḷkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟai iṭittu naṟumaṇap poṭikaḷum naṟumaṇap poruḷkaḷum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. pūkkaḷaic cūṭikkoḷvatum, paṭukkai yil tūvuvatum naṟumaṇam karutiyē yākum. palvēṟu malarkaḷaiyum, koṭikaḷaiyum, kāykaḷaiyum, cērttuk kūṭṭappeṟṟa katampa mālai kaḷaippaṟṟiyum, avaṟṟi liruntu eḻunta naṟumaṇam paṟṟiyum aṟikiṟōm. veṟiyāṭum, vēlaṉ aṭṭikam cātikkāy, takkōlak kāy, kāṭṭumalli, veṇṭa kāḷi malar ākiyavaṟṟaik kalantu katampa mālai yaṇintāṉ. kañcaṅkullaip pūvai makaḷirum, kuḻantaikaḷum talaiyil aṇiva tuṇṭu. ivvāṟu nāṉila makkaḷum palvēṟu mālaikaḷaic cūṭiyatai yaṟikiṟōm, ceṅkaḻunīr mālai, kāntaḷkaṇṇi, kaḷaṅkāykkaṇṇi, veṇṭāḷimālai, pittimālai mutaliya mālaikaḷaip paṟṟiyum aṟikiṟōm. 
poṉtakaṭu, toṇmaṇikaḷ, cantaṉam, karuppūram mutaliyavaṟṟaip paṉinīril naṉaittu iṭittuc cuṇṇam uruvākki viraimāvākamēṉip poṭiyāka-Powderp pūcikkoṇṭaṉar. maṇapporuḷkaḷai ammiyil araittuc cāntākavum pūcikkoṇṭaṉar. akiṟpukaipōṭṭut tuṇikaḷaiyum, kūntalaiyum maṇamaṭaiyac ceytaṉar. itaṉāl tuṇikaḷ pūcciyarikkāmalum kākkappaṭṭaṉa. 
viḻākkaḷum viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷum 
avvannilattiṟkēṟṟapaṭi viḻākkaḷum, viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷum iruntaṉa. potuvāka viḷakkukaḷai ēṟṟik koṇṭāṭum viḷakkīṭṭu viḻā' ellōriṭattilum kāṇappaṭṭatu. aṟumīṉ cērum muḻunilā nāḷil mālai vēḷaiyil iv viḻā naṭaipeṟum, terukkaḷil viḷakkukaḷai ēṟṟi, malar mālaikaḷait toṅkaviṭṭup pāl poṅka viṭṭu viḻāk koṇṭāṭuvar. malaikaḷiṉ uccikaḷilum viḷakkukaḷai yēṟṟuvar, tirumāl ulakattil tōṉṟiya nāḷai 'ōṇam' eṉṉum viḻāvākak koṇṭāṭuvar. aṉṟu vīrarkaḷ yāṉaikaḷaip pōriṭac ceytu kaṇṭukaḷippar. tainnīrāṭum viḻā, pērāṟukaḷil puṉal nīrāṭum viḻā, maṇiyai iṭuppilē kaṭṭikkoṇṭu teruvilē āṭum uḷḷi viḻā, mutaṉmutalil vīrar ampu toṭukkumpōtu koṇṭāṭum vīrar viḻā', kūttarkaḷ koṇṭāṭum kōṭiyar viḻā, mēḻicittirai) ōraiyil muḻunilāk kālattil koṇṭāṭum vēntar intira viḻā, iḷavēṉiṟ kālattil koṇṭāṭappaṭum kāmavēḷ viḻā mutaliya viḻākkaḷaik kaḻakakkāla makkaḷiṭaiyē kāṇkiṟōm. 
viḻākkaḷaippōlavē palvēṟu viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷilum ivarkaḷ īṭupaṭṭaṉar. palvēṟu vakaiyākat taṉittum, palar kūṭiyum nīril viḷaiyāṭiṉārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ maṇalvīṭu kaṭṭutal, kaḻaṟcikkāy, ammāṉaikkāy mutaliyaṉa āṭutal, paṟavaikaḷaivaittāṭutal, ūñcalāṭṭam, puṉalāṭṭam mutaliya āṭṭaṅkaḷil īṭupaṭutalum uṇṭu. porunaiyāṟṟaṅkaraiyil peṇkaḷ kaikōttup piṉṉiyāṭiya viḷaiyāṭṭu teṟṟik kiḻaṅku viḷaiyāṭṭu' allatu kuravai viṉai yāṭṭu' eṉappaṭukiṟatu. pāvaikaḷ, pūkkaḷ, kiḻaṅkukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaik koṇṭu, ciṟṟil kaṭṭiyum, cōṟākkik kuṭumpam naṭattiyum ciṟumikaḷ viḷaiyāṭiṉar. kaṉṟukaḷuṭaṉ cērntum, nīril mūḻkiyum, marattivēṟiyum ciṟuvarkaḷ viḷaiyāṭiṉar. ic ciṟuvar, ciṟumiyar viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷum, periyōrkaḷ viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷum nāṉilattiṟkēṟṟavāṟu palavakaiyāka iruntaṉ. vīrarkaḷ viḷaiyāṭṭuk kaḷaṅkaḷil pala viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷaiyum, uṭaṟpayiṟci kaḷaiyum ceytaṉar. yāṉaiyōṭu uṭal valimaiyaic cōtikkum viḷaiyāṭṭu, kāḷaikaḷait taḻuvum viḷaiyāṭṭu mutaliya añcattakka viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷaiyum ivarkaḷ viḷaiyāṭiṉārkaḷ. 
kartikaḷ
