kaḻakakkālattil nīr, nilam, vāṉvaḻikaḷaik kaṭakka ūrtikaḷ iruntaṉa. yāṉai, kutirai, erumai, kōvēṟu kaḻutai mutaliya vaṟṟiṉ mītum, kutirai, māṭṭuvaṇṭikaḷiṉ mītum ūrntu ceṉṟaṉar. vēntarum, vīrarum, āṇkaḷum tēril ceṉṟaṉar. tērkaḷiṉ vakaikaḷ virivāka aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vaṇṭikaḷ tavacaṅkaḷai ēṟṟikkoṇṭum, uppu muṭṭaikaḷai ēṟṟikkoṇṭum varicaiyākac ceṉṟa kāṭcikaḷ cittarikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nīraik kaṭappataṟkuk kaṭṭumaraṅkaḷum, marakkalaṅka kaḷum iruntaṉa, periya kappalkaḷ 'kalam' eṉṟum, ciṟu ōṭaṅkaḷ 'tōṇi" eṉṟum aḻaikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tōṇikaḷ kalampuka muṭiyāta kaḻikaḷilum cellum. vīṭu pōṉṟa periya paṭakukaḷil mīṉ viṟṟa nel mūṭṭaikaḷai aṭukki vantatāka aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. timili eṉṟa tōṇi nīraippiḷantu viraintu cellakkūṭiyatu. ayalakam ceṉṟa kalam vaṅkam' eṉappaṭṭatu: nāvāy eṉṟum itaṉaiyaḻaippar. vāṉvaḻiyākac cellum vāṉavūrtikaḷ kaḻakakkālattil iruntaṉavā eṉpatu paṟṟit tiṭṭamākak kūṟamuṭiyavillai. āyiṉum vāṉavūrti' eṉṉum toṭar puṟanāṉūṟṟiliruntu aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. "valavaṉ ēvā vāṉavūrti' eṉṟu aḵtu cuṭṭappaṭuvatāl ōṭṭupavaṉ illāmalēyē atu ceṉṟateṉa aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu, 
nampikkaikaḷ 
kaḻakakkāla makkaḷiṭam palatarappaṭṭa nampikkaikaḷ iruntaṉa. cakuṉam pārttal, naṟcol kēṭṭal mutaliyavaṟṟil avarkaḷ peritum nampikkai yuṭaiyavarkaḷ. katiravaṉ, nilavu, viṇmīṉkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟil iṭap peyarccikaḷiṉāl viḷaiyum kēṭukaḷaiyum naṉmaikaḷaiyum paṟṟip pala nampikkaikaḷaik koṇṭiruntaṉar. kirakaṇam ēṟpaṭuvatait tiṅkaḷaip pāmpu viḻuṅkukiṟateṉṟu nampiṉar, viṇmīṉ kuṟitta kālattil viḻumāṉāl aracar pōlum talaivarkaḷ iṟappar eṉṟu nampiṉar. pōrkkaḷattilum, iṭukāṭṭilum pēykaḷiruppatāyum, avai piṇaṅkaḷait tiṉṟu vāḻumeṉṟum nampiṉar. neyyaiyum, veṇciṟu kaṭukaiyum kalantu tūvik kaṇṇēṟu kaḻippārkaḷ. pēykaḷ neruṅkā vaṇṇamum ivaṟṟait talaiyil appikkoḷvārkaḷ. pāmpukaḷ mārikkak kaṟkaḷai umiḻum eṉṟu ivarkaḷ nampiṉārkaḷ. 
paṟavaikaḷ, ōṇāṉ, niṟaikuṭam mutaliyavai etirppaṭutalaik koṇṭum, nērattaik koṇṭum nimittam pārttu naṭakkum nampikkai cuḻacukkālat tamiḻarkaḷiṭam uṇṭu, yārō iruvar pēcum collait tām eṇṇiya eṇṇam niṟaivēṟumā eṉpataṟkup pārkkum ceyal viricci kēṭṭal eṉappaṭum. "varuntaṟka', 'varuvāṉ' eṉṟu pēcik koṇṭāl niṉaittavaṉ varuvāṉ eṉṟum, māṟākap pēcik koṇṭāl vārāṉeṉṟum nampiṉar. vaḷaiciṟuttal, uṟuppukaḷ tuṭittal, kaṉavukaḷ, palli col, kākkai karaital pūtaliyavaṟṟilum ivarkaḷukku nampikkaiyuṇṭu. maram, cuṉai, ōṭai āṟu, kaṭal mutaliya vaṟṟilum teyvikam uraivatāy nampiṉar. 
aṟattāl naṟpiṟavi kiṭṭum eṉṟum, aṉṟēl kuṟaipaṭṭa piṟaviyē kiṭṭum eṉṟum, immai, maṟumaiyil avarkaḷ nampikkai vaittaṉar. kuṟatti kuṟi collum vaḻakkamum, muṟṟattil nellaip parappik kuṟi kēṭṭal, oṟṟai iraṭṭaiyeṉayeṇṇi muṭiveṭuttal, teyvam vantu āṭutal vēlaṉ veṟiyāṭal, cāliṉi pēyāṭal) mutaliyavaṟṟilum nampikkai vaittiruntaṉar. 
muṭivurai 
kaḻakakkālak kumukāyattai āyntu, nōkkumpōtu maṉṉarum, celvarum, maṉṉar kuṭumpattiṉarum, pēratikārikaḷum, tāṉaittalai varkaḷum, ivarkaḷaic cērnta toḻilāḷarkaḷum, kalai vallunarkaḷum nakaraṅkaḷil iṉpa vāḻkkaiyai nukarntaṉar eṉṟum, ivarkaḷil uṇavu, uṭaiaṇikaḷ mutaliyaṉavum poḻutupōkkukaḷum ētō oru kaṉavu ulakil kāṇum kāṭcikaḷāka uḷḷaṉaveṉṟum aṟikiṟōm. āṉāl, nāḷil makkaḷaiyum, pālainila makkaḷaiyum paṟṟi aṟiyumpōtu nākarika maṟiyāta toṭakkakkāla makkaḷaip pōlavē vēṭṭaiyāṭiyum, āṭu, māṭu mēyttum. kiḻaṅku kāykaḷaiyuṇṭum, maravuriyum, taḻai, tōlāṭaikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟai uṭuttiyum vāḻntataik kāṇkiṟōm. kaṭaiyēḻu vaḷḷalkaḷaiyum, maṉṉarkaḷiṉ koṭaittaṉmai kaḷaiyum ivarkaḷai nāṭic cellum pulavarkaḷaiyum, pāṇar kaḷaiyum kāṇum pōtu kañcikkaḻum makkaḷ orupuṟamum, celvattai malaiyeṉakkuvit tuḷḷa celvarkaḷ oru puṟamum irukkak kāṇkiṟōm, kaṟpiṉ mēṉmai paṟṟiyum pēcappaṭum atē nērattil parattaiyar paṟṟip palavāṟu aṟikiṟōm. uvakaip peṇkaḷōṭum pal aḻakiya maṉaiviyaroṭum aracarkaḷ matuvuṇṭukaḷikkum kāṭciyum, maṟavar kāṭṭuk kiḻaṅkaik kiḷaṟiyuṇṭu, nāṭṭukkākap pōriṭum kāṭciyum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. pārppaṉar, kumukāyattil uḻaikkāmal ēṟṟam peṟṟuḷḷaṉar. vaṇi karkaḷ, peruñcelvattaik kuvittuḷḷaṉar. vaṟiyarkaḷ, vaḷḷalkaḷai nāṭip paḻuttamaram nāṭic cellum paṟavaikaḷ pōla alaikiṉṟaṉar, murukaṉ, tirumāl, vēntaṉ intiraṉ), vāraṇaṉ (varuṇaṉ), koṟṟavai mutaliya teyvaṅkaḷum, camaṇa, cākkiya camayaṅkaḷum nilaviniṉṟaṉa. catukkap pūtaṅkaḷ, pēykaḷ mutaliyaṉa paṟṟiyum tuṟakkam, maṟupiṟappu paṟṟiyum ik kāla makkaḷ aṟintiruntaṉar. 
potuvāka oḻukkamum, vīramum, paṇpum ivarkaḷ yāvariṭattum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. kaṟṟavarum, pulavarum, tuṟavikaḷum, oḻukkamuṭaiyārum, kaṟpuṭaip peṇṭirum yāvarālum pōṟṟap paṭṭaṉar. kalviyum, poru, ḷum makkaḷiṭaiyē viravi, parantu vaḷaravēṇṭumeṉṟa aṭippaṭaik koḷkai annāḷil illai. 'ciṟu mīṉkaḷaip perumīṉkaḷ viḻuṅku vataippōl vallavarkaḷē piṟarai yaṭakkiyāṇṭaṉar. aṭikkaṭi māṟum āṭciyālum, iyaṟkaik kōṭukaḷālum avatiyuṟṟa makkaḷ taṅkaḷ vāḻkkaiyaip paṟṟi mikutiyāka ōrāmal cintikkāmal taṉṉoḻukkat taiyē vaḷarttu vāḻvāṅku vāḻntaṉar. 
(ē) kaḻakak kālac camayam
kaḻakakkālat tamiḻar iyaṟkaiyait teyvamāka vaḻipaṭṭavarkaḷ, iv vaḻipāṭē paḻamaiyāṉatu. itaṉ vaḷarccippāṅkil civaṉ, māl, koṟṟavai, murukaṉ mutalāṉa teyva vaḻipāṭṭu muṟaikaḷ tamiḻar vāḻvil iṭampeṟṟaṉa. maṟṟum maram, naṭukal, pēyt teyvaṅkaḷ peṇ teyvaṅkaḷ, ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉavaṟṟaiyum kaḻakakkāla makkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. vantēṟikaḷum, vantēṟikaḷiṉ māyavalaippaṭṭavarkaḷum āriyat teyvaṅkaḷaiyum, camaṇa, pauttat teyvaṅkaḷaiyum vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar. iṉṟupōl kaḻakak kālattilum tamiḻakam palvēṟu camayaṅ kaṉiṉ pukaliṭamāka viḷaṅkiyatu. 
iyaṟkai vaḻipāṭu 
tiṅkaḷ, ñāyiṟu, maḻai ākiyavaṟṟai vāḻtti iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ tam cilappatikāra nūlait toṭaṅkukiṟār. itu tamiḻar neṟiyil amainta vaḻipāṭṭumuṟai, ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, tī ākiya muccuṭar vaḻipāṭṭumuṟai koṭi nilai, vaḷḷi, kantaḻi eṉṉum peyarkaḷāl tolkāppiyattil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu tol. 1034). piṟa kaḻakap pāṭalkaḷilum ic cuṭar vaḻipāṭu paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. viṇmīṉ vaṇakkam (naṟ. 231), kāṟṟut teyva vaṇakkam(kali. 15, kaṭal teyva vaṇakkam akam. 240) mutalāṉa vaḻipāṭukaḷum kaḻakak kālattil nilavi vantaṉa. ālamar celvaṉ (puṟam. 198), vōmpaṭik kaṭavuḷ (akam. 309), paṉaiyaṭik kaṭavuḷ(naṟ. 3030), kaḷḷi marattaṭik kaṭavuḷpuṟam. 260), uṉṉamarak kaṭavuḷ tol. 1006) mutalāṉa marattaṭik kaṭavuḷarum kaḻakak kālattil vaḻipaṭu cuṭavuḷarāy viḷaṅkiṉar. kaḻakakkāla makkaḷ vīṭṭut teyva vaḻipāṭu ceytaṉar. akam. 282, naṟ. 2, 35, kali. 133, patiṟ. 84). teyvaṅkaḷ palvēṟu uruvaṅkaḷaikkoṇṭavaiyāka viḷaṅkiṉakuṟi. A). potiyilcāvaṭi, maṉṟam, tiṇ, vīṭṭut tiṇṇai , marattaṭi mutalāṉa pala iṭaṅkaḷil kaṭavuḷiṉ tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ vaittu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. cēyōṉ murukaṉ allatu civaṉ, māyōṉ tirumāl vēntaṉ intiraṉ), vāraṇaṉ(kaṭal teyvam ākiya teyvaṅ kaḷait tolkāppiyam kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. itteyvaṅ kaḷuṭaṉ palarāmaṉ, kāmaṉ cāmaṉ(kali. 94), ēṇōṉa(kali. 26 piramaṉ(kali.2,129, naṟ. 337, muruku 165), mānitik kiḻavaṉ(akam. 5ki), kūṟṟuvaṉ(patiṟ. 71, *pācam' puṟam. 195), intiraṉmuruku 159) mutalāṉa teyvaṅkaḷum kaḻakakkālat tamiḻarkaḷāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. 
peṇ teyvaṅkaḷil koṟṟavaitol. 10051, tirumakaḷ (puṟam, 658), māyavaḷ (kali.35) kalaimakaḷkali.36), cūli(kuṟun. 21) kaṭalteyvam (akam. 370), nīrara makaḷirpuṟam. 42), varaiyar makaḷir(aiṅ. 191.204), cūrara makaḷir akam. 162, muruku 12-37 mutalāṉa teyvaṅkaḷum vaḻipaṭap paṭṭaṉa. 
' maṟṟum kollip pāvai akam. 62, naṟ. 201), tātu ceypāvai (akam. 392, vaṇṭaṟpāvai(akam. 258), uppiyaṟpāvaikali. 138) maṉṟaṅkaḷil amainta maṇ cilai akam 157mutalāṉa pāvai uruvaṅ kaḷum vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. 
accantarattakka pēy, kamutu, aṇaṅku, cūr, mutalāṉapeyarkaḷāl kuṟippiṭṭuk kaḻakakkāla makkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭuvantaṉar. 
-- makkaḷaik kākkap pōriṭṭu māynta vīrarkaḷukkuk kalnāṭṭut teyvamākki vaḻipaṭṭa naṭukal vaḻipāṭu kaḻakakkāla varalāṟṟil ciṟappiṭam peṟṟa oṉṟu. itu kōyil kaṭṭi vaḻipaṭum nilaikku vaḷarntataic cilappatikāram kāṭṭukiṟatu. 
vaḻipāṭṭu muṟai 
paccilaipuṟam. 105, muruku, 37, malar mutalāṉavaṟṟait tūviyum, naṟai virai pukaittum kuṟi. 5-7), poṅkaliṭṭup paṭaittum, arukampul, pūḷāppū, nel, tiṉai mutalāṉavaṟṟait tūviyum kaḻakakkāla makkaḷ kaṭavuḷai vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. nīrāṭiya piṉṉarē avarkaḷ vaḻipāṭu ceytaṉar. (patiṟ. 88). cammaṇam pōṭṭu uṭkārntu koṇṭum muruku]4), niṉṟu koṇṭum avarkaḷ vaḻipāṭu ceytamaikkāṉa kuṟippukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. kaṭavuḷiṉ muṉṉar yāḻ mīṭṭiyum vaḻipāṭu ceytaṉarnaṟ 189). makaḷir kūṭṭam kūṭṭamākak kōyilkaḷukkuc ceṉṟu vaḻipāṭu ceytaṉar. naṭukallukkup pīlicūṭṭiyum, tuṭi muḻakkiyum, tōppi, kaḷ mutalāṉa vaṟṟaip paṭaiyal ceytum vaḻipāṭu ceytaṉar. akam.385), veḷḷāṭṭuk kuṭṭi mutalāṉa vilaṅkukaḷaik kāvu koṭuttu vaḻipaṭṭatum uṇṭu (akam. 156. cantaṉa mālaiyai vaḻipāṭṭiṉ pōtu oru mālaiyākap cepamālaiyākap payaṉpaṭuttiyatākavum terikiṟatu acum. 13). maḻai peyvataṟkākavum, peyyum maḻaiyai niṟuttuvataṟkākavum avarkaḷ vaḻipāṭu ceytatuṇṭu puṟam.143). ivai tamiḻar vaḻipāṭṭu muṟaikaḷ. 
āriyap pārppaṉar camaṟkiruta vētam ōti, kālaiyilum, mālaiyilum vaḻipāṭu ceytaṉar. avarkaḷ tī vaḷarttu vēḷvi ceytu vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. uruttiraṉ, cuppiramaṇiyaṉ, kiruṭṭiṇaṉ mutalāṉa teyvaṅkaḷ toṭarpāṉa kaṭṭukkataikaḷait tamiḻar teyvaṅkaḷukku iṭṭukkaṭṭi iṇaittuviṭṭaṉar. itaṉāl, caṅkakālattiṟkup piṉṉar civaṇiyamcaivam, māliyam/vaiṇavam) eṉṉum camayappirivukaḷ putitāka muḷaittaṉa. 
camaṇa, pautta neṟikaḷ 
kaḻakakkālac camaṇar kukaikaḷ, tamiḻnāṭṭil paravalākak kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. maturaikkāñci, cilappatikāram, maṇimēkalai mutalāṉa nūlkaḷilum, camaṇa, pautta neṟic ceytikaḷ iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa, ivaṟṟaik koṇṭu ic camayaneṟikaḷ kaḻakakkālattil toṭakka nilaiyilum, caṅkam maruviya kālattil ōraḷavu celvākkup peṟṟa nilaiyilum viḷaṅkiṉaveṉṟu kūṟalām. 
ōka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ
36 nūlkaḷaik kaḻaka nūlkaḷ eṉṟu piṟkālattavar kuṟippiṭṭaṉar. avaṟṟuḷ 18 nūlkaḷai mēṟkaṇakku nūlkaḷ eṉṟum, 18 nūlkaḷaik kīḻk kaṇakku nūlkaḷ eṉṟum pakuttu vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷ, mēṟkaṇakku nūlkaḷuḷ pattuppāṭṭu 10 nūl), eṭṭuttokai (8 nūl) ākiyavaṟṟai aṭakkiṉārkaḷ. 
cilappatikāram, maṇimēkalai ākiya kāppiyaṅkaḷaik kaḻaka nūlkaḷ eṉṟum, kaḻakakkāla nūlkaḷ allaveṉṟum, iru vēṟu 
karuttukaḷaik kūṟivarukiṉṟaṉar. in nūlkaḷiṉ kālam toṭarpāka nām oru teḷivāṉa muṭiviṟku varamuṭiyavillai eṉṟālum avai pallavar kālattiṟku muntiyavai yeṉṟu teḷivākak kūṟalām. itaṉāl, ivaṟṟaik kaḻakam maruviya kāla nūlkaḷ eṉalām. atāvatu ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum iṭaippaṭṭa kālattavaiyeṉalām.
pattuppāṭṭu 
nīṇṭa pāṭalkaḷaik koṇṭa pattu nāṅkaḷ it tokaik kuṟippāl cuṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭai, poruna rāṟṟuppaṭai, ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭai, perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭai, kūttarāṟṟuppaṭai eṉṉum malaipaṭukaṭām ākiya aintu nūlkaḷ iravalaraip puravalarpāl āṟṟuppaṭuttum āṟṟuppaṭai nūlkaḷāka amaintuḷḷaṉa, mullaippāṭṭu kuṟiñcippāṭṭu, paṭṭiṉappālai ākiya mūṉṟum akattiṇai nūlkaḷ, maturaikkāñci talaiyālaṅ kāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉaic ciṟappittup pāṭiya puṟattiṇai nūl, neṭunalvāṭaiyum avaṉatu ciṟappiṉaik kūṟukiṟatu. itu puṟattiṇai nūl eṉpatu tollōr muṭivu. ik kāla ārāycci yāḷarkaḷil cilar akattiṇai nūl eṉavum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. inta nūlkaḷaip pāṭiyōraiyum pāṭalkaḷil ciṟappikkappaṭṭōraiyum kāṭṭum aṭṭavaṇaiyai aṭiyil kāṇalām.

eṭṭuttokai 
eṭṭuttokai nūlkaḷ intat tokaik kuṟippāl cuṭṭap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ puṟanāṉūṟu, patiṟṟuppattu, paripāṭal ākiya mūṉṟu nūlkaḷum puṟattiṇaip pāṭalkaḷaik koṇṭavai. ivaṟṟil palvēṟu maṉṉarkaḷaip paṟṟiyum kuṟunilat talaivar kaḷaip paṟṟiyum, maṟakkuṭi makkaḷaippaṟṟiyum, palvēṟu pulavarkaḷ pāṭiya pāṭalkaḷ iṭampeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivai caṅkakāla makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkaip pāṅkiṉai yum, paṇpāṭṭuc ciṟappiṉaiyum veḷip paṭuttum karuvūlaṅkaḷākat tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. 
aiṅkuṟunāṟum, kalittokaiyum akattiṇai nūlkaḷ. ivaṟṟil ovvoru tiṇaiyaic cērnta pāṭalkaḷai ovvoru pulavar pāṭi uḷḷār. itaṉaik kīḻē uḷḷa aṭṭavaṇaiyil kāṇalām.

kuṟuntokai, neṭuntokai eṉṉum akanāṉūṟu, naṟṟiṇai ākiya mūṉṟu nūlkaḷil palvēṟu pulavarkaḷ pāṭiya akattiṇaip pāṭalkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ kuṟuntokai 4-8 aṭikaḷai uṭaiya pāṭalkaḷaik koṇṭatu. naṟṟiṇaip pāṭalkaḷ -12 aṭikaḷaik koṇṭavai. akanāṉūṟṟup pāṭalkaḷ 1337 aṭiyaḷavuṭaiyaṉa. mēlē kūṟappaṭṭa akattiṇaip pāṭalkaḷ yāvum tamiḻar vāḻvil amain tirunta āṇ, peṇ uṟavup pāṅkiṉ mēṉmaiyai uṇarttu kiṉṟaṉa, pāṭalkaḷ puṉainturaiyiṉṟi iyalpāka iyaṟkai yōṭum, vāḻkkai yōṭum iyaintu kāṇappaṭuvatu ciṟappukkuriya tākum. paripāṭalil ovvoru pāṭalukkum icai kuṟippiṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉāl, itaṉai icaittamiḻ ilakkiyam eṉṟu kūṟalām. 
tirukkuṟaḷ mutalāṉa patiṉeṇkīḻkkaṇakku nūlkaḷil kumukāya nalamkaruti makkaḷ evvāṟu vāḻavēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟaneṟi pukaṭṭum pāṭalkaḷ amaintuḷḷaṉa. 
cilappatikāramum, maṇimēkalaiyum iraṭṭaik kāppiyaṅkaḷ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cilappatikāram, iyal, icai, nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻkkūṟukaḷaiyum taṉṉakattē koṇṭuḷḷa muttamiḻk kāppiyam cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya nāṭṭaiyum avaṟṟiṉ maṉṉarkaḷaiyum 
kōvalaṉ, kaṇṇaki varalāṟṟiṉ vāyilāka itu ciṟappittukkāṭṭukiṟatu. maṇimēkalai putta matattiṉ mēṉmaiyai viḷakkum nāḷ. 
tolkāppiyam 
kiṭaittuḷḷa tamiḻ nūlkaḷil paḻamaiyāṉatu tolkāppiyam. itu ilakkaṇa nūl; eṉṟālum itiluḷḷa poruḷatikārattil tamiḻariṉ vāḻkkaip pāṅkukaḷ etirolikkiṉṟaṉa. 
(ō kaḻakak kālak kalaikaḷ
kaḻakakkālat tamiḻariṉ vāḻkkai makiḻcciyākak kaḻintatu eṉpataip pārttōm, attakaiya makiḻcciyiṉūṭē avarkaḷiṉ palatarappaṭṭa kalaikaḷaip paṭaittiruppataip pārkkiṟōm. avvāṟu paṭaikkappeṟṟa kalaikaḷaik kaṭṭaṭakkalai, ōviyakkalai, ciṟpakkalai mutaliyaṉavāka aṟiyalām. 
kaṭṭaṭakkalai 
kaḻakakkālattil vīṭukaḷ, māḷikaikaḷ, kōṭṭai kottaḷaṅkaḷ kaṭṭappeṟṟaṉa. kaṭṭaṭakkalaiyeṉpatu mutalil iyaṟkaiyāka amainta cantu, kuṭai, kukai mutaliyavaṟṟiliruntē tōṉṟiyirukka vēṇṭum. ākavē, pāṟaikaḷilum, kuṉṟukaḷilum iyaṟkaiyāka amainta kukaikaḷ paṇṭaik kālat tamiḻakattil kōyilkaḷākavum, muṉivarkaḷ vatiyum iṭaṅkaḷākavum payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. pāṇṭiya vēntar ittakaiya kukaikaḷaic cākkiya muṉivarkaḷukku otukkiyatāka ilakkiyam, kalveṭṭuc cāṉṟukaḷiṉ liruntu aṟikiṟōm. maturai mānakaril vāḻnta antaṇariṉ vīṭukaḷ kuṉṟukuṭaintāṟ pōlirun taṉaveṉṟu maturaik kāñciyil kūṟap peṟuvatāl, kuṉṟukaḷaik kuṭaintu kōyilkaḷākap paṭaikkum paḻakkam iruntatai aṟiyalām. karuṅkaṟkaḷai oṉṟaṉ mēl oṉṟāka aṭukki amaikkappeṟṟa karuṅkaṟ kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ, kuṭaivaraikaḷukkup piṉ ēṟpaṭṭirukkalām ittakaiya karuṅkaṟkaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷaik kaṟṟaḷikaḷ' eṉkiṟōm. ivaṟṟiṟkellām mūlamāṉa kuṭicaip paṭivattiliruntu tōṉṟiya marattālāṉa kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ paṇṭait tamiḻakattil iruntirukka vēṇṭum. iṉṟum tillaiyil cavai maṇṭapamum, kēraḷattiluḷḷa kōyilkaḷ palavum marakkaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷāyiruppatai aṟiyalām. ceṅkaṟ kaḷaiyum, cuṇṇak kāraikaḷaiyum payaṉpaṭuttik kaṭṭappeṟṟa kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷum, kaḻakakkālattil iruntaṉa. iṉṟaiya akaḻvāyvil kaḻakakkālattilirunta ceṅkaṟ kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. itaṟkoru ciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭup putuccērikku arukiluḷḷa arikka mēṭu akaḻvāyvil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatākum. ittakaiya ceṅkaṟ kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ pala iruntaṉaveṉpataṟku ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷ ērāḷamā yuḷḷaṉa. kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅ kaṇṇaṉār eṉṉum pulavar ceppaṉiṭap peṟāta ceṅkaṟcuṭṭaṭam iṭintu pōṉatai uvamaiyākak kāṭṭi aḻakupaṭak kūṟiyuḷḷār'. kaḻakakkālac cōḻaṉ ceṅkaṇāṉ tirumāluk kum civaṉukkum eḻupatukkum mēṟpaṭṭa kōyilkaḷaik kaṭṭiṉāṉ eṉṟu piṟkālattut tirumaṅkaiyāḻvārum, tirunāvukkaracarum aḻaku paṭak kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. 
cilar kaḻakakkālattil karuṅkaṟkaṭṭaṭamē illaiyeṉṟu kūṟu kiṉṟaṉar. ki.mu. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil vaṭaintiyāvai āṇṭa acōkap pēraracar kālattil kavvai merukiṭum kalai mikacciṟantu kāṇappaṭṭa teṉpatai iṉṟum nām kaṇṭu viyakkiṟōm. mēlum, pāṟaiyaik kuṭaintu kukaiyaic camaikkum kalaiyaippaṟṟik kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kūṟappeṟukiṉṟatu. āyiṉ iyaṟkaiyākak kiṭaikkum karuṅkaṟkaḷaikkoṇṭu kaṭṭaṭam amaippataik kaḻakak kālat tamiḻar uṟutiyāka aṟintirukka vēṇṭum. mēlum ceṅkaṟ kaḷaic camaippa teṉpatu ōr ariya muyaṟciyālāṉa kalaiyākum. attakaiya muyaṟcikkum muṟpaṭṭa cuvaiyaṟivē curuṅkaṟkaṭṭaṭa mākum. itaṉ tāy marakkaṭṭaṭa meṉṟum, marakkaṭṭaṭattiṉ cēy karuṅkaṟkaṭṭaṭameṉṟum, ataṟkup piṉṉarē piṟa poruḷkaḷaik koṇṭu kaṭṭum kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉ veṉṟum kalaiñar ipi. ēval(E. B. Havell) kūṟuvatu īṇṭu. āyntu nōkkattakkatām. valivāṉa kōṭṭai kottaḷaṅkaḷil karuṅkallōṭu piṟa poruḷ kaḷum payaṉpaṭuttap peṟṟirukkalām. 
kaḻakakkālattil maṉṉarkaḷ vāḻnta kōnakarkaḷ tāmarai malar vaṭivil amaikkappeṟṟiruntaṉa. cōḻariṉ kōnakarāṉa kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattiṉ amaippup paṟṟic cilappatikāram, maṇimēkalai mutaliya ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kāṇalām. itu nīḷ catura vaṭivil amain tiruntatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ittakaiya kōnakarkaḷ miḷaikkāṭu kāvaṟkāṭukaḷ, akaḻikaḷ, puṟamatilkaḷ, akamatilkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟāl pātukākkap paṭṭaṉaveṉṟum, ivaṟṟiṟkuḷ aracaṉum, amaiccarum, piṟa atikāri kaḷum vativataṟkāṉa palatarappaṭṭa kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉaveṉṟum aṟikiṟōm. akaḻiyai nirapputaṟkuc curuṅkait tūmpiṉ vaḻiyāka nīr ōṭivantateṉṟum, aḵtu yāṉaiyiṉ tutikkai pōṉṟu nīṇṭirunta teṉṟum kūṟappeṟukiṉṟatu. kōṭṭai valivākavum, pātukāppākavum iruntateṉṟum, matil uyarvākavum akalamākavum, tiṇmaiyākavum, arumaiyākavum iruntateṉṟum kūṟappeṟukiṉṟatu. kōṭṭaikaḷ maṇṇālum, ceṅkallālum, karuṅkallālum kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. karuṅ kaṟkaḷai iṇaippataṟkuc cempu, īyam ākiya māḻaikaḷ urukki vārkkap peṟṟaṉa, matilkaḷ viṇṇuṟa vōṅki vaḷarntu niṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ mēl pantum, pāvaiyum, cilampum, taḻaiyum aḻakuṟap poṟittu aṇi ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ mēṟpuṟattilum, uṭpuṟat tuḷaikaḷilum palatarappaṭṭa poṟikaḷ poruttappaṭṭiruntaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, viṟpoṟi, kuraṅkuppoṟi, kavaṇ poṟi, yāṉaippoṟi, tūṇṭiṟpoṟi, toṭakkuppoṟi, aṭuppup poṟi, kavaippoṟi, kaḻuppoṟi, putai katavu, aiyavittulām, ciraṟpoṟi, paṉṟippoṟi, muracuppoṟi mutaliyaṉa kuṟippiṭattakkaṉa. 
kōṭṭaiyiṉ vāyilkaḷ pōrvīrarkaḷ yāṉaiyiṉ mītamarntu koṭikaḷaiyēnti uṭcellum aḷavukku akaṉṟum, uyarntum iruntaṉa. ivai malaikaḷaik kuṭaintāṟpōṉṟu amaintiruntaṉa veṉṟu maturaik kāñciyil kūṟappeṟukiṉṟatu. katavukaḷ kaitērnta kammiyarāl ceyyap peṟṟavai: avai keṭāmalirukka ney pūcap paṭṭatu. iruppup paṭṭaikaḷ avaṟṟiṉ mītu cuṟṟappaṭṭiruntaṉa. vil, puli, kayal ākiya ciṉṉaṅkaḷ muṟaiyē cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiyar katavukaḷil poṟikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ivaṟṟait tavirap pala ciṟu katavukaḷum kōṭṭaikaḷil iruntaṉa, 
āṟu kiṭantaṉṉa akal neṭun terukkaḷum kaḻakakkālattil iruntatāka neṭunalvāṭai mutaliya kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. mokañcātārō, arappā mutaliyaviṭaṅkaḷil kāṇap paṭum kaṭṭaṭaṅ kaḷum piṟa nākarikac ciṉṉaṅkaḷum ilemūriyāk kaṇṭam kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiyapōtu aṅkiruntu tappivaṭakkil kuṭiyēṟiya paṇṭaiya tiraviṭa makkaḷiṉ kaṉavuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷēyeṉṟu iṉṟaiya uruciya nāṭṭu maṇṇiyal āyvāḷarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ākavē, kaḻakakkālattu vīṭukaḷiṉ amaippaiyum, terukkaḷiṉ amaippaiyum mokañ cātārō, arappā nakaraṅkaḷil kaṇkūṭākak kāṇalām. 
maturai nakariṉ amaippai maturaikkāñci, neṭunalvāṭai, cilappatikāram mutaliya kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ciṟappuṟac ceppukiṉṟaṉa. in nakarilirunta nāḷaṅkāṭi, allaṅkāṭi ākiyavaṟṟaip paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ ēṟkaṉavē kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. taṉittaṉiyāka amainta cērikaḷ paṟṟiyum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaippōlavē pūmpukār amaippup paṟṟiyum ataṉ arukirunta maruvūrppākkam paṟṟiyum kūṟappaṭṭatu. iṉṟaiya akaḻ vāyvil kaṭṭaṭakkalaiyamaippu veḷiyulakukku aṟivikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. maruvūrppākkam, paṭṭiṉappākkam ākiya iru pakutikaḷukkiṭaiyil peruñcālai yoṉṟu iruntateṉṟum, ac cālaiyiltāṉ nāḷaṅ cāṭi eṉṉum pakaṟcantai kūṭumeṉṟum aṟikiṟōm. maruvūrppākkat til toḻi lāḷarkaḷum, piṟa eḷiya makkaḷum vāḻntaṉar. paṭṭiṉap pākkattil maṉṉaṉum piṟa uyarkuṭi makkaḷum vāḻntaṉar. ākavē, vaṇikar vatinta māṭavītikaḷ, kōviyar vīti koṭittēr vīti, pīṭikait teru, antaṇar irukkai mutaliya perunterukkaḷ palavum māṭa māḷikaikaḷum paṭṭiṉappākkattil iruntaṉa. mēlum, amarar taruk kōṭṭam veṉyāṉaikkōṭṭam, nākartaṇkōṭṭam, uccikkiḻāṉ kōṭṭam, ūrkkōṭṭam, vēṟ kōṭṭam, vaccirakkōṭṭam mutaliya kōṭṭaṅkaḷum, civaṉ, murukaṉ, tirumāl, palatēvaṉ, intiraṉ mutalāṉōrukkāṉa kōyilkaḷum iṅkiruntaṉa. "vāṉōṅku cimaiyattu vāḷoli caittiyam' pūmpukāril iruntateṉṟu kūṟap paṭuvatāl cākkiyar kōyilkaḷum, ilaṅkoḷic cilātaḷamiṭappaṭṭa vāṉperu maṉṟam' aintu terukkaḷ kūṭumiṭattil iruntateṉṟu kūṟappaṭuvatāl camaṇar kōyilkaḷum iruntaṉaveṉpatu teḷivā kiṟatu. veḷḷiṭai. maṉṟam, ilañcimaṉṟam, neṭuṅkalmaṉṟam, pūtac catukkam, pāvaimaṉṟam eṉṟa aivakai maṉṟak kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ iruntatākak kūṟappaṭuvatāl kaḻakak kālattil palatarappaṭṭa kaṭṭaṭaṅ kaḷum iruntaṉaveṉa aṟikiṟōm. pukāril mikac ciṟanta tuṟai mukamum, kalaṅkarai viḷakkamum, īmappuṟaṅ kāṭukaḷum amaikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa. kaṭaṟkaraiyaiyaṭuttirunta pakuti "neytalaṅkāṉal' eṉap paṭṭatu. iṅku matikkuṇṭam cōmakuṇṭam), katirkkuṇṭam cūriyakuṇṭam eṉṉum īrērikaḷum kuḷaṅkaḷum) kāmavēḷukku oru kōyilum iruntaṉa. iṉṟaiya akaḻvāyvāḷarāl kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷa putta vikāraiyiṉ amaippaiyum aḷavaiyum koṇṭum kaḻakak kālak kaṭṭaṭak kalaiyaik kaṇkūṭākak kaṇṭukaḷikkalām. paṭṭiṉappālaiyil uṟaikiṇaṟṟup puṟañcēri' eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappeṟum uṟaikiṇaṟukaḷ pala kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattiṉ kaṭaṟkaraiyōraṅkaḷil kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṅkuk kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷa kuḷam cuṭṭa ceṅkallāl cuṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu. ataṟku uḷpuṟam nīr varavum, veḷippuṟam nīr cellavum ēntākap poṟiyiyal nuṭpattuṭaṉ amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷa nīrppātai kaṇṭukaḷikkattakkatām. 
ivvāṟu kaḻakakkālattil taṉik kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷum, periya kōṭ ṭaikaḷum, matilkaḷum, kuḷaṅkaḷum, ērikaḷum, iyantira vāvikaḷum, kaḻinīr vaṭikālkaḷum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. pantal, ciṟṟil, māḷikaikaḷ, māṭaṅkaḷ, kōyilkaḷ, kōṭṭaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa iṭattiṟkum payaṉukku mēṟpa amaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. kuvimāṭam, matakalaimāṭam, arāmiyam, nilāmuṟṟam, muṉṟil, muṟṟam, paḷḷiyaṟai, uvaḷakam antappuram) mutaliyaṉa aḻakiya amaippukaḷoṭum, katavukaḷ, cāḷaraṅkaḷ, nilaikaḷ mutaliya amaippukaḷuṭaṉ nalla kāṟṟōṭṭamum veḷiccamum peṟutaṟ kēṟpavum amaikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa. ittakaiya ēntukaḷ kōyilkaḷil kāṇappaṭavillai. avai maṉattai īrttup pakkuvappaṭuttum vakaiyil camaikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa. kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil "mayaṉ matam' eṉṉum kaṭṭaṭak kalaikkōṭpāṭu iruntatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. neṭunalvāṭai yil kuṟippiṭap peṟum im mayaṉ vīti' iṉṟu kēraḷattil kiṭait tuḷḷa 'mayaṉ matam' eṉṉum vaṭamoḻik kaṭṭaṭakkalai nūlil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. innāl kiranta eḻuttil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉ mūvalūral kaḻakakkālattil iruntu, aḻintupaṭṭirukkalām. 
kaḻakakkālattil kaṭṭaṭakkalaiyil kaiyāḷappeṟṟa palvēṟu aḷavu muṟaikaḷaip paṟṟiyum kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntu aṟikiṟōm, eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, mātavi āṭiya mēṭai taraiyiliruntu oru kōl uyarattil amaintiruntatu. aḵtu ēḻukōl akaliṭattaiyum eṇkōl nīḷattaiyum koṇṭiruntatu. araṅkattiṉ mēlmainta uttarappalakai nālukōl uyaramiruntatu eṉak kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivvaraṅkattiṉ irumaruṅkilum paṭik kaṭṭukaḷ amaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa eṉavum kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷatu. iṅku oru kōl eṉpatu ēṟattāḻa 2 aṭi viral ( aṅkulamākum. ivvāṟu vaittuk kaṇakkiṭṭāl mātavi āṭiya araṅkattiṉ aḷavai yaṟintu koḷḷalām. 
ōviyakkalai 
kaḻakakkālattil ōviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭuvataṟkeṉṟē aracamāḷikaiyil taṉimāṭam otukkappaṭṭiruntatu eṉpataik kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntu aṟikiṟōm. pāṇṭiyaṉ naṉmāṟaṉ taṉ ōviya māṭattil taṅkiyirukkum pōtu iṟantupaṭṭatāl 'pāṇṭiyaṉ cittira māṭattut tuñciya naṉmāṟaṉ' eṉṟu puṟanāṉūṟṟil cuṭṭap paṭukiṟāṉ. maturaikkāñciyil māṅkuṭi marutaṉār pāṇṭiyariṉ ōviya māṭattaik 'kayaṅkaṇṭaṉ vaiyaṅkuṭai nakarattuc cempiyaṉ ṟaṉṉa ceñcuvar puṉaintu' eṉṟu vivarittuḷḷār. itiliruntu kiḻakkālattil perumpālāṉa ōviyaṅkaḷ cepputtakaṭukaḷaip pōl paḷapaḷappākameḻukappaṭṭa cuvarppakutiyil tīṭṭappaṭṭaṉaveṉpatai aṟikiṟōm. mēlum, ic cuvar veṇcāntu pūcappeṟṟu vaḻavaḻap pākkap peṟṟa teṉṟum, atil taccar, kammiyar mutali yōriṉ kaivaṇṇattiṉālāṉa kaṭṭaṭattiṉ uruvaṅkaḷum, ceṭi, koṭi, pūmutaliya iyaṟkaik kāṭcikaḷum tīṭṭappeṟṟaṉaveṉ ṟum aṟikiṟōm. nakkīrar pāṭiya neṭunalvāṭaiyil pāṇṭiyaṉuṭaiya cittiramāṭattil veṇcāntum, nīlamaiyum, cempuniṟamum, paccainiṟa mum koṇṭa vaṇṇaṅkaḷ, ōviyaṅkaḷ varaiyap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa veṉṉum ceytiyaṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. ittakaiya cittiramāṭam maturaikku arukiluḷḷa tirupparaṅkuṉṟam malaiyiluḷḷa murukaṉ kōyilai yaṭuttum iruntateṉpataikkuṉṟampūtaṉār eṉṉum pulavar paripāṭalil kūṟukiṟār. im māṭattil tiṭṭappeṟṟa ōviyaṅkaḷai ikkōyilukku varum makkaḷ ceṉṟu kaṇṭukaḷittaṉar. itil kāmaṉ, irati, akalikai, akavitaiyiṭam cellum intiraṉ, akalikaiyiṉ kaṇavaṉ kavutam muṉivaṉ, ammuṉivaṉaik kaṇṭatum pūṉaiyiṉ uruvaṅkoṇṭu ōṭum intiraṉ mutaliyōr ōviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. itēpōṉṟa cittirakkūṭam tiruccikku arukiluḷḷa tiruveṟumpūril iruntataik kalveṭṭuc cāṉṟu mūlam aṟikiṟōm. cittirakkūṭam eṉum peyaril ōviyakkūṭaṅkaḷ tillaip peruṅkōyil, tiruvaraṅkattup peruṅ kōyil ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil cittirakkūṭam iruntataṟkāṉa kuṟippukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. 
mēṟkaṇṭa eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷiliruntu kaḻakakkālattil ōviyak kalaiyai vaḷarppataṟkeṉṟē aracarkaḷ taṅkaḷ araṇmaṉaiyai yaṭuttuc cittirakkūṭamoṉṟaittaṉiyē cuṭṭi vaittiruntaṉar eṉpatai yum, ittakaiya cittirakkūṭaṅkaḷaik kōyil pōṉṟa puṉitaviṭaṅkaḷi lum kaṭṭi ik kalaiyaip pōṟṟikkāttaṉar eṉpataiyum aṟikiṟōm. kaṇṇil kāṇumāṟu poruḷkaḷai ōviyaṅkaḷākat tīṭṭum iv vōviyak kalaiñarkaḷ kaḻakak kālattil "kaṇṇuḷ viṉaiñar', eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭṭaṉar. tolkāppiyattil vīrakkal allatu naṭukal eṉpatu paṟṟiya ceyti kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṉṟatu. pōril viḻuppuṇpaṭṭu iṟakkum vīraṉukku ittakaiya naṭukal naṭuvatu vaḻakkam. avaṉaip putaittaviṭattil allatu avaṉ viḻuppuṇpaṭṭu viḻuntaviṭattil avaṉ niṉaivāka naṭappaṭum akkallil av vīraṉiṉ uruvattait tīṭṭuvatu vaḻakkam, 
kaḻakakkāla naṭaṉa makaḷir naṭaṉattōṭu vēṟupala kalaikaḷaiyum kaṟṟaṉar. avaṟṟuḷ ōviyakkalaiyum oṉṟāku meṉṟu cilappatikāram kūṟukiṟatu. mātavi naṭaṉamāṭiya araṅkattiṉ mēṟkūraiyil pala ōviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa veṉṟum, im mēṟkūrai 'ōviya vitāṉam' eṉappaṭṭateṉṟum, māḻaiyālāṉa kēṭayaṅkaḷilum kaṇcuvar ōviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉaveṉṟum cilappatikāram ceppukiṟatu. itaip pōlavē talaiyālaṅkāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉiṉ kōpperuntēvi paṭuttirunta paḷḷiyaṟaiyiṉ mēṟ kūraiyilum palavakai ōviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappeṟṟiruntaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ tiṅkaḷiṉ pakkattil urōkiṇi iruppatupōṉṟa ōviyamum oṉṟākum. taṉ kaṇavaṉākiya tiṅkaḷōṭu urōkiṇi iṇaintiruppatupōl tāṉum taṉ kaṇavaṉōṭu iṇaintirukka muṭiyavillaiyeṉṟu eṇṇik kōpperuntēvi perumūccu viṭṭāḷeṉṟu neṭunalvāṭai kūṟukiṟatu. 
celvar vāḻum vāḻṉaic cuvarkaḷiṉ mītum, māṭaṅkaḷiṉ mītum tēvar mutal ellāvuyirkaḷaiyum kuṟikkattakka oviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭap peṟṟiruntaṉaveṉṟum, avvōviyaṅkaḷ veṇcutai pūciya paḷapaḷappāṉa parappil tīṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉaveṉṟum maṇimēkalai -- kūṟukiṟatu. ittakaiya eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷiliruntu kaḻakakkālattil taṉiyār vīṭukaḷilum ōviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭum vaḻakkam iruntateṉṟum araṇmaṉaiyil palvēṟiṭaṅkaḷilum, paḷḷiyaṟaiyilum ōviyaṅkaḷ iṭampeṟṟiruntaṉaveṉṟum aṟikiṟōm. ' 
cuvarkaḷil maṭṭumaṉṟit tuṇiccīlaikaḷilum ōviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭum vaḻakkam kaḻakakkālattil iruntatu. ivvāṟu ōviyam tīṭṭappaṭṭat tuṇic cīlaiyaip paṭā am' allatu 'paṭam' eṉṟē aḻaittaṉar.tāṉ virumpiya peṇṇai maṇakka muṭiyāta kātalaṉ oru tuṇiyil avaḷuṭaiya uruvattai ōviyamāka varaintu koṇṭu maṭalēṟuvāṉ eṉṟu akapporuḷ nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. ōviyaṅkaḷaiyamaittu āṭai neytal kaḻakak kālattil kāṇap paṭṭa oru ciṟanta kalaiyākum. itaṉai maṇimēkalaiyil varum, 
''vittakaṉ iyaṟṟiya viḷaṅkiya kaviḷaic 
cittirac ceyaṟkaip paṭām pōrttatuvē 
ōppat tōṉṟiya uvavaṉam" 
- eṉṉum varikaḷāl aṟikiṟōm, 
oviyaṅkaḷai mutalil mēlōṭṭamāka uruvattiṉ amaippai maṭṭum kīṟṟāka (putlinc) varaivar. itaṉaic caṅkakālattil “puṉaiyā ōviyam' eṉṟu aḻaittaṉar. itaṟkup piṉṉarē vēṇṭiya vaṇṇaṅ kaḷaip payaṉpaṭutti ivaṟṟai uyirōṭṭamuḷḷa ōviyaṅkaḷākku vārkaḷ. ivvōviyak kalaiyē kaṭṭaṭakkalai, ciṟpakkalai, naṭaṉak kālai mutaliya ellāk kalaikaṭkum aṭippaṭaiyāka amaintatu. oru kaṭṭaṭattaik kaṭṭuvataṟku muṉpum, ciṟpattaic cetukkuvataṟku muṉpum avaṟṟai ōviyamāka varaintu koṇṭu, piṉṉarē kaṭṭaṭam kaṭṭum paṇiyum ciṟpam ceyyum paṇiyum toṭaṅkappeṟṟaṉa. ittakaiya ōviyaṅ kaḷaip paṟṟiya ōviya nūloṉṟu kaḻakakkālat tiliruntatu eṉpatai aṭiyārkku nallār kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār. 
ōviyattai vaṭṭikaic ceyti yeṉṟu maṇimēkalai kuṟippiṭu kiṉṟatu. vaṭṭikaiyeṉṉum peyar, ōviyam varaiyappaṭum tararikaiyāl (Brush) vantatu, ōviyaṅkaḷait tīṭṭuvataṟku muṉ it tūrikaiyāl vaṇṇaṅkaḷait toṭṭuk kuḻaikkum palakaikku vaṭṭikaip palakai yeṉpatu peyar. tūrikai, pātirip pūvaip pōliruntateṉpataṉai naṟṟiṇaiyiṉ kūṟṟāl aṟikiṟōm. ak kālattup peṇkaḷai ōviyattaip pōluḷḷaṉareṉṟum, ōrmai (cintaṉai) yilāḻntu acaiyātiruppavarkaḷai ōviyaṅkaḷaippōl uḷḷaṉareṉṟum uvamai kāṭṭip pēcuvatu vaḻakkam. itaṉaic cīvakacintāmaṇi, maṇimēkalai mutaliya nūlkaḷil kāṇalām. 
oḷiyak kalaiñaṉ nakai, uvakai, avalam, vīram mutaliya eṇvakai meyppāṭukaḷaiyum iruttal, kiṭattal, niṟṟal mutaliya oṉpāṉ vakaiyāṉa viruttikaḷaiyum taṉatu ōviyaṅkaḷil cittarittuk kāṭṭiṉāṉ eṉpataip peruṅkataiyil virikkak kāṇalām. cīvakacintāmaṇiyiṉ āciriyarāṉa tiruttakkattēvar maram, ceṭi, koṭi pōṉṟa nilait tiṇaikaḷaiyum, māṉ, maṟi pōṉṟa vilaṅkukaḷaiyum aḻakiya uṟuppu . kaḷaiyuṭaiya peṇkaḷaiyum, teyvattiṉ uruvattaiyum, mayiṟpoṟi pōṉṟa viyattaku vimāṉattaiyum tīṭṭiya kalaiñarkaḷaippaṟṟi vivarittuk kūṟukiṟār. ākavē, kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭum ōviyak kalai piṟkālattilum toṭarntu pōṟṟik kākkappaṭṭatu eṉpataic cīvakacintāmaṇi mutaliya nūlkaḷiṉāl aṟiyalām. 
ciṟpakkalai 
pōril puṇpaṭṭu māṇṭavīrarkaḷukku naṭukal naṭuvatiliruntum, kaṟpuṭaiya makaḷirukkup pattiṉikkal (makācati naṭuvatiliruntum ciṟpaṅkaḷaic camaikkum vaḻakkam ēṟpaṭṭateṉpataik kaḻakakkāla ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntu aṟikiṟōm. itaṉait tolkāppiyam - poruḷatikāram - puṟattiṇaiyiyal 5 ām pāṭṭiliruntu aṟiyalām. avvāṟu naṭukal naṭṭu avviṭattil 'vīracālai' eṉṉumoru cuṭṭaṭattaiyeḻuppi, iṟantupaṭṭa vīraṉatu pukaḻaip pāṇarkaḷaik koṇṭu pāṭumpaṭi ceyvatum akkāla vaḻakkamāka iruntatu. ittakaiya vīrakkal naṭutalum, pattiṉip peṇṭirukkum "catikkal' eṭuppatum kaḻakakkālattiṟkup piṟakum tamiḻakattil iruntuvanta marapukaḷ eṉpatai yum ivai vaṭanāṭṭil illāta tamiḻarkaḷukkēyuriya marapukaḷ eṉpatai yum piṟkālap puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmālai pōṉṟa nāykaḷiliruntum aṟiyalām. 
ki.pi. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻutappaṭṭa tivākara nikaṇṭil kal, māḻai, ceṅkal, maram, maṇ, cutai, maruppu, carukkarai, arakku, meḻuku ākiya pattup poruḷkaḷaik koṇṭē ciṟpaṅkaḷ ceyyap paṭṭaṉa veṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. maṇimēkalaiyil mācillāta maramum, maṇṇum, kallum ciṟpam ceyvataṟkuc ciṟantavai yeṉṟum, ivaṟṟālāṉa ciṟpaṅkaḷ pala pūmpukāril iruntaṉa veṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. intira viḻāviṉpōtu pūmpukārukku vanta makkaḷ an nakarilirunta māṭa māḷikaikaḷil vāṉavar mutalāṉa teyvavuruvaṅkaḷum, piṟavuyirkaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷum ciṟpaṅ kaḷākavum, ōviyaṅkaḷākavum kaṇṇaik kavarumāṟu kaḷituṟa viḷaṅkiyataik kaṇṭu viyantu niṉṟaṉareṉpatai maṇimēkalai yiṉ vāyilāka aṟikiṟōm. kaḻakakkāl nakaraṅkaḷil uyarntu niṉṟa māṭaṅkaḷilum, māṭivīṭukaḷilum, nilāmuṟṟaṅkaḷilum palatarappaṭṭa ciṟpaṅkaḷ iruntataip perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭaiyum kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. maṉṉarkaḷiṉ araṇmaṉaikaḷaiyum, piṟa kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷaiyum amaippa varcuḷ ciṟanta nūlaṟi pulavarkaḷāka iruntatōṭu, kaṇitam, vāṉanūl, nimittikam, poṟiyiyal mutaliyavaṟṟilum tērcciyaṭaintirun tārkaḷ. ittakaiya taṅkaḷ aṟivaik koṇṭu araṇmaṉaikaḷ amaittum, ticaikkēṟpat teyvap paṭimaṅkaḷaiyamaittum piṟa uruvaṅkaḷaic camaittum nākarikattaip paṭaittārkaḷeṉpatai neṭunalvāṭai pōṉṟa kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntu aṟikiṟōm. 
kaḻakakkālat tamiḻakattil irunta kōyilkaḷ, avaṟṟilirunta teyvap paṭimaikaḷ mutaliyavai paṟṟic cilappatikārattil kuṟippukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. piṟavā yākkaip periyōṉ kōyil civaṉ kōyil), aṟumukac cevvēḷ aṇitikaḻ kōyil, vālvaḷai mēṉi vāliyōṉ palarāmaṉ) kōyil, nīlamēṉi neṭiyōṉ tirumāl) kōyil mutaliyaṉa iruntaṉaveṉpataikkūṟum cilappatikārac ceytikaḷi viruntu civaṉ, murukaṉ, palarāmaṉ, tirumāl ākiya kaṭavuḷariṉ uruvaṅ cuḷum, vaṇṇaṅkaḷum paṭima vaṭivaṅkaḷil teḷivupaṭuttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpataiyaṟiyalām. 
maṇimēkalaiyil cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭattil ivvulakattiṉ pōlikai yuruvamoṉṟu maṇṇīṭṭil ceytu vaikkappaṭṭirunta ceyti kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṟatu. atil ceytu vaikkappaṭṭirunta ulaka vuruṇṭaiyaip(globe) paṟṟiya vaṇṇaṉaiyum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. "kaṭal cūḻntuḷḷatu, ataṉ naṭuvil mērumalai, mērumalaiyaic cuṟṟilum ēḻuvakaik kuṉṟukaḷ, nāṉilam kāṭṭum peruntīvu, cuṟṟilum ōrāyiram ciṟu tīvukaḷ, aṅkuḷḷa nila amaippukaḷ, aṅku vāḻnta uyiriṉaṅkaḷ, cūḻalkaḷ aṉaittum amaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. itu kāṇpōr aṟivu peṟum vakaiyil amaikkappaṭṭiruntatu. itu “mayaṉ' eṉṉum poṟiyāḷarāl amaikkappaṭṭāṟ pōṉṟu iruntatu." 
cuḻacukkālattil puttarai vaṇaṅkuvataṟku, avaruṭaiya pātapīṭikai paṭimamākac ceyyappeṟṟut toḻappaṭṭatu eṉpatai maṇimēkalai yum. cilappatikāramum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. piṟkālattiltāṉ puttarukku uruvam kaṟpikkappaṭṭu avaruṭaiya irunta kōlattaiyum, niṉṟa kōlattai yum, kiṭanta kōlattaiyum amaittārkaḷ. itē muṟaiyaip piṉpaṟṟit tāṉ vaiṇavar kaḷum tirumāliṉ uruvattaiyamaittārkaḷ. itaṉāṟ nāṉ cila aṟiñarkaḷ putta vikāraikaḷai aḻittut tirumāl kōyilka ḷākak kaṭṭiṉareṉṟu muḻakkamiṭukiṉṟaṉar. kaḻakakkālattil teyvat tiruvuruvaṅkaḷaip paṭimaṅkaḷāka vaittu vaṇaṅkuvataṟku īṭākat teyvaṅkaḷiṉ ciṉṉaṅkaḷaic ciṟpa vaṭivilamaittu vaṇaṅkiṉārkaḷ eṉpataiyum aṟikiṟōm. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka murukaṉai vaṇaṅkuvataṟku avaṉuṭaiya vēlaiyum, intiraṉai vaṇaṅkuvataṟku avaṉuṭaiya vaccirappaṭai allatu avaṉuṭaiya veḷḷaiyāṉai, kaṟpakattaru ākiya vaṟṟaiyum vaittu vaṇaṅkiṉārkaḷ. itaṉāltāṉ vēṟkōṭṭam, vaccirakkōṭṭam, veḷyāṉaikkōṭṭam, amararitarukkōṭṭam mutaliya palatarappaṭṭa kōṭṭaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya ceytikaḷaic cilappatikārat til kāṇkiṟōm. tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭaiyil, murukaṉ kaiyilēntum vēlaiyum, cēvaṟ koṭiyaiyum makkaḷ vaṇaṅkiyatāka aṟikiṟōm, ivvāṟu tamiḻakattil vaḷarnta marapu muṟaiyaip piṉpaṟṟittāṉ puttaruṭaiya pātapīṭikai pōtimaram mutaliya ciṉṉaṅkaḷai vaṇaṅkuvatu kaḻakakkālattilēyē vaḻakkāṟṟil vantateṉpatu cilappatikāram, maṇimēkalai ākiyavaṟṟāl aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu. piṉṉartāṉ kaṭavuḷarum, kaṭavuḷariṉ kuṭumpaṅkaḷum utirit tēvataikaḷum ciṟpaṅkaḷ ṉākavum cittiraṅkaḷākavum muḻuvuruvil paṭaikkap peṟṟaṉa, 
teyvavuruvaṅkaḷēyaṉṟi āṇ, peṇ, ceṭikoṭi, vilaṅkukaḷ, tiṅkaḷ katiravaṉ, viṇmīṉkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ uruvaṅkaḷaiyum kaḻakak kāla makkaḷ ciṟpaṅkaḷākac camaittārkaḷ, 
''nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipulam tillāṉ eḻilnalam 
maṇmāṇ puṉaipāvai yaṟṟu " 
eṉak kūṟum tirukkuṟaḷiliruntu maṇṇālum makkaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉaveṉpatai aṟiyalām. aḻakāṉa āṭavaṉaip paṟṟi varuṇikkum pōtu avaṉ vallāṉ iyaṟṟiya pāvai pōl iruntā ṉeṉṟu maturaik kāñciyil kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. tēr, cakaṭam, vaṇṭil, kaṭṭil, irukkai, maṇimuṭi mutaliyaṉa ceyyum kalaiñarkaḷum, vāḷ, vēl mutaliyaṉa ceyyum kammiyarum, kaṭṭaṭap pulavarum, maṇṇīṭ ṭāḷarum, cuṟṟaccarum, poṟkollarum ittakaiya paṭimaṅkaḷ ceyvatil kūṭṭuṟavākac ceyalpaṭṭaṉar eṉpatai maṇimēkalaiyil kāṇkiṟōm. ivarkaḷukkellām talaimaiyēṟṟa ciṟpiyaip perun taccaṉ' eṉṟaṉar. ittakaiya toḻilkaḷ yāvum marapu koṭi vaḻiyāka vaḷarntaṉa veṉpataip perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭai, akanāṉūṟu, cilappatikāram mutaliya nūlkaḷiliruntu aṟikiṟōm. 
tamiḻakattilirunta palatarappaṭṭa marapuvaḻik kalaiñarōṭu piṟanāṭṭuk kalaiñarum vantu iṇaintu paṇiyāṟṟiṉareṉpatai maṇimēkalai kūṟukiṟatu. vēntaṉiṉ pūṅkā maṇṭapattai amaippatil makata viṉaiñar, marāṭṭak kammiyar, avantikkollar, yavaṉat taccar ākiyōr tamiḻakattuk kalaiñarōṭu iṇaintu ceyalpaṭṭaṉar. ic ceytiyiliruntu ovvoru kaiviṉaiñarum ovvoru toḻilil nuṭpat tiṟamaiyaip peṟṟiruntaṉar eṉpataiyum, ittakaiyat toḻil tiṟamai koṭivaḻiyāka vaḷarntiruntatōṭu, nāṭu vaḻiyākavum vaḷarntu, kalaikku ākkamum ūkkamum tantatu eṉpataiyum aṟikiṟōm. itaṉāl tāṉ ittakaiya nuṇṇiya tiṟamai paṭaitta kalaiñarkaḷai nuṇkalaik kammiyar, kaiviṉai muṟṟiya nuṇkalaikkollar, curuntoḻiṟ kollar eṉak kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. pāvai viḷakku amaippatil yavaṉarē talaiciṟantavar eṉṟu neṭunalvāṭaikūṟukiṟatu. 
(ō) kaḷappirar
kaḻakacaṅkak kālam tōrāyamākak ki.pi. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil muṭintatu. ataṟkuppiṉ ki.pi. nāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal āṟām nāṟṟāṇṭu varai tamiḻakattil kaḷappirar" eṉṉum putiya iṉattavar tōṉṟi, tamiḻaka varalāṟṟaik 'kaḷaṅkam aṭaiyac ceytaṉar. ik kaḷappirariṉ tāyakam etu? avarkaḷiṉ paṇṭaiya varalāṟu yātu? eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavai iṉṉamum āyntu muṭivu kāṇap peṟāmal uḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, ivarkaḷaip paṟṟik ki.pi. eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil māṟaṉ caṭaiyaṉ eṉappaṭum jaṭi laparāntaka pāṇṭiyaṉāl veḷiyiṭappeṟṟa vēḷvikkuṭic ceppēṭukaḷil mutaṉmutalāka aṟikiṟōm.pāṇṭiyar nāṭṭaik kaḷappirar veṉṟaṉar eṉṟum ataṉai jaṭilaparāntakaṉ mīṇṭum kaippaṟṟiṉāṉ eṉṟum iv vēḷvikkuṭic ceppēṭṭi liruntu aṟikiṟōm. mēlum, kaḷappirar kaliyaracaṉ eṉpāṉ maṉṉarkaḷ palarai veṉṟu nāṭukaḷaik kaippaṟṟikkoṇṭāṉ eṉpataiyum avaṉaip pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉ kaṭuṅkōṉ veṉṟu avaṉiṭamirunta nāṭukaḷaittāṉ kaippaṟṟik koṇṭataiyum icceppēṭṭiliruntu aṟikiṟōm 
ik kaḷappirar yāvar, eṅkiruntu vantaṉar eṉṟa viṉākkaḷukku iṉṉamum uriya viṭaikaḷ illai. 'kaḷappāḷar' eṉṟa iṉattavarē kaḷappirar eṉṟu aṟiyappaṭṭaṉar eṉavum, ivarkaḷ tamiḻakattaic cērntavarkaḷ eṉṟum piṭicīṉivācaiyaṅkār mutalāṉōr karutukiṉṟa ṉar. tiruvēṅkaṭamalaikku arukil vāḻnta 'kaḷavar' eṉpārē kaḷappirar eṉṟu ecu. kiruṭṭiṇacāmi aiyaṅkār, ār.cattiyanātaiyar mutalāṉōr karutukiṉṟaṉar. "kaḷvar' eṉṟa collē piṉṉar 'kaḷappirar' eṉa maruviya tākaṭiē.kōpinātarāyar kūṟukiṟār. mēlum, muttaraiyar ik kaḷappirar iṉattavarē eṉṟum avar karutukiṟār. teṉṉakattil vāḻnta oḷiyar eṉṟa vakuppaic cārntavartām kaḷappirar eṉpatu ṭivi. makāliṅkattiṉ karuttu. kuṟumparkaḷ oṉṟucērntu kalakam viḷaivittu mūvēntaraiyum veṉṟu nāṭṭaik kaippaṟṟiyāṇṭatāl avarkaḷ 'kaḷappirar' eṉap paṭṭaṉar eṉpatum oru kōṭpāṭākum. 
mēṟkūṟiya yāvum ūkaṅkaḷē. āyiṉ kaḷappirar yāvar? avarkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷ allar. eṅkiruntu vantaṉar? kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭiliruntu vantaṉar. cāṉṟukaḷ yāvai? (periyapurāṇam, kallāṭam mutaliyavaṟṟil kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. karunāṭaka maṉṉaṉoruvaṉ pāṇṭiya nāṭṭaik kaippaṟṟi yāṇṭāṉ eṉṟum, avaṉ kālattil tamiḻakattil caivam cāyntu camaṇam taḻaittatu eṉṟum ivaṟṟiliruntu aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mēlum kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭil pēḷūrukku arukil kiṭaittuḷḷa kalveṭṭilum, ōcupeṭṭukku arukil kiṭaittuḷḷa oru vīrakkallilum ivarkaḷaip paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ik kuṟippukaḷiliruntu iṅku vatintavar 'kaḷpōrar' eṉṟum, ivarkaḷ nanti malaiyaṭiyil ki. pi. eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭu varai āṇṭaṉar eṉṟum, piṉṉar avarkaḷuṭaiya nāṭu cāḷukkiyar, mēlaik kaṅkar nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ iṇaikkappaṭṭu viṭṭatu eṉṟum aṟikiṟōm. itaṉai yāpparuṅkalamum valiyuṟuttu kiṟatu. 
ivarkaḷ ēṉ tamiḻakam vantaṉar? cātavākaṉap pēraracāl nerukkap paṭṭum, camaya veṟikoṇṭum ki.pi. nāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭil ivarkaḷ tamiḻakam pōntaṉar. ivarkaḷ camaṇa, cākkiya camayaṅkaḷaic cārnta varkaḷ eṉpatum, tamiḻakattil ēṟkeṉavē kuṭikoṇṭirunta ic camayaṅkaḷukku ūkkamum, ākkamum tēṭavē ivarkaḷ tamiḻakam pōntaṉ reṉpatum ūkittaṟiya vēṇṭiyavai yākum. ivarkaḷ tamiḻakam pōnta nērattil camaṇam, cākkiyam puttam cōḻa, pallava nāṭukaḷil cāykā luṭaṉ viḷaṅkiṉa. eṉavētāṉ camaṇattaip parappum nōkkuṭaṉ ivarkaḷ kipi 47-l tamiḻakattil tiramiḷa caṅkam' eṉṟa kaḻakattai niṟuvic camaṇacamayak kōṭpāṭukaḷaip parappiṉar. caṅkakālantoṭṭē kaṉṉaṭar tamiḻakattiṟku vantu kuṭiyēṟiya vaṇṇam iruntatāl kaḷappirar tiṭṭavaṭṭamākcipi nāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭiltāṉ tamiḻakam pōntaṉar eṉṟum kūṟamuṭiyavillai. paḻanimalaiyil ēṟkeṉavē kuṭiyēṟiya teṉkaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭavar ataṉaip 'paṉṟināṭu' eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ āḷumai ceytataiyum aṟikiṟōm. ākak kaṉṉaṭar kipi. nāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭu varai kūṭṭam kūṭṭamākat tamiḻakattil kuṭiyēṟip peruvāriyāṉa nilapparappil āḷumai peṟṟaṉar eṉpataip palvēṟu cāṉṟukaḷāl aṟikiṟōm. avvāṟu vantavarkaḷiṉ talaivarkaḷaip paṟṟiyum avarkaḷ naṭattiya pōrkaḷaip paṟṟiyum vivaramillai. āyiṉum " accutavikkantaṉ' eṉpāṉ māperum paṭai tiraṭṭi vantu tamiḻnāṭṭu mūvēntar kaḷaiyum veṉṟāṉ eṉṟa ceyti kipi, aintām nāṟṟāṇṭil vāḻnta putta tattar eṉpār eḻutiya 'viṉaiya viṉiccayā' eṉṟa nūlil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. inta accuḷikkantaṉ cōḻa nāṭṭil āṭci purintāṉ eṉpataiyum, ivaṉ putta camayattavaṉ eṉpatum in nūliliruntu aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṉaip pōlavē toṇṭai nāṭṭilum kaḷappirar āṇṭaṉar eṉpatum aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. civaṉaṭiyāril oruvarāṉa "kūṟṟuva nāyaṉar' 'kaḷantaimaṉṉar kaḷappirar' eṉṟu cēkkiḻār kūṟuvatiliruntu itaiyaṟikiṟōm. ivvāṟu oṉṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa kaḷappirar kuṭumpaṅkaḷ tamiḻakattil kuṭiyēṟi āṅkāṅkē āṇṭamai aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. 
kaḷappirar āṭciyāl tamiḻakattil viḷaintavai yāvai? amaiti keṭṭatu; kalakaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa; makkaḷ vāḻkkai cīrkulaintatu; tamiḻ marapu nūlkaḷ aḻintaṉapōṉṟa kuṟṟac cāṭṭukaḷē kḷappirar āṭciyaip paṟṟik kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. caivattil nāṭṭam koṇṭavarē ittakaiya kuṟṟaccāṭṭukaḷaik kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. kaḷappirarāl tamiḻk kaḻakam aḻiyavillai . ēṟkeṉavē kaḻakakkālam muṭintu, kaḻakamaruviya kālam toṭaṅkiyiruntatu. kaḷappirar vēṟṟu moḻiyiṉar camaṇa, cākkiya camayaṅkaḷaip piṉpaṟṟiyavarkaḷ; avarkaḷ tamiḻakattil āḷumai peṟṟa pōtu ic camayaṅkaḷ cāykāluṭaṉ iruntaṉa. ākavē, avarkaḷiṉ toṭakkakāla ākkam ac camayaṅkaḷiṉ koḷḷiṭa moḻikaḷāy viḷaṅkiya pāli, pirākirutam, camaṟkirutam ākiya moḻikaḷukkē payaṉ paṭṭatu, itu varalāṟṟup pōkkil vaḷarnta putiya iyalē yaṉṟik kaḷappirarāl viḷainta kēṭu eṉil muṟṟum poruntātu. āyiṉ, piṉṉar vanta pallavar kālattil camaṟkirutam ākkam peṟṟatēṉ? 
kaḷappirar āṭciyil camaṇarkaḷ tamiḻaip pēṇik kāttaṉar. tamiḻil pala ariya ilakkaṇa ilakkiya nūlkaḷ ivarkaḷāl eḻutap peṟṟaṉa. ēṟkeṉavē ki.pi, 470 -l vaccirananti eṉpāṉ amaitta "tiramiḷa caṅkam' camaṇa camaya vaḷarccikku uṟutuṇaiyāka niṉṟu ittakaiya naralkaḷ veḷivara utaviyatu. nālaṭiyār pōṉṟa patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakku nūlkaḷum, cilappatikāram, maṇimēkalai ākiya nūlkaḷum camaṇa cākkiya camayac cārpuṭaiya nūlkaḷē. ivai kaḻakam maruviya kālat til tōṉṟiyavaiyē. ākavē, kaḷappirar āṭciyāl tamiḻakattil putiya polivu ēṟpaṭṭatu, moḻi vaḷarcci yēṟpaṭṭatu eṉpataiyē uṇaramuṭikiṟatu. āṉāl, kaḷappirar tamiḻakattil iruṇṭa kālattai ēṟpaṭuttiviṭṭaṉar eṉap palarum pulampukiṉṟaṉar. avvāṟē eḻutiyum nampavaittu viṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ pulampalukkum kāraṇam illāma villai, kaḷappirar varukaiyāl, tamiḻakattil kālkoṇṭirunta 'varuṇācirama tarumam' āṭṭaṅkaṇṭatu: pārppaṉiyam nacintu pōyiṟṟu. camaṇamum, puttamum ivaṟṟai oḻikkavē vīṟiṭṭeḻunta camayaṅka ḷeṉpataiyum, kaḷappirar ic camaya vīrarkaḷeṉpataiyum maṉattiṟ koṇṭu nōkkiṉāl uṇmai oḷi teriyum. intiya varalāṟṟil eppoḻutellām eṅkellām pārppaṉiyam ceḻittuk kāṇappaṭum kiṟatō appoḻutellām aṅkellām poṟkālam' eṉap pōṟṟappaṭuvataiyum, pārppaṉiyattiṟku māṟākat tōṉṟum āṭci, allatu māṟākat tiḷaikkum karuttuccuṭar nilavum pōtellām "iruṇṭa kālam' eṉa iḻitturaikkappaṭuvataiyum kāṇkiṟōm. ulaka varalāṟṟilēyē ciṟanta iṭattaip peṟuvatu acōkar kālam' intiya varalāṟṟil puttam talaiciṟantu viḷaṅkiyatum acōkar kālamē. āṉāl, itaṉaip poṟkālam eṉap pōṟṟiṉarā? illai, āriyam ceḻittu, miḷirnta kuptar kālattaiyē poṟkālam eṉkiṉṟaṉar. meḷariyar kālattil puttakak kalaikaḷum, aṟiviyal nūlkaḷum ērāḷamāyt tōṉṟiṉa; ulaka nāṭukaḷilellām paraviṉa; ulaka nāṭukaḷiliruntu palvēṟu paṇpāṭukaḷ intiyāviṟkuḷ nuḻaintaṉa. āyiṉum, itaṉaip 'poṟkālam' eṉṟaṉarillai. itaip pōlavē puttam pukaḻpeṟṟa kucāṉar kālamum, varttaṉar kālamum poṟkālamākavillai, ivai yāvum āriyariṉ 'varuṇācirama tarumattiṟkum vēḷvi muṟaikkum māṟākac ceyalpaṭṭa āṭciyamaippukaḷ. tamiḻakattilum āriyattiṟku ēṟṟamaḷitta pallavar āṭcik kālattaiyum, cōḻar āṭciyiṉ oru pakutiyaiyum poṟkālam eṉṟaṉar. ākavē, camaṇamum puttamum taḻaikkavum, "varuṇācira tarmam' oḻiyavum pāṭupaṭṭaḷappirar āṭcik kālattai iruṇṭa kālam' eṉṟu aḻaippataik kaṇṭu viyappaṭaiyat tēvaiyillai. āriyam ciṟantōṅki niṉṟa kālaṅkaḷaiyē intiya varalāṟṟil poṟkālam' eṉpar. itu kaṇmūṭittaṉamākak kaiyāḷap paṭum potu neṟi,
kaḷappirar vīḻcci 
- kipi. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil pāṇṭiya, pallava aracukaḷ taḻait tōṅkat toṭaṅkiyatāl kaḷappirar vīḻcciyaṭaintaṉar. pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉ kaṭuṅkōṉum, pallava maṉṉaṉ cimmavicuṇuvum kaḷappirarai veṉṟu, tamiḻakattiṉiṉṟu veḷiyēṟṟiṉar. āyiṉum, kipi. eṭṭām nāṟṟāṇṭuvara iṅkumaṅkumākat tamiḻakattil ciṟṟaracarkaḷāy ivarkaḷ āṇṭu irukkalām. ivarkaḷ piṉvanta "muttaraiyarkaḷāka' āṇṭaṉar eṉpatu poruntātu. muttaraiyar vēṟu, kaḷappirar vēṟu eṉpatē teḷinta muṭipu. kaḷappirar kaṅkarēyeṉṟum, ivarkaḷ orucila āṇṭukaḷ tamiḻakattai āṇṭaṉareṉṟum orucilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. itu āyvukku uriyatē. 


5. tamiḻakam 
1. (ki.pi. 6 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiliruntu 9 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu varai) 
4 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal 9 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu varaiyil tamiḻakattil pallavarkaḷiṉ āṭci nilai peṟṟiruntatu. takkaṇattil kipi. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiliruntu paṉṉiraṇṭām nāṟṟāṇṭu varaiyilum cāḷukkiyar āṇṭaṉar. pallavarkaḷiṉ cama kālattil āṇṭa vātāpic cāḷuk kiyarkaḷ muṟkāla mēlaic cāḷukkiyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷil mutalām pulikēciki.pi. 535-586) im marapil 'makārācā' eṉṉum virutuṭaṉ āṇṭa mutalaracaṉ āvāṉ. ivaṉukkuppiṉ mutalām kīrtti varmaṉ(kipi 57 - 598), maṅkaḷēcaṉkipi. 598-50, iraṇṭām pulikēci (kipi. 10-542) ākiyōr āṇṭaṉar. iraṇṭām pulikēci teṉṉakak kaṭaṟkaraiyōramākap paṭaiyeṭuttup pala kōṭṭaikaḷaik kaippaṟṟi ṉāṉ eṉṟu avaṉuṭaiya aykōlik kalveṭṭuk kūṟukiṟatu. pallava maṉṉaṉ makēntiravarmaṉait taṉṉuṭaiya kāñcik kōṭṭaikkuḷ patuṅkumpaṭi ceytāṉ eṉṟum, piṉṉark kāviri āṟṟaik kaṭantu cōḻar, kēraḷar, pāṇṭiyaruṭaṉ naṭpuṟavu pūṇṭu pātāmi tirumpiṉāṉ eṉṟum ak kalveṭṭuk kūṟukiṟatu. ivaṉ kālattil pallavarukkum, cāḷukkiyarukkum iṭaiyē pariyalam, maṇi maṅkalam, cūramāram mutaliya 
iṭaṅkaḷil pōr naṭantatākap pallavar ceppēṭukaḷum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. kaṭaiciyāka ivaṉ pallavariṭam tōṟṟut taṉ kōnakaraiyē iḻanta tākavum aṟikiṟōm. iraṇṭām pulikēcikkup piṉ mutalām vikkira mātittaṉ kipi 855-57), vinayātittaṉcipi. 5-6ātu, vicayātittaṉ kipi. 69-13), iraṇṭām vikkiramātittaṉ ki.pi. 734-745), iraṇṭām kīrttivarmaṉ pi.745-757 ākiyōr āṇṭaṉar. ivarkaḷ kālattilum pallavarukkum, cāḷukkiyarukkum iṭaiyē pōrkaḷum, caccaravukaḷum muṇṭaṉa. kaṭaiciyāka, inta iraṇṭām kīrttivarmaṉuṭaṉ vātāpi (allatu) pātāmic cāḷukkiyar āṭci maṟaintatu. piṉṉar irunūṟu āṇṭukaḷ varai ivvāṭci irāṭṭirakūṭar āṭciyiṉ kīḻiruntatu. inta irāṭṭirakūṭaraik kipi. 973-l iraṇṭām tailaṉ eṉpavaṉ tōṟkaṭittu iraṇṭām mēlaic cāḷukkiyar āḷkuṭi marapai ēṟ paṭuttiṉāṉ. ivarkaḷ kalyāṇiyaikkōnakarākakkoṇṭu āṇṭaṉar. tamiḻakam (kipi 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭiliruntu 9ām nūṟṟāṇṭu varai) 137 
pātāmic cāḷukkiyar āṭciyaik kaippaṟṟiya irāṭṭirakūṭar kaḷiṉ āṭci kipi. eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiliruntu takkaṇattil ciṟantu viḷaṅkiyatu. ivarkaḷil mutalām kiruṭṭiṇaṉ ki.pi. 158-773), iraṇṭām kōvintaṉkipi.773-780), turuvaṉ kipi 780-793) mūṉṟām kōvintaṉ(kipi. 793 - 516, amōkavarcaṉkipi. 814-878), iraṇṭām kiruṭṭiṇaṉkipi 876-14) mutaliyōr kuṟippiṭattakkavar. ivarkaḷil turuvaṉ, mūṉṟām kōvintaṉ ākiyōr kālaṅkaḷil irāṭṭirakūṭa rukkum pallavarukkum mōtalkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kaṭaiciyākak ki.pi. 973-l irāṭṭirakūṭar āṭciyai mēṟkūṟiya iraṇṭām tailaṉ kaippaṟṟi iraṇṭām mēlaic cāḷukkiyar āṭciyai ēṟpaṭuttiṉāṉ. 
mēlaic cāḷukkiya maṉṉarkaḷāṉa iraṇṭām tailaṉ kipi 973 997), mutalām cōmēcuvaraṉ ki.pi. 104257), iraṇṭām cōmēcuvaraṉ ki.pi. 108-75), āṟām vikkiramātittiyaṉ(ki.pi. 1076-1126), mūṉṟām cōmēcuvaraṉkipi. 1757-36) ākiyōr toṭarntu āṇṭapiṉ mēlaic cāḷukkiyar āṭciyum oru muṭivukku vantatu. kaṭaiciyākak ki.pi. 1190-l itu maṟaintatu. 
ki.pi. ēḻām nāṟṟāṇṭiliruntē vēṅkiyait talainakarākak koṇṭu āṇṭa kīḻaic cāḷukkiyar āṭciyum iruntatu. iraṇṭām pulikēci marāttiya nāṭṭiṉ vaṭapakutiyaiyum, teṉ pakutiyaiyum ki.pi. 15-l veṉṟu taṉ tampiyāṉa vicuṇuvarttaṉaṉaikipi. 815-33) āḷac ceytāṉ. inta vicuṇuvarttaṉaṉtāṉ piṉṉar ip pakutiyait taṉittāṇṭu kīḻaic cāḷukkiya marapait tōṟṟuvittāṉ. ivaṉukkup piṉ mutalām ceyacimmaṉ ki.pi. 633-63 mutalāṉōr toṭarntu āṇṭaṉar. im marapil tōṉṟiya vimalātittiyaṉ eṉpavaṉ cōḻa aracaṉ mutalām irācarācaṉ makaḷāṉa kuntavai eṉpavaḷai maṇantāṉ, ivaṉ makaṉ irācarāca vicuṇuvarttaṉaṉ tāymāmaṉ mutalām irāca rācaṉ makaḷ ammaṅka tēviyai maṇantāṉ. ivaṉ makaṉ iraṇṭām irācēntiraṉ. ivaṉ ki.pi. 1067-l cāḷukkiya nāṭṭiṉ aracu kaṭṭil ēṟiṉāṉ. ivaṉ kālattil cōḻa nāṭṭiṉ aracurimaikkup piṟaṅkaṭai yillātatāl ivaṉē cōḻa nāṭṭiṉ maṉṉaṉ āṉāṉ. avvāṟu aracaṉāṉa vuṭaṉ ivaṉ 'mutalām kulōttuṅkaṉ' eṉap peyar pūṇṭāṉ. atāvatu iraṇṭām irācēntiraṉ eṉṉum iyaṟpeya ruṭaṉ cāḷukkiya nāṭṭaiyāṇṭa ivaṉ cōḻa nāṭṭiṉ aracaṉāka muṭicūṭṭik koṇṭavuṭaṉ muṭicūṭṭup peyarāka apicēka nāmam) *mutalām kulōttuṅkaṉ' eṉṉum peyaraic cūṭikkoṇṭāṉ. itaṉāl cōḻanāṭum cāḷukkiyanāṭum oru kuṭaikkīḻ iṇaintaṉa. 
ivvāṟu, takkāṇattil irunta cāḷukkiya irāṭṭirakūṭa aracar cuḷukkum pallava, cōḻa aracarkaḷukkum toṭarpukaḷ iruntatai yaṟikiṟōm. ciṟappāka, īṇṭu mutalil pallavarkaḷaippaṟṟiyum, piṉṉarc cōḻarkaḷaip paṟṟiyum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. 
(a) pallavar varalāṟu
iṉṟuvarai pallavariṉ tāyakam paṟṟi āynta aṟiñarkaḷ muṭinta muṭivāka etaiyum kāṇavillai. āyiṉum, avarkaḷiṉ āyvukaḷiṉ taṉmaikaḷaippaṟṟi maṭṭum īṇṭuk kūṟutal iṉṟiyamaiyātatu. ivarkaḷaip pahlavar eṉṟum, pāracīka nāṭṭiliruntu vantavarkaḷ eṉṟum, kuṟumpar kōpālar, pāvar pōṉṟa in nāṭṭu iṉattavari liruntu vantavar eṉṟum iruvakai āyvukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tāṟkālikamāka ivarkaḷ āntirap pēraracu(cātavākaṉap pēraracu) aḻintapiṉ avarkaḷukkukkīḻc ciṟṟaracarāyirunta ivarkaḷiṉ mūtātaiyar ciṟitu ciṟitāka valuppeṟṟut tamiḻakam pōntu, pallavap pērāṭciyai . ēṟpaṭuttik koṇṭaṉar eṉṟu kūṟalām, 
ip pallavar āṭciyai niṟuviyavaṉ 'papparatēvaṉ' eṉṟum, ivaṉukkup piṉ toṭarntu maṉṉarkaḷ palar āṇṭaṉar eṉṟum pal cāṉṟukaḷiliruntu aṟikiṟōm. ivvāṟu āṇṭa pallavarkaḷai muṟkālap pallavarkaḷ, iṭaikkālap pallavarkaḷ, piṟkālap pallavarkaḷ eṉa mūvakaiyākap pirittum kūṟuvar. 
kipi. 250 mutal 340 varaiyil āṇṭa pallavar muṟkālap pallavar - eṉṟum, kipi. 340 mutal 55 varaiyil āṇṭavar iṭaikkālap pallavar eṉṟum, kipi.75 mutal 900 varai āṇṭavar piṟkālap pallavar eṉṟum aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉar, īṇṭu nām piṟkālap pallavarkaḷaip paṟṟic curukkamāka aṟiyalām. ivarkaḷilum mutal ēḻu pallava maṉṉarkaḷ “mūtta kālvaḻiyiṉar' eṉṟum, piṉvarum maṉṉarkaḷ 'iḷaiya kālvaḻiyiṉar' eṉṟum aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. cimmavikaṇumutal toṭaṅkum ivarkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyal aparācitavarmaṉ varaiyil ceṉṟu muṭikiṟatu. cimmavicuṇu kipi.570 mutal 60 varaiyil āṇṭāṉ. aparācitavarmaṉ kipi. 895 mutal 13 varai āṇṭāṉ. āka, piṟkālap pallavarkaḷiṉ āṭci tōrāyamāka mūṉṟarai nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ iruntatu eṉalām.
cimmavicuṇu (ki.pi. 570-60 
cimmavicuṇu iṭaikkālap pallavariṉ kaṭaiciyaracaṉāṉa mūṉṟām cimmavarmaṉ ki.pi. 550-570) eṉpāṉiṉ makaṉ āvāṉ. aḵtāvatu mutalām nantivarmaṉ eṉpāṉiṉ oṉṟuviṭṭa uṭaṉpiṟantāṉiṉ makaṉ āvāṉ. ivaṉuṭaiya tampiyē pīmavarmaṉ eṉpavaṉ. ituvarai ivaṉ kālattup paṭṭayam etuvum kiṭaikkavillai, "avanti cuntari katai yi viruntu ivaṉ kaṅka aracaṉāṉa turuvinītaṉ eṉpāṉukkum, cāḷukkiya aracaṉāṉa vicuṇuvarttaṉaṉ eṉpāṉukkum camakālattavaṉ eṉpatai aṟikiṟōm. kaṅka, cāḷukkiya aracarkaḷiṉ kālavaraiyaṟaikaḷai mūlamākak koṇṭum vēlūrppāḷaiyam, kācakkuṭi, kūram paṭṭayaṅ kaḷaikkoṇṭum ivaṉuṭaiya kālattaik kaṇikkalām. ivaṉ makaṉāṉa mutalām makēntiravarmaṉ, tāṉ iyaṟṟiya 'mattavilācap pirakacaṉam" eṉṉum nūlil 'pallavakkulattait tāṅkum malai pōṉṟavaṉ' eṉṟum, 'nukarccip poruḷkaḷ aṉaittaiyu muṭaiyavaṉ' eṉṟum, 'pal nāṭukaḷaiyum veṉṟavaṉ' eṉṟum, 'vīrattil intiraṉaiyum, celvat til kupēraṉaiyum ottavaṉ' eṉṟum, aracarkaḷuḷ arimāveṉṟum cimmavicuṇuvaip pukaḻnturaikkiṟāṉ. cimmavicuṇu, cōḻa pāṇṭiya cērarai veṉṟatōṭu, īḻattilum pallavarāṭciyai nilaināṭṭiṉāṉ eṉpataip piṟkālap paṭṭayaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. 
vēlūrppāḷaiyam ceppēṭukaḷil ivaṉ cōḻanāṭṭai veṉṟa ceyti ciṟappākac cittarikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. “oḷirum kamuka maraṅkaḷaiyum, cennel vayalkaḷaiyum taṉakku aṇikalaṉkaḷāka aṇin tuḷḷa kavēraṉ makaḷāṉa kāviriyiṉāl ceḻumaiyaṭaiyum cōḻa nāṭṭaic cimmavicuṇu kaippaṟṟiṉāṉ.' 
'ataṉ piṟaku ip puviyulakil arimāp pōṉṟu ciṟantirunta cimmavicuṇu cimmaṉ eṉpāṉai veṟṟi koṇṭāṉ. māḷavattaiyum piṟaku kaḷappirar, cōḻar, pāṇṭiyar ākiya maṉṉar kaḷaiyum, taṉ tōḷvaliyāl iṟumāp puṟṟirunta ciṅkaḷa aracaṉaiyum, kēraḷa aracaṉaiyum veṟṟi koṇṭāṉ' eṉṟu kācakkuṭic ceppēṭukaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu'. 
ittakaiya cāṉṟukaḷiliruntu ivaṉ teṉṉāṭṭu aracarkaḷai veṉṟu, kāviriyāṟu vara taṉatu āṭciyai virivupaṭuttiṉāṉ eṉpatai yum, ivaṉē mutaṉmutalākak kāñciyaik konakarākak koṇṭu āṇṭāṉ eṉpataiyum ūkittaṟiyalām. ivaṉ kālattil tāṉ kāñci kaḷappirariṭamiruntu kaippaṟṟappaṭṭatu eṉak karutuvārum uṇṭu. ivaṉ kāñciyaik kaippaṟṟiyatāl kaṟṟavaraip pakaivariṭamiruntu kāppāṟṟiṉāṉ eṉṟu avanti cuntari katai'yil pōṟṟappeṟukiṟāṉ. iṅkuk "kaṭikā' eṉpataik kāñcipuram eṉṟē yāvarum koḷvar, 'kaṭikā' eṉpatu maṟaiyiyal kallūri yeṉṟum, aḵtu amaikkap peṟṟirunta kāñcipurattiṟkē ataṉ vaḻi ac ciṟappup peyar ēṟpaṭṭa teṉṟum ār. kōpālaṉ, carācup pātiriyār mutaliyōr karutu kiṉṟaṉar'. cimmavicuṇu putukkōṭṭai varaiyiluḷḷa cōḻa nāṭṭai veṉṟāṉ eṉpataik kañcaṉūrarp paṭṭayattiliruntu aṟikiṟōm, ivaṟṟai viḻuppuram māvaṭṭatti luḷḷa tirukkōvilūr mutaliyaviṭaṅ kaḷaik koṇṭa, ak kāla nāṭu maḻavar nāṭu -malaināṭu allatu malāṭu eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭṭatu. ataṉaiyum cimmavicuṇu veṉṟāṉ. 
ivvāṟu oru perum nilapparappiṟku aracaṉāyt tikaḻnta cimmavicuṇu kāñciyaik kōnakarāykkoṇṭu ciṟanta āṭci muṟaiyai nilaināṭṭiṉāṉ. pala pōrkaḷukkup piṉ amaitiyai nilaināṭṭiya ivaṉ oru ciṟanta vaiṇavaṉ āvāṉ. vicuṇuvait 'taṉ pattiyāl vaṇaṅkiyavaṉ' eṉṟu ivaṉai utayēntiram ceppēṭu pukaḻnturaik kiṟatu. māmallapurattiluḷḷa ātivarākar kōyililuḷḷa cimmavicuṇu viṉ cilai avaṉuṭaiya camayappaṟṟai uṟutippaṭuttuvatāyuḷḷatu. āṉāl ikkōyil avaṉuṭaiya makaṉāṉa mutalām makēntiravarmaṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. . 
ivvuruvac cilaiyiṉ etirēyuḷḷa pāṟaiyil 'srīmakēntiraṉ pōtāti rājaṉ' eṉṟu pallava- kiranta eḻuttukaḷil poṟikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. iṅkuk kāṇappaṭum maṟṟōr uruvac cilai makēntiravarma ṉuṭaiyatu. ivaṉ iḷavaracaṉāyiruntapōtu taṉ tantai cimmavicuṇu vuṭaṉ ikkōyilukku vantirukkak kūṭumeṉṟum, eṉavē, ikkōyil cimma vicuṇuviṉ kālattiltāṉ kaṭṭappeṟṟirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟum kūṟuvār. āṉāl, itu tavaṟāṉa karuttākum. ikkōyil makēntiravarmaṉ kālattiltāṉ kaṭṭappeṟṟateṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar muṭivākum. 
cimmavicuṇu ciṟanta vaiṇavappittaṉāka iruntatōṭu ciṟanta kalaiñaṉākavum, kalaiñarkaḷaip pōṟṟip purappavaṉākavum viḷaṅkiṉāṉ. taṇṭi eṉṉum vaṭamoḻip pulavarum, pāravi eṉṉum maṟṟoru vaṭamoḻip pulavarum ivaṉuṭaiya avaiyiliruntōril kuṟippiṭattakkavar āvar. avanti cuntari katai, ciṟanta ilakkaṇa nūlākiya taṇṭiyalaṅkāram mutaliya nūlkaḷait taṇṭiyum, kiratārccuṉiyam eṉṉum nālaip pāraviyum eḻutiṉar. avanticuntari kataiyiliruntu kīḻaic cāḷukkiya maṉṉaṉāṉa vicuṇuvarttaṉiṉum, kaṅka aracaṉāṉa turuvinītaṉum cimmavicuṇuviṉ kālattavar eṉpatai aṟikiṟōm. ivaṉ" kālattil māṟavarmaṉ avaṉi cūḷāmaṇi eṉpāṉ pāṇṭiya nāṭṭu aracaṉāka iruntāṉ. ivvāṟu cimmavicuṇu teṉṉakattil pallavaruk kāṉa oru putiyaviyalait toṭaṅkit taṉ vāḻnāḷilēyē pallavamarapai varalāṟṟil iṭampeṟac ceytuviṭṭu maṟaintāṉ. ivaṉukkup piṉ ivaṉ makaṉāṉa mutalām makēntiravarmaṉ ariyaṇaiyēṟiṉāṉ, 
mutalām makēntiravarmaṉ (ki.pi. 600-630) 
cimmavikaṇuvukkup piṉ ariyaṇaiyēṟiya avaṉuṭaiya makaṉāṉa mutalām makēntiravarmaṉ, taṉ tantaiyāl āḷappeṟṟa vaṭakkē kiruṭṭiṇaiyāṟu mutal teṟkē putukkōṭṭai varaiyi luḷḷa perunilap parappiṟkut talaivaṉ āṉāṉ. ivaṉ iḷavaraca ṉāka irunta pōtu teluṅku nāṭṭil taṅkiyiruntāṉ eṉṟum, itaṉaik kuṇṭūr māvaṭṭattil cēcarlā eṉṉumiṭattiluḷḷa kapōtēcuvaraṉ kōyil kalveṭṭuk kuṟippiṭukiṟatu eṉṟum kūṟuvar. ivaṉ kālattil pallava - nāṭṭiṉ vaṭavellaiyilirunta cāḷukkiya nāṭṭaiyāṇṭa iraṇṭām pulikēci katampar, kaṅkar, āḷupar, mayūrar mutaliya ciṟṟaracarkaḷaiyaṭakkip pēraracai nilai nāṭṭiṉāṉ. ivaṉuṭaiya aykōlik kalveṭṭil tāṉ vēṅkiyai veṉṟapiṉ pallava nāṭṭaiyum tākkiyatākak kuṟippiṭu kiṉṟāṉ. itiliruntu cimmavicuṇu kālattil toṭaṅkiya pallavar cāḷukkiyar pakaimai toṭarntu makēntiravarmaṉ kālattilum nilaviyatai aṟikiṟōm. 'pulikēci taṉṉuṭaiya cēṉaiyai naṭattic cellum pōtu kiḷampiya tūḷiyāṉatu etirkkavanta pallava maṉṉaṉiṉ oḷiyai maṅkac ceytatu, pulikēciyiṉ perum paṭaikkaṭalaik kaṇṭu kāñcipurattaracaṉ kāñcikkōṭṭaiyil oḷintu koṇṭāṉ eṉṟum, pallavarkaḷiṉ paṭaiyākiya kuḷirnta paṉikkuk kaṭuṅkatirā yuḷḷa katiravaṉaip pōṉṟa avaṉ (pulikēci) cōḻa, pāṇṭiya, kēraḷar kaḷukkup perum makiḻcciyai uṇṭākkiṉāṉ eṉṟum aykōlik kalveṭṭuk kūṟukiṟatu' 
ittakaiya cāṉṟukaḷiliruntu mutalām makēntiravarmaṉ cāḷuk kiya nāṭṭai yāāṇṭa iraṇṭām pulikēci (ki.pi. 500-542eṉpāṉiṭam tōṟṟu āntira nāṭṭilirunta taṉ nāṭṭiṉ oru pakutiyaic cāḷuk kiyarukkup paṟikoṭuttāṉ eṉpatai aṟikiṟōm. appakuti vēṅkiyai uḷḷaṭakkiyatākum. ap pakutiyaip pallavariṭamiruntu kaippaṟṟiya iraṇṭām pulikēci taṉ tampiyākiya vicuṇuvart taṉaṉait taṉakku aṭaṅkiyāḷumpaṭi amarttiṉāṉ. piṉṉar vicuṇuvarttaṉaṉ itaṉait taṉittāḷa muṟpaṭṭut taṉimarapaiyum ēṟpaṭuttik koṇṭāṉ. ivaṉ vaḻittōṉṟiyavēṅkināṭṭuc cāḷukkiyar *kīḻaic cāḷukkiya reṉṟu aḻaikkap peṟṟaṉar. ataṉāl vātāpiyait talainakarākak koṇṭu āṇṭa mūlac cāḷukkiyar "mēlaic cāḷukkiyar' eṉṟu aḻaikkap peṟṟaṉar. 
makēntiravarmaṉ iraṇṭām pulikēciyiṭam tōṟṟut taṉatu āṭciyiṉ vaṭavellaiyai iḻantāṉāyiṉum, kaṭaiciyākap pulikēci yaiyum avaṉukkut tuṇaiyāka niṉṟa aracarkaḷ palaraiyum kāñcikku vaṭakkē patiṉaintu kal tolaiviluḷḷa puḷḷalūr eṉṉumiṭattil puṟaṅkāṇac ceytāṉ. puḷḷalūril etirikaḷ aṉaivaraiyum avaṉ aḻittu avarkaḷai vēraṟuttāṉ. piṉṉar avaṉ kaṭṭaḷai aṉaiva rālum ēṟṟukkoḷḷappeṟṟatu eṉṟum, intiraṉaip pōṉṟu kīrtti 
vāynta makēntiravarmaṉ pūmaṇṭalattaip pātukāttu vantāṉ eṉṟum kācakkuṭic ceppēṭukaḷ pukaḻnturaikkiṉṟaṉa. 
taṉ nāṭṭiṉ vaṭapakutiyaip pulikēciyiṭam tōṟṟapiṉ makēntira ṉiṉ āṭci vaṭakkē vaṭapeṇṇaiyāṟṟiliruntu teṟkē putuk kōṭṭai varaiyil toṇṭai maṇṭalattaiyum, cōḻa maṇṭalat taiyum koṇṭatāka iruntatu, aḵtāvatu iṉṟaiya nellūr māvaṭṭattiṉ teṉpakuti, cittar, vēlūr, tiruvaṇṇāmalai, kāñcipuram, kaṭalūr, viḻuppuram, tirucci, tañcāvūr, putukkōṭṭai , cēlam ākiya māvaṭṭaṅkaḷai uḷḷaṭakki niṉṟatu. 
ivaṉ kālattil mallai allatu kaṭalmallai iṉṟaiya māmallapuram ciṟanta tuṟaimukappaṭṭiṉamākat tikaḻntatu. itu toṇṭaimāṉ iḷantiraiyaṉ eṉṉum cōḻa maṉṉaṉ kālattilēyē ciṟantu viḷaṅkiya tuṟaimukappaṭṭiṉamākum. itaṉaik kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār pāṭiya perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭaiyil kāṇ kiṟōm'. 
makēntiraṉ oru ciṟanta kaṭṭaṭakkalai vallunaṉ. ivaṉāl amaikkap peṟṟa ūrkaḷum, ērikaḷum iṉṟum ivaṉ peyarai nilaināṭṭi niṟkiṉṟaṉa. māmaṇṭūrppaṟṟil taṉ peyarāl makēntira maṅkalam' eṉṉum ūrai ēṟpaṭuttiṉāṉ eṉpataṉait tirupparuttik kuṉṟattu varttamāṉar kōyil kalveṭṭuk kūṟukiṟatu'. vaṭārkkāṭu māvaṭṭam vālājāpēṭṭai vaṭṭattil cōḻaliṅka purattiṟkut teṉkiḻakkē mūṉṟu kal tolaiviluḷḷa 'makēntiravāṭi' eṉṉum ūrum ivaṉāl ēṟpaṭuttap peṟṟatākum. ivvūrukkuc ciṟanta nīrp pācaṉa ēntiṟkāka 'smakēntirat taṭākam' eṉṉum ēriyaiyum ivaṉ veṭṭuvittāṉ. itu pōṉṟa maṟṟoru ciṟanta ēriyai māmaṇṭūrilum amaittāṉ. ataṟkuc cittiramēkat taṭākam' eṉṟu peyar, 
makēntiraṉ taṉ peyarāl 'makēntirappaḷḷi" eṉṉum uraiyum, civaṉ kōyilaiyum cōḻanāṭṭil amaittāṉ. iṉṟu ivvūr *kōyilaṭippāḷaiyam eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟatu. ic civaṉ kōyil ceṅkal, kal, kārai, māḻai mutaliyavaṟṟāl amaikkap peṟṟirukkalām. itupōṉṟa pala kaṭṭumāṉak kōyilkaḷai ivaṉ amaittāṉ eṉpatai ārāycciyāḷarkaḷ palarum oppukiṉṟaṉar. 
ivaṉuṭaiya virutup peyarkaḷai ivaṉ kālattuk kuṭaivaraik kōyilkaḷilum, piṟa cāṉṟukaḷilum kāṇalām. vallam kuṭaivaraiyil 'mayēntirap pōttarecaru' eṉṟu tamiḻilum, māmallapurattu varākar kuṭaivaraiyil "srīmahēntra pōtrāti rājaṉ' eṉṟu pirākirutatti lum, pallāvaram, tirucci ākiya kuṭaivaraikaḷil 'srīmavēhantra vikram' eṉṟu camaṟkirutattilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa, “piṇa piṇakku' eṉṟu tamiḻilum ivaṉ virutupeyar tiruccik kuṭaivaraiyil poṟikkappaṭṭa ṭuḷḷatu. ivaṉ iḷavaracaṉāka iruntapōtu āntira nāṭṭil iruntāṉ. itaṉāl teluṅkilum ivaṉukkup pala ciṟappup peyarkaḷ uḷḷaṉa, avai civilntu, tilvilōṉaiyampu, vēntuluvittu, pacarampu, cilampu, malāyu, kaṭuttarampu mutaliyaṉavākum. makēntiravikramavarmaṉ, makēntira pōttaracaṉ, pākappiṭuku, lalitāṅkuraṉ, kuṇaparaṉ, matta vilācaṉ, avaṉiyācaṉ, cattiya cantaṉ, purucōttamaṉ, catturumallaṉ, caṅkīrṇa cāti, citrakārappuli, cētkāri, vicitracittaṉ, aluptakāmaṉ, kalakap piriyaṉ, apimukaṉ, makāmēkaṉ narēntiraṉ mutaliya ciṟappup peyarkaḷum ivaṉukkuṇṭu. ivaṉ kuṭaivaraikaḷai amaittatāl cētakāri (caittiyakkāraṉ eṉṟum, icaikkalaiyil vallavaṉāṉatāl caṅkīrṇa cāti eṉṟum, ōviyakkalaiyil vallavaṉāṉatāl cittirakkārappuli eṉṟum, mattavilācam eṉṉum nakaiccuvai nāṭakattai ivaṉ eḻutiya tāl mattavilācaṉ eṉṟum ciṟappāka aḻaikkappeṟṟāṉ. 
ittakaiya virutuppeyarkaḷaik koṇṭē ivaṉ kālattil ivaṉāl vaḷarkkappeṟṟa kalaikaḷaippaṟṟi aṟiyalām. kuṭaivaraik kōyilkaḷait tamiḻakattil mutaṉ mutalil ciṟappuṟa amaittavaṉ ivaṉē. ivaṉukku muṉṉum kuṭaivaraikaḷ tamiḻakattil iruntaṉa eṉpataṟkāṉa ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. āyiṉ iṉṟaḷavum nām kaṇṭukaḷikkum nilaiyiluḷḷa palvēṟu kuṭaivaraikaḷaic camaittavaṉ ivaṉēyātalāl ivaṉ peritum pōṟṟappaṭukiṟāṉ. 'kōyil' eṉpatē ivaṉ kālattiltāṉ mutaṉ mutalākat tamiḻakattil kaṭṭappeṟṟatu eṉṟu niṉaippatu tavaṟu. kaḻakakkālattilum, ataṟku muṉṉum tamiḻakatti lirunta maṇṭaḷi, kaṟṟaḷikaḷaippaṟṟi virittaṟikiṟōm. kaṭṭaṭak kalaiyait tamiḻarkaḷ cevvaṉē aṟintu vaḷarttaṉar. atiloru putumaiyai ivaṉ pukuttiṉāṉ eṉpataṟkākavē ivaṉaip pōṟṟu kiṟōm. kaṟṟuṇṭu kaḷai aṭukkik cuṟṟaḷikaḷai amaitta ivaṉ piṉṉar perumpāṟai kaḷaik kuṭaintu kuṭaivaraikaḷai ēṟpaṭuttiṉāṉ eṉpatālēyē ivaṉaic cērkkāri' eṉṟaṉar. ivaṉāl amaikkap peṟṟa cuṟṟaḷikaḷ aḻintupaṭṭaṉa. kāñci ēkāmparanātar kōyil veḷḷu varā peḷarṇami maṇṭapakkaruṅkaltūṇ oṉṟil kāṇappaṭum - apimuka, citrakāra puvi, kūṟṟampu mutaliya veṭṭeḻuttukkaḷaik koṇṭu ivaṉāl kaṭṭappeṟṟa oru kaṟṟaḷiyilirunta it tūṇ eñci niṉṟu im maṇṭapattiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttappeṟṟatu eṉpataiyaṟikiṟōm. itaippōlavē mēṟkūṟiya makēntirap paḷḷic civaṉ kōyilum ivaṉāl kaṭṭappeṟṟatē. iṉṟu intu aḻintupaṭṭālum tiruñāṉa campantariṉ tēvārattiliruntu itaṉ ciṟappaik kāṇalām. ivaṉāl camaikkappeṟṟa kuṭaivaraikaḷaip paṟṟiyum, ivaṉuṭaiya piṟa kalaip paṇikaḷaippaṟṟiyum pallavar kalaippaṇi" eṉṉum talaippil viḷakkam kāṇalām. 
camayam 
makēntiravarmaṉ kālattil tamiḻakameṅkum puttamum camaṇamum ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉa. makkaḷuḷ perumpālōr ivviru camayaṅkaḷaiyē piṉpaṟṟiṉar. makēntiraṉum camaṇaṉākavē iruntāṉ. ivaṉatu piṟkāla āṭciyil caivam talaitūkkat toṭaṅkiyatu. tiruñāṉacampantariṉ tantaiyāṉa civapāta virutaiyar ivaṉ kālattil vāḻntavar. avar puttamum, camaṇamum ceḻittu, caivam maṅki niṉṟataik kaṇṭu maṉam varuntiṉār eṉpatait tiruñāṉa campantarē tam tēvārattil kūṟi yuḷḷār? 
pāṇṭiya nāṭṭilum camaṇam ciṟappuṟṟuc caivam kuṉṟip pōṉataic cēkkiḻār perumāṉum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. 
ik kālattil caivamum, vaiṇavamum kuṉṟiyiruntaṉa. āyiṉum, avviru camayaṅkaḷum makkaḷāl piṉpaṟṟappeṟṟaṉa. orupuṟam ivviru camayaṅkaḷum eḻucciyuṟat tuṭittaṉa. maṟupuṟam camaṇamum puttamum ciṟappuṟṟu niṉṟaṉa. āka ik kālattil iṟaineṟi iyakkam' nāṭeṅki lum viṟuviṟuppāka vaḷarntu niṉṟatu, nāḷāvaṭṭattil caivanāyaṉmār kaḷum, vaiṇava aṭiyārkaḷum peritum muyaṉṟu taṅkaḷ camayaṅkaḷai vaḷarkkavum, piṟa camayaṅkaḷai vīḻttavum muṟpaṭṭaṉar. itaṉāl ivaṉ kālattil camayap pūcalkaḷ nāṭeṅkaṇum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. ip pōril camaṇa - putta camayaṅkaḷ vīḻcciyaṭaintu maṅkiṉa, caivam talaitūkki niṉṟatu. itaṟkup peritum uṟutuṇaiyāy niṉṟavar tirunāvukkaracar āvār. tam vāḻnāḷil perum pakutiyaic camaṇattiṉ camayakuruvāka iruntu kaḻitta ivar tam cūlainōy tīrac civaṉai vaḻipaṭṭuc caivar āṉār. pāṭalipurattil camaṇar kuruvāka iruntapōtu ivaraic camaṇarkaḷ 'tarumacēṉar' eṉṟu aḻaittaṉar. caivarāṉapiṉ caiva iṟaineṟi iyakkattaip parappit tirunāvukkaracar' eṉṉum ciṟappup peyar peṟṟār. ivaruṭaṉ cērntu tiruñāṉacampantarum caivattaip parappiṉār. campantar iḷaiñar; mutumaiyil caivam cērnta nāvuk karacarai ivar apparē' (tantaiyē eṉṟu aḻaittatāl nāvukkaracarukku 'appar' eṉṉum ciṟappup peyarum uṇṭāyiṟṟu. 
camaṇam viṭuttuc caivam taḻuviya tirunāvukkaracarē camaṇattil āḻntirunta makēntiravarmaṉaiyum caivam taḻuvac ceytār. caivam taḻuviya makēntiraṉ piṉṉar camaṇattai aḻikka muṟpaṭṭāṉ eṉpataṉaip periyapurāṇattiliruntu aṟikiṟōm. periyapurāṇattil varum ap pāṭalil makēntiraṉ pāṭalipurattuc camaṇap paḷḷiyai iṭittu, ak kaṟkaḷaik koṇṭu, tiruvatikaic civaṉ kōyilaik kaṭṭiṉāṉ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu'. itil varum kāṭavaṉ, pallavaṉ, kuṇataraṉ eṉṉum ciṟappup peyarkaḷ makēntiraṉaiyē kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. 
avaṉatu tiruccikkuṭaivaraiyil viṅkattai vaḻipaṭum kuṇaparaṉ eṉṟa ciṟappup peyar koṇṭa aracaṉ inta liṅkattiṉāl puṟac camayattiliruntu tirumpiya ñāṉam meyyaṟivu) ivvulakattil nīṇṭu nilainiṟpatāka" eṉṟu poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu' 
ivvāṟu, periyapurāṇac cāṉṟukaḷālum, mēṟkaṇṭa kalveṭṭuc cāṉṟālum makēntiraṉ camaṇattiliruntu caiva camayattiṟku ! māṟṟap peṟṟāṉ eṉpatait teḷivāka aṟikiṟōm. mēlum, tiruvatikaiyilirunta kuṇataravīccaram' eṉṉum civaṉ kōyilaik kaṭṭiya "kuṇaparaṉ' ivaṉē eṉpataiyum palvēṟu cāṉṟukaḷiliruntum teḷivuṟukiṟōm. ivaṉ tōrāyamāka ki.pi. 620 - l caivaṉāka māṟiyirukkalām ivaṉait toṭarntu kuṭimakkaḷum viraintu caivar āyiṉar. ivaṉāl camaikkappeṟṟa vallam, taḷavāṉūr, cīyamaṅkalam, pallāvaram, tirucci mutaliya kuṭavaraikaḷ civaṉ kōyilkaḷākavum, makēntiravāṭik kuṭai varai perumāḷ kōyilākavum iruppatāl vaiṇavattai ivaṉ veṟukkavillai eṉpatai aṟiyalām. āṉāl, camaṇam kuṉṟi aḻiyattoṭaṅkiyatu.
kalaippaṇi 
makēntiravarmaṉ pāṟaikaḷaik kuṭaintu kōyilkaḷaic camaikkum ciṟanta kalaippaṇiyaic ceytāṉ eṉakkūṟiṉōm. ituvaṉṟi icai, naṭaṉam, ciṟpam, ōviyam, nāṭakam ākiya kalaikaḷaiyum ivaṉ ciṟappuṟa vaḷarttāṉ. cittaṉṉavācalil uḷḷa kuṭaivaraik kōyili luḷḷa ōviyaṅkaḷ ivaṉ kālattavai eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatu. āṉāl iṉṟu ārāycciyāḷarkaḷ ivai pāṇṭiyar kālattavai eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. naṭaṉamātarkaḷ iruvar iṅku kaṇkavar ōviyaṅkaḷākat tiṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. iṉtoru camaṇak kōyilātaliṉ tīrttaṅkarar ' uruvac cilaikaḷum itil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟāl akkālac ciṟpak kalaiyiṉ perumaiyai uṇaramuṭikiṟatu. eṉavē, makēntiravarmaṉiṉ cittaṉṉavācal kuṭaivaraik kōyil ōviyam, ciṟpam, naṭaṉam ākiya mūṉṟu kalaikaḷaiyum uṇarttum kūṭamākat tikaḻkiṟatu. ivaṉ oru ciṟanta icaip pulavaṉum āvāṉ. ak kalaiyiṉ vaḷarccikkākap putukkōṭṭaiyiluḷḷa kuṭumiyāṉmalaip pāṟai oṉṟil ivaṉ icaiyiṉ ōcai nayaṅkaḷaip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷaic cetukkiyuḷḷāṉ. 'intak kalveṭṭu icai māṇavar naṉmaikkākap peṇkaḷai vakuttut tanta uruttirāccāriyār eṉpavariṉ māṇavaṉāṉa maṉṉariṉ kaṭṭaḷaip paṭi veṭṭappaṭṭatu" eṉṟu ak kalveṭṭil kuṟikkappeṟṟuḷḷatu. cilar ikkalveṭṭu ivaṉ kālattiṟkup piṟaku veṭṭapeṟṟatu eṉṟum kūṟuvar. eṉavē, itupaṟṟiya iṟutiyāṉa muṭivu iṉṉum ēṟpaṭavillai ! māmaṇṭūriluḷḷa kalveṭṭu oṉṟil kāṇappaṭum collāṭcikaḷai yum avaṟṟil kāṇappaṭum curam, varṇam, ivaṟṟai vakutta vāṉmīkiyā raip paṟṟiyatākak kāṇappaṭum kuṟippaiyum, mattavilācap pirakacaṉam paṟṟiya kuṟippaiyum, aṟinta aṟiñarkaḷ cilar ivai kuṭumiyāṉ malaiyiluḷḷa kalveṭṭōṭu ottu iruppatākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar, mēlum, kuṭumiyāṉmalaik kalveṭṭum, tirucciyiluḷḷa kuṭaivaraik kōyil kalveṭṭum palavaṟṟālum oṉṟē pōl uḷḷaṉa. eṉavē tāṉ kuṭumiyāṉ malaik kalveṭṭumakēntiraṉ kaṭṭaḷaiyāltāṉ veṭṭap peṟṟatu eṉṟum, itaṉāl avaṉ icaik kalaiyil vallavaṉāka iruttal vēṇṭumeṉṟum ārāycciyāḷar karutukiṉṟaṉar'. 
makēntiravarmaṉ oru ciṟanta nūlāciriyaṉum āvāṉ. māmaṇṭūrk kalveṭṭil kuṟikkappeṟṟa mattavilācap pirakacaṉam eṉṉum nakaic cuvai nāṭakattai ivaṉ vaṭamoḻiyil eḻutiṉāṉ. itil pala camayattavarum, taṉippaṭṭa nilaiyil naṭattiya vāḻkkai muṟaikaḷai nakaiccuvaiyōṭu makēntiraṉ viḷakkukiṉṟāṉ. in nūlil catturu mallaṉ, avaṉipākaṉ, kuṇaparaṉ, mattavilācaṉ eṉṉum avaṉuṭaiya ciṟappup peyarkaḷum iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. pārppaṉaṉukkup pūnūlum, cupālicuṉukkum cupāvamum iṉṟiyamai yātavai eṉpataṉaiyum, putta pikkukaḷ taṅkaḷ tuṟavuneṟikaḷai maṟantu iṉpamācukkālam kaḻittārkaḷ eṉpataṉaiyum, ic camayattavar yāvarum oruvarukkoruvar taṅkaḷukkuḷ pūcaliṭṭuk koṇṭiruntataiyum vēṭikkaiyākavum, nakaiccuvaiyāka vum in nāl vivarikkiṟatu. , . - 
mutalām naracimmavarmaṉ (māmallaṉ) (ki.pi. 630-668) 
mutalām makēntiraṉukkup piṟaku avaṉ makaṉāṉa mutalām naracimmaṉ tōrā) kipi. 630-l aracu kaṭṭil ēṟiṉāṉ. ivaṉ kipi.130 iliruntu nīkki varai āṇṭāṉ'. ivaṉai "vātāpikoṇṭa naracimma varmaṉ' eṉṟē ciṟappuṟa aḻaippar. ulakap pukaḻpeṟṟa māmallapurattu aḻiyāta ciṟpaṅkaḷaic camaitta ivaṉai māmallaṉ' eṉa aḻaippatē cālavum ciṟappuṭait teṉpar. ivaṉ iḷavaracaṉāka iruntapōtu kaṭalmallai eṉṉum tuṟaimukappaṭṭiṉattil vāḻntu vantāṉ eṉṟum, tāṉ aracuk kaṭṭilēṟiyatum taṉ peyaraiyē itaṟku iṭṭu 'māmallapuram' eṉṟu aḻaittāṉ eṉṟum aṟikiṟōm. ivaṉukkum paṉṉeṭuṅkālattiṟku muṉpiruntē itu kaṭalmallai, nīrppeyaṟṟu, mallai (malaṅku eṉṉum peyarkaḷāl aḻaikkap peṟṟa ciṟanta tuṟai mukappaṭṭiṉamāka iruntatu. itaṉaip paṇṭaiya kirēkkarum urōmarum yavaṉarum naṉku aṟintirukkiṉṟaṉar. pukārum koṟkaiyum pōlavē it tuṟaimukappaṭṭiṉamum paṇṭaiya kaṭal vāṇika naṭuva mākat tikaḻntatait taṉṉaka, ayalakac cāṉṟukaḷiliruntu aṟikiṟōm. im māmallaṉ muṭicūṭṭik koṇṭa pōtu naracimmavarmaṉ eṉṟa paṭṭap peyaraic cūṭik koṇṭāṉ eṉṟum, ivaṉ iyaṟpeyar māmallaṉē eṉṟum kūṟuvar. 
pallava-cāḷukkiyap pōr 
tantaiyaip pōlavē vīramum, tīramum koṇṭu tikaḻnta ivaṉuṭaiya kālattil teṉṉaka varalāṟṟil ciṟappiṭam peṟṟa pallavar cāḷukkiyap pōr' naṭantatu. māmallaṉiṉ tantaiyiṭam kaṭaiciyākap paṭutōlviyaṭainta cāḷukkiya maṉṉaṉ iraṇṭām pulikēci ivaṉ aracuk kaṭṭilēṟiya cila āṇṭukaḷukkuḷ pallava nāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭai yeṭuttāṉ. āṉāl, naracimmaṉ cāḷukkiyar paṭaikaḷ kāñciyai aṇukumuṉ an nakarukku irupatu kal tolaiviluḷḷa maṇimaṅkalam eṉṉumiṭattilum, pariyaḷam cūramāram mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum cāḷuk kiyap paṭaikaḷait tākki muṟiyaṭittāṉ. itaṉaik kūram ceppēṭu mika aḻakākac cittarikkiṟatu. " 'im marapil utayakiriyiliruntu kiḻakku malaiyi liruntu) ātavaṉum nilavum tōṉṟuvatu pōl tōṉṟi, talai vaṇaṅkāta im marapu maṉṉarkaḷiṉ maṇimuṭiyil cūṭāmaṇi pōl viḷaṅki, pakai maṉṉarka ḷākiya yāṉaik kūṭṭaṅkaḷukku arimā pōṉṟu naracimmamūrttiyē maṇṇulakil araca kumāraṉāka avatarittataip pōl naracimmaṉ utittāṉ; ivaṉ cōḻar, kēraḷā, kaḷappirar, pāṇṭiyarkaḷait tirumpat tirumpaveṉṟu āyiraṅkai kaḷaik koṇṭavaṉ(kārttavīriyaṉ pōl viḷaṅkiṉāṉ; pāriyaḷam, maṇimaṅkalam, cūramāram mutaliya pōrkaḷil puṟaṅkāṭṭiyōṭiya pulikēciyiṉ mutukākiya paṭṭayattil veṟṟi" eṉṉum collaip poṟittāṉ. itaṟkuppiṉ, kumpamuṉivar (akattiyar), vātāpiyaiā vātāpi eṉṟa acuraṉai aḻittataip pōl ivaṉ vātāpiyai pulikēciyiṉ kōnakarai aḻittāṉ. 
pallavariṉ veṟṟi kaṇṭum maṉam niṟaivu peṟāta naracimmavarmaṉ pallavariṉ piṟavip pakaivaṉāṉa iraṇṭām pulikēciyai aṭiyōṭu aḻittu viṭat tīrmāṉittāṉ. itaṟku āyattamāyirunta pōtu pulikēci mīṇṭum pallava nāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭaiyeṭuttu vantāṉ. naracimmavarmaṉ im muṟai avaṉai yetirttup pōr ceytu veṟṟi peṟṟatōṭu avaṉaip pōrk kaḷattilēyē koṉṟāṉ. attuṭaṉ nillāmal cāḷukkiyar paṭaikaḷait turattic ceṉṟu an nāṭṭiṉ kōnakarākiya vātāpiyaiyum kaippaṟṟi aḻittāṉ. itaṉāl, ivaṉukku 'vātāpi koṇṭa naracimmaṉ' eṉṟa virutup peyar ēṟpaṭṭatu. ivaṉ vātāpiyai veṟṟi koṇṭa ceytiyai utayēntiram paṭṭayamum, kācak kuṭip paṭṭayamum', vēlūrppāḷaiyam paṭṭayamum ciṟappuṟac ceppu kiṉṟaṉa. 
naracimmavarmaṉ vātāpiyiṉ mēṟ paṭaikoṇṭu ceṉṟapōtu ap paṭaikkut talaimai tāṅkic ceṉṟavar parañcōtiyār eṉpavar āvār. ivarē piṉṉar ciṟuttoṇṭa nāyaṉār, eṉṟu tiruttoṇṭat tokaiyil iṭampeṟṟār. ivaruṭaiya vātāpi veṟṟiyaiyum aṅku ivar peṟṟa celvattaiyum paṟṟic cēkkiḻār tamatu periyapurāṇattil pukaḻntēṟṟiṉār. 
vātāpiyai veṟṟikoṇṭa naracimmaṉ pulikēciyait tāṉ vātāpiyaik kaippaṟṟuvataṟku muṉṉamēyē koṉṟu viṭṭāṉā allatu vēṟu aracarkaḷ pulikēciyaik koṉṟu viṭṭārkaḷā eṉṟa aiyam aṟiñarkaḷiṭaiyē iṉṉamum tīravillai. iraṇṭām pulikēci kipi.642l iṟantāṉ eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷariṉ muṭivu. āyiṉ, naracimma varmaṉum kipi.642-l tāṉ vātāpiyaik kaippaṟṟiyirukka vēṇṭum. an nakar ēṟakkuṟaiya patiṉmūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷ pallavar kaiyil iruntatu eṉpataṟkāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. vātāpiyiluḷḷa takkāṇa īrappaṉ eṉṉum kōyilukkarukiluḷḷa kampam oṉṟil naracimmavarmaṉatu patiṉ mūṉṟām āṇṭuk kalveṭṭu oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. atiluḷḷa veṭṭeḻuttu citaintu kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉum, naracimmavarmaṉ taṉatu vātāpi veṟṟiyai atil poṟittuḷḷāṉ eṉpatai aṟiya muṭikiṟatu '. atiluḷḷa veṭṭeḻuttu oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. taṉ tantai muṉṉar cāḷukkiyariṭam iḻanta āntirap pakutiyaiyum, naracimma varmaṉ veṉṟatōṭu vātāpiyaiyum kaippaṟṟiyāṇṭa perumai pallavariṉ vīravaralāṟṟil poṉṉeḻuttukaḷil poṟikkappaṭṭa tākum. ki.pi. 555 - l pallavariṭamiruntu mīṇṭum vātāpi cāḷukkiyar vacamāyiṟṟu. ataṉaip pulikēciyiṉ iḷaiya makaṉāṉa mutalām vikkira mātittaṉ kipi. 555 - iliruntu 81 varaiyil āṇṭāṉ. ivaṉ aracuk kaṭṭilēṟiyavuṭaṉ pallavaraip paḻivāṅkum poruṭṭu mīṇṭum pallava nāṭṭiṉmītu paṭaiyeṭuttāṉ. itaṉāl, pallavar- cāḷukkiyar pakai valuppeṟṟatu. vikkiramātittaṉ taṉṉuṭaiya karunāl paṭṭayattil taṉatu tantaiyākiya iraṇṭām pulikēci mūṉṟu pacaiyaracarkaḷāl tōlviyuṟṟa tākavum itaṉāl vātāpiyilirunta kōyilkaḷ varuvāyiṉṟit tavitta tākavum kuṟippiṭukiṉṟāṉ. im mūṉṟu aracarkaḷ yāvar eṉpatil aṟiñarkaḷiṭaiyē karuttuvēṟupāṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa, pulikēci yai veṉṟa aracarkaḷ mūvar naracimmavarmaṉ māṉavarmaṉ, camarāpirāmaṉ ākiya mūvar āvar.māṉavarmaṉ eṉpavaṉ ilaṅkai aracukku uriyavaṉ; taṉatu aracaiyiḻantu naracimmavarmaṉiṭam vantu aṭaikkalam pukuntu kāñciyilē neṭuṅkālam taṅkiyiruntavaṉ. ivaṉ naracimmavarmaṉuṭaṉ cērntu pulikēciyuṭaṉ pōr ceytāṉ eṉpataṉai makāvamcam eṉṉum īḻanāṭṭu varalāṟṟu nūlil kāṇkiṟōm. mūṉṟāvatu aracaṉāṉa camarā pirāmaṉ putukkōṭṭaiyaic cērnta koṭumpāḷūrai āṇṭavaṉ āvāṉ. ivaṉ cāḷukkiya aracaṉai atirāmaṅkalam eṉṉum ūril koṉṟāṉ eṉṟum, ivaṉuṭaiya takappaṉāṉa paraturkkamarttaṉaṉ vātāpiyai veṉṟāṉ eṉṟum, koṭumpāḷūril uḷḷa mūvarkōyil kalveṭṭuk kūṟukiṟatu. āṉāl, inta mūṉṟāvatu aracaṉ āntira nāṭṭil pallavariṉ paṭinikarāḷiyākavirunta cimmavicuṇuviṉ tampiyāṉa iraṇiyavarmaṉiṉ vaḻittōṉṟaluḷ oruvaṉāka irukkalām eṉṟu karutuvārum uṇṭu pōrkkaḷattil pulikēci iṟantapiṟakutāṉ 
naracimmaṉ vātāpimēl paṭaiyeṭuttāṉ eṉpatē poruttamāṉatu. 
pallavar - pāṇṭiyar pōr 
naracimmavarmaṉ kālattil pāṇṭiya nāṭṭu aracaṉāka iruntavaṉ arikēcariparāṅkucaṉneṭumāṟaṉ) (ki.pi. 641-570) eṉpavaṉ āvāṉ. ivaṉ cōḻariṭam peṇ koṇṭu uṟaviṉaṉākic cēraraiyum, piṟa kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷaiyum veṉṟu teṉṉāṭṭilēyē ciṟanta taḷapatiyāka viḷaṅkiṉāṉ. ivaṉ cakkaramaṅkai eṉṉumiṭattil pallavaṉaip puṟaṅkaṇṭāṉ eṉṟu ciṉṉamaṉūrc ceppēṭu kūṟukiṟatu'. āṉāl, naracimmavarmaṉ cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya, kaḷappiraruṭaṉ pōriṭṭāṉ eṉṟu kūram ceppēṭukaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. pulikēci mutaṉmuṟaiyākap pallava nāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭaiyeṭuttapōtu, 'cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiyar maṉam makiḻumpaṭi kāvirikkaraiyai aṭaintu pallavaṉaip puṟaṅ kaṇṭāṉ' eṉṟu cāḷukkiyar ceppēṭu kūṟuvatāl it tamiḻaracar mūvarukkum potup pacuaivarācup pallavar iruntaṉar eṉpatu teḷivā kiṟatu. aṭuttu, iraṇṭām muṟaiyākap pulikēci pallavar nāṭṭiṉmītu paṭaiyeṭuttapōtu naracimmavarmaṉ avaṉait tōṟkaṭittuk koṉṟa tōṭu avaṉuṭaiya kōnakaraiyum kaippaṟṟiṉāṉ eṉpatai aṟivōm. kōnakar vātāpiyaik kaippaṟṟuvataṟku oru perumpaṭaiyuṭaṉ tam tāṉait talaivarāṉa parañcōtiyai aṉuppiviṭṭut tāṉ maṭṭum mūvēntarkaḷuṭaṉum, kaḷappiraruṭaṉum naracimmaṉ pōriṭṭāṉ. mutalil cakkaramaṅkai eṉṉumiṭattil tōlviyuṟṟa avaṉ taṉatu perumpaṭai utavikku vantavuṭaṉ in nālvaraiyum tōṟkaṭittirukkalām. āka, naracimmavarmaṉ cāḷukkiyarkaḷaiyum cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya, kaḷappirar kaḷaiyum muṭivil veṟṟi koṇṭāṉ eṉpatil ettakaiya aiyamum illai
pallavar-kaṅkar pōr 
iraṇṭām pulikēci ki.pi. 642-l iṟantataiyum vātāpi patiṉmūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷ pallavar vacam iruntataiyum, ki.pi. 655-l pulikēciyiṉ makkaḷ mūvaril oruvaṉāṉa mutalām vikkiramātittaṉ aracuk kaṭṭilēṟiyataiyum aṟivōm. āṉāl ivaṉ iyalpāka aracukkaṭṭil ēṟavillai , ivaṉukkuc cantirātittaṉ, ātitta varmaṉ ākiya aṇṇaṉ mār iruntaṉar. ātittavarmaṉukkum mutalām vikkiramātittaṉukkum aracurimaip pōr ēṟpaṭṭatu. itil ātittavarmaṉ naracimmavarmaṉiṉ tuṇaiyai nāṭiṉāṉ. vikkiramātittaṉ taṉ tāy vaḻippāṭṭaṉāṉa turvinītaṉ tuṇaiyai nāṭiṉāṉ. turvinītaṉukku naracimmavarmaṉukkum ēṟkaṉavē pakaimai uṇṭu. turvinītaṉ āṇṭa kaṅkanāṭṭiṉ oru pakuti yāṉa koṅku nāṭṭai naracimmavarmaṉ kaippaṟṟit turvinītaṉuṭaiya tampiyaik koṅkunāṭṭiṟku aracaṉākkiyatāl, turvinītaṉukkum, naracimmavarmaṉukkum pakaimai ēṟpaṭṭatu. itaṉāl, cāḷukkiya nāṭṭu aracurimaip pōril kaṅkaṉum, pallavaṉum etir etirkkaṭcikaḷai ātarittaṉar. piṉṉar, taṅkaḷukkuḷḷum pōriṭṭuk koṇṭaṉar. kaṭaiciyil, ip pōrilum naracimmavarmaṉē veṟṟi peṟṟāṉ eṉak kōṭal ciṟappuṭaittākum. 
īḻanāṭṭup pōr 
īḻanāṭṭu aracaṉ māṉavarmaṉ (māṉavammā) eṉpavaṉai aṭṭa tattaṉ eṉpavaṉ turattiyaṭittuviṭṭu aracaik kaippaṟṟik koṇṭāṉ, māṉavarmaṉ pallavanāṭu pōntu naracimmavarmaṉiṭam aṭaikkalam pukuntu avaṉ maṉam makiḻumāṟu avaṉukku uṟutuṇaiyāy niṉṟu ariya pala paṇikaḷaic ceytāṉ. naracimma varmaṉ iraṇṭām pulikēciyuṭaṉ ceyta pōrilum māṉavarmaṉ āṟṟiya paṇi aḷaviṭaṟkariyatu. ittakaiya ceyalkaḷāl makiḻcci aṭainta naracimmavarmaṉ, māṉavarmaṉukku māpaṭai, makkaḷpaṭai, marakkalappaṭai mutaliyavaṟṟai aṉuppi, avaṉ iḻanta paṭṭattaip peṟa utaviṉāṉ. mutalil veṟṟikaṇṭamāṉavarmaṉ tamiḻar paṭaikaḷ kāñcikku mīṇṭatum pakaivaṉāl tōṟkaṭikkappaṭ ṭāṉ. mīṇṭum māṉavarmaṉ kāñcikku vantu cila kālattukkup piṉṉar naracimmavarmaṉiṉ perumpaṭaiyuṭaṉ māmallapurattut tuṟaimukappaṭ ṭiṉattiliruntu kappalēṟi īḻattiṟkuc ceṉṟu, taṉ pakaivaṉai veṉṟu āṭciyaik kaippaṟṟiṉāṉ. ivvāṟu iraṇṭu muṟai pallavap paṭaikaḷ kaṭal kaṭantu ceṉṟu ilaṅkaiyil veṟṟi peṟṟatōṭu māṉa varmaṉukku avaṉuṭaiya aracurimaiyaiyum mīṭṭut tantaṉaveṉpataik kācakkuṭic ceppēṭṭilum, īḻanāṭṭu varalāṟṟaik kūṟukiṉṟa makā vamcam eṉṉum nūlilum teḷivāka aṟikiṟōm, 
camayam 
naracimmavarmaṉ caiva camayattaip piṉpaṟṟiṉāṉ. ivaṉ tantaiyiṉ kālattil caivam ceḻittōṅkat toṭaṅkiyatu eṉpataiyum muṉṉarē kaṇṭōm. in nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiya iṟaineṟi iyakkam camaṇa, putta camayaṅkaḷai aḻikkavum, caivaneṟiyaip parappavum muṉaintatu. naracimmavarmaṉ kālattil pallava nāṭṭiṟku vanta ki.pi. 640} yuvāṉ cuvāṅku eṉṟa cīṉappayaṇi pallavariṉ kōnakarākiya kāñcipuram āṟu kal cuṟṟaḷavu uḷḷateṉṟum, ak kālattut tamiḻaka nakaraṅkaḷuḷ itu māperum nakaramākak kāṭciyaḷitta teṉṟum, in nakaril nūṟṟukkum mēṟpaṭṭa puttap paḷḷikaḷ iruntaṉa veṉṟum, avaṟṟil patiṉāyiram pikkukaḷ taṅki aṟam vaḷarkka ēntukaḷ amaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉaveṉ ṟum kuṟippiṭuvatiliruntu ik kālattil putta camayam peṟṟirunta mēmpāṭṭaiyaṟiyalām. ip paḷḷikaḷil payiṉṟu periya aṟiñarākiya varkaḷil tarumapālar eṉpārum oruvarāvar. ip pēraṟiñartāṉ nāḷantāp palkalaikkaḻakattil putta camayap pērāciriyarākat tikaḻntavar āvār. yuvāṉ cuvāṅkiṉ kuṟippiliruntu kāñcipurattil eṇpatiṟkum mēṟpaṭṭa kōyilkaḷ iruntaṉaveṉṟum, avai perum pālum tikampararkaḷukku camaṇarkaḷukku uriyavaiyeṉṟum terikiṟatu. mēlum, in nakaril acōkap pēraracarāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa nūṟu aṭi uyaramuḷḷa oru paḷḷi iruntateṉṟum ivaṉ kuṟippiṭukiṟāṉ. putta perumāṉ in nakarukku vantu taṅki makkaḷait tam camayattiṟku māṟumpaṭi ceytā reṉṟum avaṉ kūṟukiṟāṉ. tāṉ īḻattiliruntu kāñcikku vantirunta munnūṟu puttappikkukaḷuṭaṉ uraiyāṭi yataiyum cīṉappayaṇa kuṟippiṭu kiṟāṉ. ivaṉuṭaiya kuṟippukaḷi liruntu kāñcipuram oru talaiciṟanta palkalaikkaḻaka naṭuvamāka viḷaṅkiyatu eṉpataiyum, in nāṭṭil kaṟṟuttērnta aṟiñarkaḷ kūṭukiṉṟa iṭamāka itu irunta teṉpatai yum iṅkiruntu īḻanāṭṭiṟkuk kaṭal vaḻiyākac celvataṟku mūṉṟu nāḷkaḷ ākumeṉpataiyum aṟikiṟōm. pallava nāṭṭil maṭṭumē yaṉṟip pāṇṭiya nāṭṭilum puttam ciṟappuṟṟiruntatu. in nāṭṭiṉ talainakarākiya maturaiyil puttam ciṟupāṉmaiyākavum, tikampara camayam perumpāṉmaiyākavum iruntateṉṟum, innakariṉ kiḻakkup pakutiyil acōcap pēraracariṉ tampiyāṉamakaṉ makēntiraṉ cuṭṭiya putta vikārai iṭintupōṉa kaṭṭaṭamāka iruntateṉṟum yuvāṉ cuvāṅku kuṟippiṭu kiṟāṉ. 
ivvāṟu acaikkamuṭiyāta aḷaviṟkup perum cāykāl peṟṟu viṭṭa putta, camaṇacamayaṅkaḷukku etirākat tirunāvukkaracar, appūti yaṭikaḷ, ciṟuttoṇṭar, tiruñāṉacampantar, tirunīlakaṇṭar, neṭumāṟa nāyaṉār, maṅkaiyarkkaraciyār, kulacciṟaiyār, tirunīlanakkar, muruka nāyaṉār, kuṅkiliyak kalaiyar mutalāṉa caivacamaya aṭiyārkaḷ caiva camayattai makkaḷiṭaiyē parappattoṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. ivarkaḷil tiruñāṉacampantarum, tirunāvukkaracarum tamiḻnāṭeṅkilum cuṟṟup payaṇam ceytu putta, camaṇa camayaṅkaḷai muṭukkamāka etirttup pōrāṭi makkaḷaic caivar ākkiṉārkaḷ. ivarkaḷāl pāṭappeṟṟa tēvāram, tiruvācakap pāṭalkaḷ piṉṉar caiva camayat tirumuṟaikaḷākat tokukkappeṟṟaṉa. itaṉāl, tamiḻariṉ camaya neṟikkum, moḻi vaḷarccikkum pērukkamum pērāḷumaiyum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. 
caiva aṭiyārkaḷaip pōlavē vaiṇava aṭiyārkaḷum iṟai neṟi iyakkattai muṭukkamākap parappuvataṟkup pāṭupaṭṭārkaḷ. mutal āḻvārkaḷ eṉṟu ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭum poykaiyāḻvār, pūtattāḻvār, pēyāḻvār ākiya mūvarum pāṭiya paktippāṭalkaḷ tamiḻariṉ tūya vāḻvaiyum, tamiḻiṉ mēṉmaiyaiyum uyarttiṉa. ivarkaḷait tavirat tirumaḻicai āḻvār eṉpavarum naracimmavarmaṉ kālattil iruntār. ivarkaḷāl vaiṇava camayam maṟumalarcci peṟṟatu. ivarkaḷ iyaṟṟiya teyvīkap pāṭalkaḷ yāvum 'nālāyirat tivyap pirapantam' eṉṉum peru nāṭācut tokukkappeṟṟatu. makēntiravarmaṉ kālattil toṭaṅkappeṟṟa iṟaineṟi iyakkam naracimmavarmaṉ kālattil peritum valuppeṟṟu viṭṭatāl camaṇamum puttamum kuṉṟic caivam talaiciṟantu niṉṟatu. vaiṇavamum maṟumalarccipeṟṟatu. 
mēṟkūṟiya icaineṟi iyakkattiṉ ciṉṉaṅkaḷāka vātāpi koṇṭa naracimmaṉ palatiṟappaṭṭa kuṭaivaraikkōyilkaḷai māmalla purattilum, paravalākat tamiḻnāṭeṅkaṇum camaittāṉ. tirucciyiluḷḷa kuṭaivaraik kōyilum, nāmakkal malaiyaṭiyiluḷḷa naraciṅkap perumāḷ kuṭaivaraik kōyilum ivaṉ kālattavaiyeṉak karutappaṭum kiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟait tavira māmallapurattil makiṭācura maṇṭapam, varāka maṇṭapam, tirumūrtti maṇṭapam mutaliyaṉavum iṉṟu pañcapāṇṭavar irataṅkaḷ' eṉap potuvāka aḻaikkappaṭum oṟṟaik kaṟṟōrkaḷum im maṉṉaṉāl camaikkappaṭṭavaiyākum. ivvāṟu ivaṉ kalaikkum, camayattiṟkum oruṅkē paṇipurintu ulakam pōṟṟum kalaic celvattai māmallaiyil paṭaittu, ataṉōṭu tāṉum aḻiyātu niṟkiṉṟāṉ. māmallapurattil perum pāṟaikaḷiṉmītu ivaṉāl cetukkappeṟṟa puṭaippōviyaṅkaḷ ulakak kalaiñarkaḷiṉ cuvaṉattai īrppaṉavāy uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ kōvarttaṉa malaiyaik kuṭaiyākap piṭikkum kaṇṇaṉiṉ ciṟpamum, kaṅkaikkaraik kāṭciyum ciṟappākak kuṟippiṭat takkaṉavākum. ivaṉuṭaiya kuṭaivaraik kōyilkaḷil oṟṟaik kaṟṟērkaḷilum pāṟaic ciṟpaṅkaḷilum kāṇappaṭum kalaiyaḻaku kalaiñariṉ eṇṇattaik kiḷarttuvatāyuḷḷatu. ivaṉāl, tiruccikku arukil tiruttavattuṟai lālkuṭiyaiyaṭutta peruvaḷanallūrar eṉṉum ūrukku arukiluḷḷa pallāvaram eṉṉum ciṟṟūril oru kōṭṭai caṭṭappeṟṟateṉṟum, piṉṉar, avaratu aḻintupaṭṭateṉṟum kūṟuvar. 
taṉ tōḷvaliyālum, maṉavaliyālum, aṟivuttiṟattālum mēmpaṭṭa naracimmavarmaṉ, tāṉ camaitta kōvilkaḷilellām taṉatu virutuppeyarkaḷ palavaṟṟai veṭṭuvittuḷḷāṉ. avai māmallaṉ, srīparaṉ, srīmēkaṉ, puvaṉamājaṉ, attiyantakkāmaṉ, amēyamāyaṉ, iraṇacayaṉ, nayanāṅkuraṉ mutaliyaṉavākum. māmallapurattut tirumūrttik kuṭaivaraiyil ivaṉ peyar 'malla' eṉṟu pallavak kiranta eḻuttil cetukkappeṟṟuḷḷatu. tirukkaḻukkuṉṟattu oṟṟaik kal maṇṭapak. kuṭaivaraiyil "vātāpi koṇṭa naraciṅkap pōttaracar' eṉṟu tamiḻil cetukkap peṟṟuḷḷatu. camaṟkirutattilum ivaṉuṭaiya virutup peyarkaḷ poṟikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ittakaiya pērum pukaḻum peṟṟu vāḻnta māmallaṉuṭaiya pallavap pēraracu vaṭakkē vaṭapeṇṇaiyāṟu mutal teṟkē veḷḷāṟu varaiyil paraviyiruntatu. atāvatu itu toṇṭai nāṭṭaiyum, cōḻanāṭṭaiyum taṉṉuḷ koṇṭiruntatu. tarumarācar tēril ivaṉuṭaiya uruvam puṭaippuc ciṟpamāka amain tuḷḷatu. inta uruvam taṉiyē camaikkappaṭṭu iruppatālum, kātu cuḷil kuṇṭalaṅkaḷum, mārpil pūṇūālum, veṟumēṉiyum koṇṭu tikaḻvatālum ivaṉuṭaiyacaivacamayappaṟṟai itaṉ tōṟṟattiliruntu aṟiyakkūṭum eṉpar. 


iraṇṭām makēntiravarmaṉ (ki.pi. 668-70) 
iraṇṭām makēntiravarmaṉ mutalām naracimmavarmaṉukkup piṟaku ariyaṇaiyēṟiṉāṉ. āṉāl, avaṉukku muṭicūṭṭu viḻā eppōtu naṭantatu. avaṉ ettaṉaiyāṇṭukaḷ āṇṭāṉeṉpaṉa paṟṟiya tiṭṭa vaṭṭamāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭaikkavillai. piṟkālap paṭṭaiyaṅkaḷilum ivaṉuṭaiya āṭciyil naṭanta nikaḻvukaḷ paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷ ētum kāṇappaṭavillai, āṉāl, ivaṉ avvakuppār oḻukavēṇṭiya muṟaikaḷaik kūṟum aṟanūl vaḻi āṇṭāṉeṉṟu maṭṭumē vēlūrp pāḷaiyam paṭṭayam kūṟukiṟatu.
